V2D Track 2 工作文档

截至 10/20 12:01（UTC-6）。榜（12:01）：新队 Team OPS 10/20 在 12 个榜里交了 12 个、Javier Medel, MSc. 交了 Tier 1，分数都在 maniphys.ai 之后；Team OPS 的 Tier 3 SP-SR 0.16666 排第 2（maniphys.ai 0.26666 仍第一），FF 降到第 3；Open4D 的 Tier 3 MPPE 86.66 → 72.81。maniphys.ai 各榜名次不变：第一的仍是 Tier 2 SP-SR（0.73333）与 Tier 3 SP-SR，Tier 1 AUC 第 4（0.58258，DAP 0.60030）。12 个榜名次直接相加：按 Kaggle 的同分规则 FF 22、maniphys.ai 26，按官网的 23、28；每个指标先三档平均再排名，5 比 7（results/board/diff_1020_1201.txt）。提交：10/18 10:54 之后没有新提交；一个榜要按 4096 世界的期望和导出那一局都遥遥领先才交（agent-doc/strategy-2026-10-19.md）。只为少丢一个名次、拿不到第一的提交要不要另定规矩，待定。差距的实情：现在交的 Tier 2 清单 30 集 4096 世界 MPPE 平均 18.02 cm，榜上的 15.32 是导出那一局；按期望领先 1 cm 要把 30 集合计降约 160 cm。Tier 1、Tier 2 里最大的两块都是 ep25（114.10、117.09 cm）与 ep4（109.91、111.49 cm）。全失败的集为什么失败（agent-doc/plan-t2dg-2026-10-20.md、agent-doc/plan-t12lift-2026-10-20.md）：Tier 2 的全失败集机器人都摔了（骨盆朝向误差过 0.5 rad），成功的 ep0 没摔；ep1、ep4 在参考接触物体之前就摔（第 27、99 帧），ep25、ep17、ep9、ep28 在物体掉下之后 9–50 帧摔。ep4 的球棒 96.9% 的世界里没被碰动，是机器人没走到跟前。参考上的走速、转速、骨盆高度、腰部限位、支撑脚滑动五种统计都分不开成败。参考接触段合手重训 ep25、ep4 没用。在做：保底：Tier 2 ep25、ep28 与 Tier 1 ep25 换成两条手臂不去够物体的参考重训（物体不动时 ep25 约 49.5 cm），ml40 上 Tier 2 两条约 13:30 出读数，Tier 1 一条约 17:00；交不交待定。修平衡：仿真里记下骨盆、躯干、两只脚的位姿，找摔倒之前先坏的一项（ep4、1、25 对照 ep0、16），排在 ml40 约 17:00 之后。Tier 3：gb4 交付的 11 集在 mf4090 上重训，12:01 训到第 7 条（ep18），约 21:50 训完、23:30 出读数；10/22 16:14 起 Tier 3 多 1 次额度，交不交待定（agent-doc/plan-t3g4-2026-10-19.md）。ml40：rxzhou 的保底重训在跑（results/ops/status_ml40_1020_1201.txt）。

10/17 18:30（UTC-6）时：榜（18:15）：与 14:49 相同。12 个榜里 maniphys.ai 5 个第一（Tier 2 的 AUC、SP-SR、MP-SR，Tier 3 的 SP-SR、MPPE），FF 7 个第一（Tier 1 四个、Tier 2 MPPE、Tier 3 AUC，以及各队都是 0 的 Tier 3 MP-SR：同分按先提交者在前）（results/submission/leaderboard_1017_1815.txt）。总排名的算法（10/17 查官网与 FAQ，results/ops/challenge_faq_1017_sentences.txt）：各指标的名次相加，和最小者第一；三个档位权重相同；赛道第一获 DGX Spark，第二获 3K 美元 GPU 额度，另有评审团评选的产业创新奖，11/12 在 CoRL tutorial 公布。档位与指标具体怎么合，官方没有写明：12 个榜的名次直接相加（基线不计名次），FF 17、maniphys.ai 20；每个指标先三档平均再排名：AUC FF 领先，SP-SR、MPPE maniphys.ai 领先，MP-SR 两队平均完全相同、同分怎么判没写明，判给 FF 是 6 比 6，判给先提交的 maniphys.ai 是 5 比 7（results/submission/rank_sum_1017_1815.txt）。差距最小的几处：Tier 1 AUC 差 0.00107，Tier 1 MP-SR 差 1 集，Tier 3 MP-SR 先有 1 集成功即可第一，Tier 2 MPPE 差 1.44 cm，Tier 3 AUC 差 0.107。提交上限：每档每周最多 5 次（最后三天不限）；过去 7 天 Tier 1 用了 3 次、Tier 2 2 次、Tier 3 4 次。仓库共享：组委会账号 Nvidia-V2D-Challenge 仍不是代码仓库的协作者（results/ops/collab_check_nvidia_1017_1830b.txt），规则要求每份提交里的 commit 链接组委会能打开；添加要 Manifold-ai 的管理员操作，待办。Tier 3 换到 gb3：计划 agent-doc/plan-t3-gb3-switch-2026-10-17.md（第 2 版）在 gb3 参考上重训 8 条：mf4090 ep20、0、22、21，ml40 ep6、8、7、29。换不换按出结果前登记的规则：候选 4096 世界 MPPE 不超过现选 run 加 4.0 cm，且 SP-SR ≥ 0.5。ep21（侧机位）只报；ep22 进不进选集待定（10/16 定 ep22 留旧参考，人脚低于地面 3.7 cm；gb3 的 ep22 人脚 −3.77 cm，旧参考 −3.76 cm）。准备已做完：11 集经 HF 转到 mf4090，哈希都对；重定向间隙 −0.5 到 0 mm；转到 ml40（199 个文件、850.5 MB）；两台配置核对都过。两台现在的链结束、卡空后自动接上，估计 10/18 07:00 前后出读数。在训：ml40 Tier 1 的 seed 43、mf4090 Tier 2 的 ep2、11、17，都在训第 3 条，约 21:00 出结果（估计）。10/17 的提交：14:39 Tier 1 c10_1017a（AUC 0.56315、SP-SR 0.76666、MP-SR 0.66666、MPPE 13.92082 cm），14:47 Tier 3 kfix1017（MPPE 55.76705 cm、SP-SR 0.13333、AUC 0.04885、MP-SR 0），判据 6 读作支持内参修正。

0.42499）、SP-SR、MP-SR 仍是 maniphys.ai 第一。FF 14:55 又交了 Tier 1，MPPE 从 8.22045 降到 8.03204 cm，Tier 1 四个榜仍是 FF 第一；Tier 3 没有变化（results/submission/leaderboard_1016_1850.txt）。Tier 3 新参考：用现有 checkpoint 对新参考重新评测与导出（11:21 起），两道核对都过：包装层恒等，评测器与 10/11 的评测一致（SR、AUC 的差都在 0.002 以内，MPPE 在 0.01 cm 以内）。按登记的规则，ep24（MP-SR 0.933 → 0.902）、ep26（1.000 → 0.984）可换；ep0（0.965 → 0.000）、ep18（0.862 → 0.013）、ep29（SP-SR 0.713 → 0.025）不换；ep20 换成方块桌面的参考后 SP-SR 0.895 → 0.248，也不换（results/t3kfix/reexport_mf4090.txt）。跟不住的集参考变得多：物体相对机器人根的位置变化中位，ep0 6.2 cm，ep24 0.78 cm（results/t3kfix/ref_pair_diff_ep0_mf4090.txt、results/t3kfix/ref_pair_diff_ep24_mf4090.txt）。13:32 起 mf4090 按新参考重训 ep0、18、29：ep0 15:58、ep18 18:14 训完，ep29 在训；之后接着训方块桌面的 ep20；20:50、23:30 前后各出一次读数（估计）。只换 2 集，按登记的读法这次不提议交 Tier 3，重训后够 3 集再议（待定）。yqchen 的 gb3 世界坐标系在主机位与新参考一致：物体位置、人体参数逐位相同，物体朝向最多差 0.33°（results/t3kfix/kfix_vs_gb3_world_ml40_1016.txt）；yqchen 11:05–11:14 跑完 gb3 的世界坐标系转换（54 条），最后的汇总一步没有完成，18:40 时 logs 目录在 11:14 之后没有新文件（results/ops/t3_queue_ml40_1016_1845.txt）。Tier 1、Tier 2：r3o 第二段两台都训完、评完、导出，没有失败（mf4090 10:52 训完、11:17 导出完；ml40 13:13、13:46），两档跟丢的集都没修好（Tier 1 7 集、Tier 2 8 集，修好 0、有起色 0；对照 Tier 1 ep20 0.837、Tier 2 ep12 0.735 过门槛），判“没用”，10/16 定下这个方向停（results/r3o/verdict_P1_mf4090.txt、results/ml40/r3o/verdict_P2_ml40.txt、results/r3o/health_t1ob_mf4090.txt、results/r3o/health_t2ob_ml40.txt）。Tier 1 跟丢集的诊断（13:28–13:42）：物体在首次接触后 9–32 帧越线（ep4 例外，接触前就越线）；接触后 30 帧内物体自转的中位，跟丢的 7 集 16.4°，其余各集 1.0°（results/t1diag/contact_onset_t1_ml40.txt、results/t1diag/contact_window_mf4090.txt）；接触标记碎成很多段，例如 ep28 左手 12 段（results/ml40/t1diag/c10_relabel_ml40.txt）。据此在 ml40 上试“接触标记提前”：每段接触起点提前 10 帧、补上 15 帧以内的空档，参考轨迹不变；同一策略对新旧两份参考评出来一致（results/ml40/t1diag/eval_pair_c10_ep25_ml40.txt）。ep25、28、11、1 16:49 起在训，10/17 02:00 前后出判读（估计；agent-doc/plan-t1-contact-anticipation-2026-10-16.md）。Tier 2 只用自己的数据做了同样的诊断（18:31），三条特征都过，提前量按登记的规则取 5 帧；Tier 2 的 ep28、ep4 ep25、9、1）的计划已写，起不起按登记的条件定（agent-doc/plan-c10-extend-2026-10-16.md）。

10/16 07:30（UTC-6）时：榜（06:26）：Tier 3 10/16 04:34 交了内参 A/B 两版（10/16 定下）。按吊顶方格标定的内参把帧参考绕地面法向转 −4.65° 的一版，MPPE 56.87427 cm、SP-SR 0.03333，两个榜第一；AUC 0.00690 排第二（Xueyi Wang (FF) 0.15606）；MP-SR 三家都是 0。按墙面结构标定反向转 +1.20° 的对照版 MPPE 63.44727 cm，比不转的探路版（60.70360）差，按提交前登记的规则记“支持方格内参”（results/submission/leaderboard_1016_t3ab.txt）。Tier 2 四个榜仍是 maniphys.ai 第一。Tier 1 四个榜都是 FF 第一；DAP 10/16 01:08 交了 Tier 1，MPPE 12.65831 cm 排第二，maniphys.ai 的 16.55939 cm 排第三（results/submission/leaderboard_1016_0630.txt）。Tier 3 的世界坐标系：主机位 24 集的世界坐标系就是主机位相机的坐标系，俯仰、横滚与离地高度由发布的地面和房间结构的标定确定，偏航由这次 A/B 支持。主机位内参 f ≈ 866、主点 ≈ (702, 531)；重建流程用的是 f 830、主点居中 (768, 576)。按标定内参算，单这一项让参考位置偏约 32 cm（中位；两份估计之间的差，不是对真值的误差）。偏航部分已用帧参考上榜修正，深度部分要换参考：rxzhou 按标定内参给跟住了自己参考的 7 集（ep0、18、20、22、24、26、29）重新生成参考，来源、深度口径、几何、地面复现四项检查都过；与旧参考比，物体离相机远 5.1%，人走的路程长 3.1–4.5%（agent-doc/plan-t3-kfix-2026-10-16.md、results/t3world/kfix_ref_diff.txt）。人脚检查：7 集合起来离地中位 1.55 cm，在 3 cm 以内；逐集只有 ep22 低 3.71 cm，旧参考的 ep22 也低 3.76 cm，旧参考主机位 24 集里有 7 集超过 3 cm，属于人体重建本身（results/t3world/kfix_feet_compare.txt）。地面校正（转 1.22°、缩放 0.984）的证据两边都有：人脚检验按登记的规则判“人脚没有物体拟合出的那个横滚”，但区间两头都只差一点（results/t3world/feet_roll.txt）。待定：新参考用现有 checkpoint 重新评测导出、按新参考重训还是先不动；新参考怎么传到 mf4090；地面校正留不留；ep22 怎么处理。第二、侧机位 6 集的内参吊顶方格定不住，暂不投入（待定）。Tier 1、Tier 2：r3o 第一段 10/15 23:38（mf4090）、10/16 00:10（ml40）结束，各 4 条训完、评完、导出，没有失败，看守扫描没有非有限值；对照过门槛（Tier 1 ep20 的 4096 世界 MP-SR 0.837，Tier 2 ep12 0.735），跟丢的 6 集（Tier 1 ep25、28、11，Tier 2 ep25、28、1）都是 0.000，判“没用”，段间门判继续（results/r3o/health_t1oa_mf4090.txt、results/r3o/health_t2oa_ml40.txt）。第二段没有自动接上（起链前的配置核对漏跑，预演没过），两台空闲到 02:19，补跑核对后再起：mf4090 Tier 1（ep4、9、1、2），ml40 Tier 2（ep4、17、9、2、11），重点是朝向先越线的 ep4、ep9。07:30 时 mf4090 训完 ep4、ep9，ml40 训完 ep4、ep17；预计 mf4090 约 11:15 训完、12:00 前后评完，ml40 约 13:15 训完、14:00 前后评完（估计）。yqchen：10/15 23:37–23:41 生成新版本 dr2s3 与 dr2s3_dyn7（各 29 集）；10/16 00:40–02:16 在 ml40 的空档里跑完 4 集（ep13、28、4、3）的 CARI4D 初始化试点（重跑第 06–08 步，替换不可靠的帧），结果待 yqchen 判读（results/ops/t3_init_pilot_ml40_1016_0730.txt）。

10/15 19:30（UTC-6）时：榜：Xueyi Wang (FF) 10/15 00:11 交了新的 Tier 1，SP-SR 从 0.70000 升到 0.80000，Tier 1 四个榜现在都是 FF 第一；Tier 2 四个榜仍是 maniphys.ai 第一，MPPE 领先 0.02 cm（results/submission/leaderboard_1015_1137.txt）。Tier 3 第一次提交：rxzhou 10/15 加入 Tier 3 的 4 个比赛，16:14 交了探路版（30 集：best_hm 18 集，旧版 v2 8 集，ep9、10、11、28 是只训 400 万步的占位）。公榜 MPPE 60.70360 cm 排第一（FF 67.33010、CHORD 基线 70.17205），AUC 0.00000 排第三（FF 0.15606、CHORD 基线 0.00226），SP-SR、MP-SR 三家都是 0（results/submission/leaderboard_1015_t3probe.txt）。按提交前登记的读法，记为“真值上接近基线，重建与真值的对齐可能是瓶颈”：AUC 的阈值是 1–9 cm，0 分说明 30 集几乎没有一帧的误差小于 9 cm；对照自己的重建跟得好的 ep7、8、22（本地 MPPE 2–4 cm）也是这样，误差主要在重建本身（推断）。Tier 1、Tier 2：按 MP-SR 的尺度（位置 3 cm、朝向 0.52 rad）重读逐帧终止，Tier 1 跟丢的 7 集里 5 集是位置先越线，此前“先丢朝向”的读法是 10 cm 的位置门槛偏宽造成的。新配方 r3o（变体 A 加一项物体朝向奖励）作为唯一还没试过的杠杆照做：14:54 在 mf4090 起 Tier 1 第一段（对照 ep20，跟丢的 ep25、28、11），15:09 在 ml40 起 Tier 2 第一段（对照 ep12，跟丢的 ep25、28、1），判据与段间门都在出结果前登记。19:16 时 Tier 1 训完 2 条、Tier 2 训完 1 条；第一段约 10/16 00:00、02:30 评完（估计），过了段间门自动接第二段。

10/15 07:30（UTC-6）时：10/14 16:46 之后两档都没有新的提交；10/14 20:00 再看 12 个榜，与 18:30 相同，maniphys.ai 仍有 5 个第一（Tier 2 四个榜，Tier 1 SP-SR），Tier 2 MPPE 领先 Xueyi Wang (FF) 0.02 cm（results/submission/leaderboard_1014_2000.txt）。复现检查没过：Tier 1 已交的 ep19 checkpoint 在 ml40 上按官方做法又导出两次，三份轨迹各不相同（SP-SR 1、1、0，MP-SR 都是 0，MPPE 3.68、3.29、15.72 cm，results/first/zero_ep19_ml40_rep2.txt），每集交的那 1 局是一次随机抽样；更早 checkpoint 的探查结果因此不用。10/14 定下：挑选与汇报都把导出的那 1 局与 4096 个世界的统计并列，以 4096 个世界为主；规则写明闭赛后主办方用不分发给参赛者的数据评各队选定的方案，每队最多选 2 个 Final Submission（results/ops/kaggle_rules_submission_1014.txt）。Tier 1：选项甲（ep14、ep19 各补一条 seed 43）10/15 凌晨跑完：ep14 seed 43 的 4096 世界 MP-SR 0.565（seed 42 的变体 A 0.677），导出的那 1 局失败；ep19 seed 43 是 0.030（seed 42 的变体 A 0.258），按登记的停手规则 ep19 不再补（results/jia/eval_table_jia_s43.txt）。跟丢的 7 集做了逐帧终止分析（选项丙，计划 agent-doc/plan-frame-onset-2026-10-14.md）：物体位置偏 10 cm 或朝向偏 0.6 rad（约 34°）记为物体越线，95–100% 的世界是物体先越线，多为第一次接触后不久朝向越线，骨盆晚 1.5–13.5 秒才越线；之前“几乎每个世界都是骨盆失败”只说明最后几乎都摔，不说明先后。ep4 的干净参考里，球棒在手碰到之前原地转了 67.5°，评测的朝向误差不计对称。Tier 2：没有新的训练与提交。Tier 3：动力学修复版试点 10/14 跑完，对照各自参考的 4096 世界 MP-SR：ep15 0.000、ep3 0.106、ep16 0.000（best_hm 上都是 0.000），ep12 的 best_dyn4 0.562、best_dyn4s 0.000（results/t3dyn/eval_table_t3pdyn.txt、results/t3dyn/eval_table_t3qdyn.txt）。按开训前登记的读法，主判据读作看不清：算数的只有 ep15；yqchen 10/14 10/10 21:00（UTC-6）时：Tier 3 第二轮换用 yqchen 的校正版（地面上 14 集的常数偏移从第一轮的 +135.5 mm 降到 −5.1 mm，f830 版），ep24 训练后 4096 世界 MP-SR 0.933（第一轮 0.000），对照的是训练用的校正版参考，不是干净真值；ep8 重定向后穿地 33.9 mm，没训。这一版 20 集里 11 集过训练前门槛（|间隙| ≤ 30 mm 且有手部接触）。

10/8 07:30（UTC-6）时：Tier 1 逐条选（10/8 定：按实际交上去的那条轨迹的分数选）：每条 episode 在已训好的 run 里，用 1 世界导出轨迹的本地分数挑一个（本地分数与计分端同一套代码，即排行榜分数），换了 6 条，都换成变体 A。新 CSV 四项都比 10/3 好：AUC 0.3780 → 0.4198、SP-SR 0.5333 → 0.5667、MP-SR 0.2667 → 0.4000（8 → 12 条成功）、MPPE 25.17 → 24.84 cm；没有提交，见 官方评测与提交。另加了一页 成功与失败案例：6 段策略在物理仿真里的 rollout，包括变体 A 物体先丢、约 1.7 s 后摔倒的典型世界。

10/7 06:00（UTC-6）时：第 3 步配方试验出了最终判定。变体 A 只改两个配置取值（物体位置奖励的 σ 从 0.2 m 收到 0.05 m，物体朝向终止从 1.5 rad 收到 0.6 rad），每档在 5 条差一点的 episode 加 1 条对照上试，按两个种子的均值判：Tier 2 通过，5 条的平均 MP-SR 从 0.055 升到 0.989（对照 v5.4 参考），S1–S6 全过；Tier 1 不通过，MP-SR 平均从 0.199 升到 0.758，ADD-AUC、SP-SR 也变好，但平均 MPPE 从 5.00 变成 5.84 cm，超过登记的 +0.5 cm，主要来自 ep18、ep27。Tier 2 铺不铺开、每条交哪一个、Tier 1 下一步怎么走，都待定，见 第 3 步配方试验。Tier 2 降噪只用 Tier 2 的评价试了四个改进，都没过判据，保持 v5.4（见 Tier 2 降噪）。提交：两档 CSV 10/3 打好，提交前的全面检查 C1–C5 两档都过；10/2 的新 kit 不影响这两份 CSV，但要求私有仓库把 Nvidia-V2D-Challenge 邀请为协作者。什么时候交、交哪一档、邀不邀请，都待定；未经 rxzhou 同意不提交（见 官方评测与提交）。

10/3 22:15（UTC-6）时：两档各 30 条都已导出，并打成提交用的 CSV，上传器预检两档都过；没有提交。什么时候交、交哪一档，以及 kit 要求的代码链接怎么让组委会读到（仓库现在私有），待定。缺的 ep7 用带非有限防护的训练副本重训补上：两档的方盒 ep7 都在 1.33–1.43 亿步发散，查清机理后加了防护，闸全部通过，10/2 晚重训的两条都跑完、按批量判据通过（Tier 1 MP-SR 0.653，Tier 2 0.067），见 ep7 防护与重训。方盒另外 4 条训完，按批量判据都不通过（MP-SR 都是 0）。Tier 2 第 1 批 27 条对照 v5.4 参考通过 6 条，这些分数不和 Tier 1 比。MP-SR 为 0 的序列诊断（工作顺序第 2 步）跑完了，但正向对照不过，只报数、不下结论，下一步待定。Kaggle：Tier 1、Tier 2 的 8 个比赛都已加入，tier2-auc 的规则已接受，队伍名已改为 maniphys.ai（rxzhou 手动操作）。10/2 重申：未经 rxzhou 同意不提交。见 官方评测与提交。

Tier 1（截至 9/30 14:40）：第 1 批 21 条逐帧满配全部结束，按批量判定脚本的判据通过 9 条、不通过 9 条、无效 3 条（ep1、ep7、ep28）。连同之前的 9 条，30 条里 27 条有最终 checkpoint 的判定，MP-SR ≥ 0.95 的 5 条、0.5–0.95 的 2 条、0 到 0.5 之间 6 条、恰好为 0 的 14 条。无效的三条都用方盒支撑面：FlashCHORD v0.4.0 的导入器不读 USD 的缩放，把方盒建成边长 1 m 的实心立方体，参考物体在第 0 帧就已嵌进立方体（未核实）。上游 #185 已修。ml40 上的 GPU 对照 14:35 全部跑完，按登记判据 A.3 的最终判定是 H1′ 支持：ep7 冒烟（400 万步、L40S）配对种子 43–50，v0.4.0 的导入器 5 / 8 发散，换上 #185 0 / 8，单侧 Fisher p = 0.0128。能下的结论是 v0.4.0 把方盒建成 1 m 立方体是 ep7 冒烟发散的触发，ep1 只有单种子的旁证、方向相同，ep28 只描述；fp16 存储与 BN 统计量在其中各占多少，这组对照回答不了。组委会 10/1 13:43 重新发布 kit，评测运行时改钉在 8c778f9，这个版本已含 #185。原定要问组委会的前三问因此由 rxzhou 自行定下（决定记录 agent-doc/decision-organizer-q1-q3-2026-10-01.md）：评测与导出用 8c778f9；方盒三条两档各重训一条；只有地面的序列继续用 scene.support=false。方盒三条两档各重训了一条，结果见上。封榜 2026-11-04 17:00 EST，从 10/3 算还有 32 天。

“通过”指批量判定脚本 stages/evaluate/batch_verdict.py 的判据（chord_sr > 0、MP-SR > 0、ADD-AUC 高于这条序列的不动基线），不是排行榜上的成功率。MP-SR 是本地 4096 个世界里成功的比例，排行榜每条 episode 只评 1 个世界。来源：results/day2/batch_verdict_{ml40,mf4090}_batch1{a,b}.txt、results/batch1/error_table_with_mf4090_b.txt、results/ml40/diverge/support_import_v040.txt、results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt、results/diverge/support_py_versions.txt、results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt、agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md（“最终判定”一节）、results/submission/kit_update_diff.txt。运行时与三问的决定：agent-doc/decision-organizer-q1-q3-2026-10-01.md、results/submission/runtime_pin_facts.txt、results/submission/eval_runtime_facts.txt。Tier 2 第 1 批：results/t2/status_{ml40,mf4090}_1300.txt、results/t2/pace_{ml40,mf4090}_t2b_{2000,0700}.txt、results/t2/pace_ml40_t2c_1300.txt、results/t2/batch_verdict_{ml40,mf4090}_t2{a,b}.txt、results/t2/batch_verdict_ml40_t2c.txt、results/t2/ep10_smoke_failure_ml40.txt、agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-01-t2-batch.md。兼容性验证与方盒：results/submission/compat/mf4090_*.txt、results/ml40/submission/compat/ml40_*.txt、results/box/t1ep7_divergence_mf4090.txt、agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-02-box6-eval-runtime.md。提交流程：agent-doc/plan-submission-exports-2026-10-02.md、results/submission/pack/t{1,2}_rehearsal{,2}.txt、agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-02-submission-exports.md。诊断：agent-doc/plan-diag-mpsr0-2026-10-02.md、agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-02-diag-mpsr0.md。Kaggle：results/submission/kaggle_join_status_1002_1300.txt。10/13 的第一次提交、榜单与差距：results/submission/submit_t1x1013.txt.failed、results/submission/submit_t1x1013_mpsr.txt、results/submission/leaderboard_1013_1800.txt、results/t1gap/t1_gap_1013.txt。Tier 3 暂停：results/t3p4/stop_t3p4b_mf4090.txt、results/t3q/stop_t3qa_ml40.txt、results/ops/mf4090_disk_1013_2150.txt；Tier 3 训完的结果：results/t3q/eval_table_t3qa.txt、results/t3p4/eval_table_t3p4b.txt；Tier 1 起链：results/t1x/arm_t1yb_ml40.txt、results/t1x/arm_t1xb_mf4090.txt。

V2D Track 2 working notes

As of 12:01 on 10/20 (UTC-6). Boards (12:01): a new team, Team OPS, submitted to all 12 boards on 10/20 and Javier Medel, MSc. submitted Tier 1; both sit behind maniphys.ai everywhere. Team OPS's Tier 3 SP-SR 0.16666 is second (maniphys.ai still first with 0.26666), pushing FF to third; Open4D's Tier 3 MPPE went from 86.66 to 72.81. maniphys.ai's places did not change: it still leads Tier 2 SP-SR (0.73333) and Tier 3 SP-SR, and is fourth on Tier 1 AUC (0.58258 against DAP 0.60030). Plain rank sums over the 12 boards: FF 22, maniphys.ai 26 under the Kaggle tie rule, 23 and 28 under the site rule; averaging each metric over the tiers first gives 5 and 7 (results/board/diff_1020_1201.txt). Submissions: none since 10:54 on 10/18; a board gets a submission only with a wide lead in both the 4096-world expectation and the exported world (agent-doc/strategy-2026-10-19.md). Whether a submission that only avoids losing a rank, without taking first, gets its own rule is to be decided. The real gap: the current Tier 2 list averages 18.02 cm 4096-world MPPE over 30 episodes; the board's 15.32 is the exported world. A 1 cm lead in expectation needs about 160 cm off the 30-episode sum. The two largest pieces in both Tier 1 and Tier 2 are ep25 (114.10 and 117.09 cm) and ep4 (109.91 and 111.49 cm). Why the all-fail episodes fail (agent-doc/plan-t2dg-2026-10-20.md, agent-doc/plan-t12lift-2026-10-20.md): in every all-fail Tier 2 episode the robot falls (pelvis orientation error above 0.5 rad), and the successful ep0 does not; ep1 and ep4 fall before the reference touches the object (frames 27 and 99), and ep25, ep17, ep9 and ep28 fall 9–50 frames after the object drops. In ep4 the bat stays put in 96.9% of the worlds: the robot never reaches it. Five reference statistics (walking speed, turn rate, pelvis height, waist limits, stance-foot sliding) do not separate failures from successes. Retraining ep25 and ep4 with closed fingers in the reference contact phase was no use. In progress: a fallback, retraining Tier 2 ep25 and ep28 and Tier 1 ep25 on references whose arms do not reach for the object (a still object gives about 49.5 cm for ep25); the two Tier 2 runs on ml40 read out around 13:30 and the Tier 1 run around 17:00; whether to submit is to be decided. Balance: record the pelvis, torso and foot poses in simulation and find what goes wrong first before the falls (ep4, 1 and 25 against ep0 and 16), queued on ml40 after about 17:00. Tier 3: the 11 episodes of the gb4 delivery are retraining on mf4090, on the seventh run (ep18) at 12:01, done around 21:50 with the read-out around 23:30; one more Tier 3 submission opens at 16:14 on 10/22; whether to submit is to be decided (agent-doc/plan-t3g4-2026-10-19.md). ml40: rxzhou's fallback retraining is running (results/ops/status_ml40_1020_1201.txt).

As of 18:30 on 10/17 (UTC-6): Boards (18:15): unchanged since 14:49. Of the 12 boards maniphys.ai leads 5 (Tier 2 AUC, SP-SR and MP-SR; Tier 3 SP-SR and MPPE) and FF leads 7 (all four of Tier 1, Tier 2 MPPE, Tier 3 AUC, and Tier 3 MP-SR, where every team has 0 and ties go to the earlier submission) (results/submission/leaderboard_1017_1815.txt). How the final ranking works (site and FAQ, checked on 10/17, results/ops/challenge_faq_1017_sentences.txt): the ranks in each metric are summed and the lowest sum wins; the three tiers carry equal weight; first on the track wins a DGX Spark, second wins $3K of GPU credits, and a panel picks a separate Industry Innovation Award; results are announced on 11/12 at the CoRL tutorial. How tiers and metrics combine is not spelled out: summing the ranks of the 12 boards (baselines unranked) gives FF 17 and maniphys.ai 20; averaging each metric over the tiers before ranking puts FF ahead on AUC and maniphys.ai ahead on SP-SR and MPPE, with identical MP-SR averages; how that tie is broken is not stated, giving 6 against 6 if it goes to FF and 5 against 7 if it goes to the earlier submitter, maniphys.ai (results/submission/rank_sum_1017_1815.txt). The smallest gaps: Tier 1 AUC 0.00107, Tier 1 MP-SR one episode, Tier 3 MP-SR where the first success takes the lead, Tier 2 MPPE 1.44 cm, Tier 3 AUC 0.107. Submission limit: at most 5 per week per tier (unlimited in the final three days); the last 7 days used 3 for Tier 1, 2 for Tier 2 and 4 for Tier 3. Repository sharing: the organizer account Nvidia-V2D-Challenge is still not a collaborator on the code repository (results/ops/collab_check_nvidia_1017_1830b.txt); the rules require the commit link in every submission to be readable by the organizers, and adding the account needs a Manifold-ai administrator (to do). Tier 3 switch to gb3: the plan agent-doc/plan-t3-gb3-switch-2026-10-17.md (version 2) retrains 8 runs on the gb3 reference: ep20, 0, 22 and 21 on mf4090, ep6, 8, 7 and 29 on ml40. The switch rule was registered before the results: the candidate's 4096-world MPPE at most the current run's plus 4.0 cm, and SP-SR ≥ 0.5. ep21 (side camera) is report-only; whether ep22 may enter the selection is to be decided (on 10/16 ep22 was kept on the old reference because the feet sit 3.7 cm below the floor; gb3's ep22 has −3.77 cm and the old reference −3.76 cm). The preparation is done: 11 episodes reached mf4090 through HF with all hashes matching, the retarget gaps are −0.5 to 0 mm, the relay to ml40 carried 199 files (850.5 MB), and the configuration checks pass on both machines. Each machine starts on its own once the current chain ends and the GPU is free; readings are expected around 07:00 on 10/18. Training: Tier 1 seed 43 on ml40 and Tier 2 ep2, 11 and 17 on mf4090 are on their third run, with results around 21:00 (estimate). Submissions on 10/17: Tier 1 c10_1017a at 14:39 (AUC 0.56315, SP-SR 0.76666, MP-SR 0.66666, MPPE 13.92082 cm) and Tier 3 kfix1017 at 14:47 (MPPE 55.76705 cm, SP-SR 0.13333, AUC 0.04885, MP-SR 0); criterion 6 reads as support for the intrinsics correction.

Diagnosis of the lost Tier 1 episodes (13:28–13:42): the object crosses the threshold 9–32 frames after first contact (ep4 is the exception and crosses before contact); in the 30 frames after first contact the median object self-rotation is 16.4° for the 7 lost episodes and 1.0° for the others (results/t1diag/contact_onset_t1_ml40.txt, results/t1diag/contact_window_mf4090.txt); the contact labels are split into many segments, for example 12 for the left hand of ep28 (results/ml40/t1diag/c10_relabel_ml40.txt). ml40 therefore tries contact anticipation: each contact segment starts 10 frames earlier and gaps of up to 15 frames are filled, with the reference trajectory unchanged; one policy evaluated against the old and the new reference gives the same numbers (results/ml40/t1diag/eval_pair_c10_ep25_ml40.txt). ep25, 28, 11 and 1 train from 16:49, and the verdict comes around 02:00 on 10/17 (estimate; agent-doc/plan-t1-contact-anticipation-2026-10-16.md). Tier 2 ran the same diagnosis on its own data only (18:31): all three features pass and the registered rule sets the lead to 5 frames; in the Tier 2 references of ep28 and ep4 the object moves for a long time with no contact label, a different failure that gets its own plan (results/t2diag/contact_onset_t2_ml40.txt, results/t2diag/motion_before_contact_t2_ml40.txt). The plan for tonight's two extensions (Tier 1 ep9 and ep2; Tier 2 ep25, 9 and 1) is written, and registered conditions decide whether each starts (agent-doc/plan-c10-extend-2026-10-16.md).

As of 07:30 on 10/16 (UTC-6): Boards (06:26): two Tier 3 intrinsics A/B entries went in at 04:34 on 10/16 (decided on 10/16). The entry that rotates the frame references by −4.65° about the floor normal, per the intrinsics calibrated from the ceiling grid, scores MPPE 56.87427 cm and SP-SR 0.03333, first on both boards; AUC 0.00690 is second (Xueyi Wang (FF) 0.15606); MP-SR is 0 for all three teams. The control entry, rotated the other way by +1.20° per the wall-structure calibration, scores MPPE 63.44727 cm, worse than the unrotated probe (60.70360), so the rule registered before the submission reads "supports the ceiling-grid intrinsics" (results/submission/leaderboard_1016_t3ab.txt). maniphys.ai still leads all four Tier 2 boards. FF leads all four Tier 1 boards; DAP submitted Tier 1 at 01:08 on 10/16, and its MPPE of 12.65831 cm is second, ahead of maniphys.ai's 16.55939 cm (results/submission/leaderboard_1016_0630.txt). Tier 3 world frame: for the 24 main-camera episodes the world frame is the main exo camera frame; pitch, roll and camera height follow from the released floor and the room-structure calibration, and this A/B supports the yaw. The main camera's intrinsics are f ≈ 866 with the principal point at about (702, 531); the reconstruction pipeline used f 830 with a centered principal point (768, 576). With the calibrated intrinsics, this alone moves the reference by about 32 cm (median; a difference between two estimates, not an error against the truth). The yaw part is corrected on the boards through the frame references; the depth part needs a new reference: rxzhou regenerated the reference with the calibrated intrinsics for the 7 episodes whose policies track their own reference (ep0, 18, 20, 22, 24, 26, 29), and the source, depth-convention, geometry and floor-reproduction checks all pass; against the old reference, objects sit 5.1% farther from the camera and the person walks 3.1–4.5% farther (agent-doc/plan-t3-kfix-2026-10-16.md, results/t3world/kfix_ref_diff.txt). Feet check: the pooled median foot height over the 7 episodes is 1.55 cm, within 3 cm; per episode only ep22 fails, at −3.71 cm, and the old reference's ep22 sits at −3.76 cm, with 7 of its 24 main-camera episodes beyond 3 cm, so this comes from the body reconstruction itself (results/t3world/kfix_feet_compare.txt). The evidence on the floor correction (a 1.22° rotation and a 0.984 scale) points both ways: by its registered rule the feet check reads "the feet do not show the roll fitted on the objects", but both ends of the interval miss by little (results/t3world/feet_roll.txt). To be decided: re-evaluate and re-export the existing checkpoints against the new reference, retrain on it, or hold; how the new reference reaches mf4090; whether the floor correction stays; how ep22 is handled. The ceiling grid cannot pin the intrinsics of the second and side cameras (6 episodes), so no work on them for now (to be decided). Both controls pass (4096-world MP-SR 0.837 for Tier 1 ep20, 0.735 for Tier 2 ep12), the 6 lost episodes (Tier 1 ep25, 28 and 11; Tier 2 ep25, 28 and 1) all reach 0.000 and read "not useful", and the segment gate reads continue (results/r3o/health_t1oa_mf4090.txt, results/r3o/health_t2oa_ml40.txt). The gap to the baseline: SP-SR needs 4 more successes (21 / 30 for the baseline, 18 / 30 in the submitted CSV), the MPPE sum over the 30 episodes must drop by 204.0 cm and the ADD-AUC sum must rise by 1.173; MP-SR is already ahead (16 against 6). The 9 episodes with MPPE above 15 cm hold 91.0% of the MPPE sum. Tier 3: on 10/13 it was decided to keep the delivery version (neither of yqchen's two depth-correction candidates passes the replacement criteria yqchen registered before the results). yqchen reports four open problems in the current reconstruction: root jitter of the human; a slow object drift of 0.05–0.15 m/s that the current criteria do not catch; the robot palm far from the object after retargeting; no check of the object orientation. yqchen suggested a stop, and on 10/13 it was decided to pause the rest of the Tier 3 training: the reconstruction will change, and runs trained on the current references would then need retraining. At 21:46 both machines got a stop marker: t3p4b on mf4090 has trained 7 episodes and its last one, ep27, is training; t3qa on ml40 (armed at 10:22) stopped at 21:53 after ep8, ep9–11 did not start, and t3qb is not armed; its evaluation ended at 22:20 and its export at 22:26. Against the own reconstruction reference (not the clean trajectories used for scoring), ep7 and ep8 reach 4096-world MP-SR 0.996 and 0.998, ep6 0.198 (SP-SR 0.978), and ep5 and ep4 0 (results/t3q/eval_table_t3qa.txt). The 8 t3p4b episodes on mf4090 finished training at 23:07, and their evaluation and export ended at 23:53: against the own reconstruction reference only ep21 clears the registered 0.5 line (MP-SR 0.501, SP-SR 0.876), and the other 7 have MP-SR 0, including ep12, 16 and 27, which variant A tracks in Tier 2 (results/t3p4/eval_table_t3p4b.txt). When training resumes is to be decided; the preconditions are a new reconstruction from yqchen and orientation and slow-drift checks in the pre-training gate. Between 19:54 and 23:05 on 10/13 yqchen committed an object dynamics check and fix to the Tier 3 branch. The check reads the robot-level reference that training actually uses. It first decides the contact state of each frame (touched, supported or in the air), then decides whether the motion in that frame is physically possible; its checks include the accumulated drift while untouched (3 cm) and a shape-aware rotation drift. The fix changes only the object: symmetric objects take a continuous equivalent orientation from frame to frame, untouched rest segments are locked to one pose, and touched segments absorb the differences at both ends; the human gets no temporal smoothing. The fixed candidate data is best_dyn4. By yqchen's check, 25.5% of object frames violate it for best_hm and 6.3% for best_dyn4 (29 episodes), the episodes that violate it within the first 30 frames drop from 29 to 3, and the image alignment IoU goes from 0.700 to 0.697 (results/t3dyn/handoff_tier3_dynamics_b5203ec.txt). The delivery version is still best_hm; whether to run a training pilot on best_dyn4 (yqchen suggests ep15 and ep3 first, which reach 0.8 or more in Tier 2) is to be decided. The freed GPUs go to Tier 1 first (decided on 10/13). SP-SR is the metric that keeps the submission behind the baseline, so both machines train episodes whose SP-SR fails (variant A, seed 42, plan agent-doc/plan-t1-chase-2026-10-13.md). On ml40, t1yb trains ep16, 2, 3, 19 and 13 with the 10M buffer; with variant A in Tier 2 these 5 reached SP-SR 0.42–0.94; it was armed at 22:29, and all 5 smoke runs passed by 22:55, when the formal runs started. On mf4090, t1xb trains the 5 remaining variant A candidates with the SP-SR failures first (ep3, 19, 13, then ep10, 14; 4M buffer; all smoke runs passed on 10/12), armed at 23:56, once the Tier 3 chains ended. At 07:30, t1yb had trained 4 runs (ep16, 2, 3, 19, about 2 hours each) and ep13 had been training since 07:10; t1xb had trained 3 (ep3, 19, 13), ep10 had been training since 06:15, and ep14 comes last; the watcher scans of both parts found no non-finite values. Evaluation starts only when a whole part is trained: t1yb should finish training at about 09:15 and its evaluation and export at about 09:50, and t1xb at about 10:45 and 11:20 (estimates), followed by a new per-episode selection. The 7 episodes where variant A showed nothing in Tier 2 either (ep1, 4, 9, 11, 17, 25, 28) need a separate look, to be decided. mf4090 had 87508 MB free at 07:30, so the 5th archive batch is not needed for now. Tier 2: b1013, the second selection with the t2xb candidates (packed and checked at 10:26 on 10/13), was first submitted at 00:05 on 10/14, and all four boards took it on the first try: AUC 0.51379, SP-SR 0.73333, MP-SR 0.53333 and MPPE 16.54028 cm. Tier 1 (as of 14:40 on 9/30): all 21 frame-playback full-recipe runs of batch 1 are done: by the batch verdict script's criteria, 9 pass, 9 fail and 3 are invalid (ep1, ep7, ep28). With the 9 earlier runs, 27 of the 30 episodes have a verdict on the final checkpoint. MP-SR is at least 0.95 for 5 of them, between 0.5 and 0.95 for 2, between 0 and 0.5 for 6, and exactly 0 for 14. The three invalid episodes all use box supports: the FlashCHORD v0.4.0 importer ignores the USD scale and builds each box as a solid cube with 1 m edges, and the reference object is already inside the cube at frame 0 (unverified). Upstream #185 fixes this. The GPU controls on ml40 all finished at 14:35, and the final verdict under the registered criteria A.3 is that H1′ is supported: in ep7 smokes (4M steps, L40S) with paired seeds 43–50, 5 of 8 runs with the v0.4.0 importer diverge against 0 of 8 with #185, one-sided Fisher p = 0.0128. What can be concluded is that v0.4.0 building the box as a 1 m cube triggers the divergence of the ep7 smokes; ep1 has only single-seed supporting evidence pointing the same way, and ep28 is description only. These controls cannot say how much fp16 storage and the BatchNorm statistics each contribute. The organizers republished the kit at 13:43 on 10/1 and now pin the evaluation runtime to 8c778f9, which includes #185. The first three questions meant for them were therefore settled without asking (decision record agent-doc/decision-organizer-q1-q3-2026-10-01.md): evaluate and export with 8c778f9; retrain the three box sequences once in each tier; keep scene.support=false for floor-only sequences. The three box sequences were retrained once in each tier, with the results above. The freeze is 2026-11-04 17:00 EST, 32 days from 10/3.

"Pass" means the criteria of the batch verdict script stages/evaluate/batch_verdict.py (chord_sr > 0, MP-SR > 0, ADD-AUC above the sequence's do-nothing baseline), not a leaderboard success rate. MP-SR is the fraction of 4096 local worlds that succeed; the leaderboard evaluates one world per episode.   • Training
    All-fail lift episodes: Tier 1 and Tier 2 togetherThe robot falls in every failing episode and not in the successful ones; in ep4 it never reaches the object, and ep25 and others drop the object soon after the lift and then fall. Fallback: retrain Tier 2 ep25 and ep28 and Tier 1 ep25 on references whose arms do not reach for the object (ml40, read-outs around 13:30 and 17:00; whether to submit is to be decided). Balance: record the pelvis, torso and feet in simulation and find what goes wrong first before the falls, queued on ml40 after about 17:00. A 1 cm lead in expectation needs about 160 cm off the 30-episode Tier 2 sum
    rxzhou

Progress

As of 2026-10-18 07:30 (UTC-6). maniphys.ai has not submitted since 14:47 on 10/17; a new team's Tier 1 AUC of 0.96908 (Vi Nguyễn Văn) moves FF and maniphys.ai down one place on that board, with no change between the two. On equal scores Kaggle puts the earlier submission first and the site orders by team name: summing the 12 boards gives 21 against 18 (Kaggle) or 19 against 20 (site), and averaging over the tiers first gives 5 against 7. Tier 1 seed 43 switches ep11 and ep28, the exported-world SP-SR reaches 0.80000, and all four 4096-world metrics improve; the AUC CSV (local 0.56618, above FF) is packed, and whether to submit it is to be decided. The exported world reads as an independent fair draw. Tier 2 contact anticipation shows no use on ep2, 11 and 17. The two current ep25 picks knock the bottle off the table in almost every world; the checkpoint sweep on mf4090 found none below 100 cm, and ml40 is still evaluating. Tier 3 gb3 switches 5 episodes under rule S, with ep22 and the submission to be decided; yqchen produced gb4 deliveries. Training: Tier 1 ep14 and ep11 seed 44. At 18:30 on 10/17: the boards match 14:49: maniphys.ai leads 5 of the 12 boards and FF leads 7. The site and FAQ state that the final ranking sums the ranks of the metrics with the three tiers weighted equally, but not how they combine: summing the 12 boards gives FF 17 and maniphys.ai 20; averaging over the tiers first gives 6 against 6 or 5 against 7 (how the MP-SR tie is broken is not stated). Each tier allows 5 submissions per week; Tier 3 used 4 in the last 7 days. The 8 gb3 retrains for the Tier 3 switch are ready and start on their own once the current chains end, with readings expected around 07:00 on 10/18; ep22 is to be decided. The organizer account is still not a repository collaborator. At 14:50 on 10/17: Tier 1 and Tier 3 went in on 10/17: Tier 1 improves on all four boards (AUC 0.56315, SP-SR 0.76666, MP-SR 0.66666, MPPE 13.92082) but leads none; Tier 3 leads MPPE (55.76705) and SP-SR (0.13333) and is second on AUC (0.04885), and criterion 6 reads as support for the intrinsics correction. FF submitted Tier 2 again on 10/17 with MPPE 13.87967, so maniphys.ai is second there. Contact anticipation fixes Tier 1 ep2 and improves ep11 and ep28, and shows no use on Tier 2 ep25, 9 and 1; both machines train Tier 1 seed 43 and the other eligible Tier 2 episodes, with results around 21:00. yqchen's gb3 is delivered. At 18:45 on 10/16: FF submitted a new Tier 2 entry at 09:18 on 10/16 with MPPE 14.31296 cm, so FF now leads the Tier 2 MPPE board and maniphys.ai is second; maniphys.ai still leads the other three Tier 2 boards. On the new Tier 3 reference only ep24 and ep26 can switch with the existing checkpoints; mf4090 retrains ep0, 18 and 29, then ep20 with the box table. r3o is “no use” in both tiers and the direction is stopped. The lost Tier 1 episodes fail soon after first contact; ml40 tries contact anticipation on 4 episodes, with a verdict around 02:00 on 10/17; Tier 2 ran the same diagnosis on its own data and the features pass. At 07:30 on 10/16: The Tier 3 world frame is settled: for the 24 main-camera episodes it is the main camera frame, and the reconstruction pipeline used the wrong intrinsics; the intrinsics A/B went on the boards at 04:34 on 10/16, and the ceiling-grid entry is first on MPPE and SP-SR and second on AUC; a new reference with the calibrated intrinsics is generated for 7 episodes and passes its checks, and how to use it is to be decided. None of the 6 lost episodes of the first r3o segments is fixed, and the second segments are training. DAP entered the Tier 1 boards and is second on MPPE, with maniphys.ai third. At 19:30 on 10/15: Tier 3 was submitted for the first time at 16:14 on 10/15 (the probe): first on MPPE with an AUC of 0, read as the alignment of the reconstruction with the truth possibly being the bottleneck. On 10/15 FF raised Tier 1 SP-SR to 0.80000 and leads all four Tier 1 boards; Tier 2 is still first on all four. Re-read at the MP-SR scale, 5 of the 7 lost Tier 1 episodes cross on position first; the first segments of the new recipe r3o (variant A plus an object orientation reward) started on both machines at 14:54 and 15:09. At 07:30 on 10/15: neither tier had been submitted since 16:46 on 10/14, and all 12 boards at 20:00 on 10/14 match 18:30: first on all four Tier 2 boards, first on Tier 1 SP-SR and second on the other three. The Tier 1 reproducibility check failed (the same checkpoint gives a different exported world each time), and on 10/14 selection and reports were set to lead with the 4096-world numbers; both option A runs finished early on 10/15 (ep14 seed 43 0.565; ep19 seed 43 0.030, no more seeds); the frame-by-frame termination analysis shows that the lost episodes lose the object's orientation first. The Tier 3 pilot's main criterion reads unclear, and of the 8 episodes that fail at the start, the frame-by-frame analysis places 6 as either reference objects turning on their own or simulated objects starting far off.

EnvironmentReadymf4090: Isaac Lab 2.3.2 image, upstream v0.4.0, FlashCHORD image. ml40: FlashCHORD image rebuilt (first 11 layers and all 104 dependencies match mf4090); on 9/27 docker, containerd and the repository copy moved to /lab_data, and the post-migration checks match. Since 9/30 ml40 also holds a FlashCHORD source copy with the upstream #185 patch, /lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/fc_src/v040_p185. stages/policy/make_patched_fc_src.sh builds it from third_party/patches/flash_chord-v0.4.0-cube-support-scale-8c778f9.patch, and its support.py has the same md5 as upstream 8c778f905c (4d6eab11…; results/ml40/diverge/support_import_p185.txt, results/diverge/support_py_versions.txt). A queue line with V2D_FC_SRC=<copy> mounts it at /workspace in the container; scripts/dev/in-flash-chord first checks that the copy has a SOURCE_V2D.txt, then prints one mount line to stderr. An empty V2D_FC_SRC=, or none at all, mounts the original upstream v0.4.0 source in third_party/video_to_data. Up to 10/1 the copy served only the divergence investigation. Under the box plan (v2, deployed on the evening of 10/1) the 6 box runs train with it (mf4090 got a copy of its own), and evaluation and export mount a second copy on each machine that is byte-identical to upstream 8c778f9 (mf4090 ~/v2d/fc_src/fc_8c778f9, ml40 /lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/fc_src/fc_8c778f9); all were checked on the evening of 10/1 (results/box/fc_src_{mf4090,ml40}.txt). From 9/28 to 10/7 rxzhou was the only one using either card; since 10/8 yqchen runs Tier 3 reconstruction on the ml40 L40S (yqchen said on 10/8 that it fills the GPU memory), so GPU work on ml40 needs an agreed slot first, while scoring without a GPU goes on as usual (results/ops/gpu_procs_ml40_1009_0735.txt). Training chains still check for other processes before they start. From 10/1, stages/policy/chain_preflight.sh checks the source, the GPU, the image fingerprint, the upstream HEAD, the upstream working tree and the md5 of support.py before a chain starts; both machines gave PREFLIGHT_OK before Tier 2 batch 1 (results/t2/preflight_{ml40,mf4090}.txt). On the evening of 10/2 both machines also built a training copy with the non-finite guard patch, fc_src/v040_p185_nfg (patch v5, checked against a sha256 manifest); the ep7 retrains of both tiers train with it, while evaluation and export still mount fc_8c778f9 (see ep7 guard and retrain); all new runs of the step 3 trial use it too. On 10/5 an idle container on mf4090 that does not belong to rxzhou held the GPU (2.7 GB of memory, 0% utilization); with rxzhou's consent it was stopped at 12:51 and can be restored with docker start at any time (results/ops/mf4090_stop_person_container_1005.txt)

Tier 2 降噪

Tier 2 的噪声是从 0 开始、有上限的随机游走（旋转与平移上限约 30° / 30 mm）。静止段里观测到的增量就是漂移增量，v1 以后的各版本都建立在这一点上。下图为各版本对 Tier 1 真值的 ADD-AUC。

读图

虚线框的 v5.2、v5.3 参数来自 Tier 1，合规有疑问，只作参照。绿色的 v5.4 用于提交，所有参数只由 Tier 2 推出，代价 −0.041，见 ADR-00002。

10/5：只用 Tier 2 的改进评价，保持 v5.4

10/4 定下 Tier 2 降噪改进与第 3 步并行，并且只用 Tier 2 自己的数据验证。计划 agent-doc/plan-tier2-denoise-2026-10-04.md 第 2 版；执行记录 agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-05-t2-denoise-eval.md，结果 results/t2denoise/p1_full.txt（mf4090，10/5 11:35 开跑，6 分钟跑完）。全程在 forbid_tier1() 下，没有读 Tier 1 的任何文件，也没有在 Tier 1 上量任何东西，所以上图那种对 Tier 1 真值的 ADD-AUC 这里没有。

评价换成五项只用 Tier 2 的量：E1 用“全身离物体都远就不可能在动”的物理伪标签量静止误判；E2a 去掉一段的锚点，看这段的倾斜残差；E3 在 v5.4 输出上加净室模型的合成漂移，量对伪真值的 ADD-AUC（唯一看得到偏航与水平位置的一项）；E4 量穿地与“没有施动者却在动”；E5 检查新增的静止段是否超出纯白噪声的 P99。每个候选只改一处，开关全关时重算的 v5.4 与 runs/recovery/tier2_v54 30 条逐位相同。

候选没过的判据主要数（与 v5.4 比）
A1 窗口覆盖G1、G2、G3、E5新增静止段 61.8%（76 / 123）超出白噪声 P99；核心运动帧误判 0.0579；E3 主集平均 0.6568 对 0.6688，逐条最差 −0.181
A1′ 带守门的窗口覆盖G1、G2、G3、E5E5 22.9%（30 / 131）；E3 逐条最差 −0.131
B1 沿用修正G2E2a 倾斜残差变低的段 58%（要 ≥ 60%）；G1、G3、G4 都过
C1 支撑面顶不穿透C1静置段穿支撑面顶的帧 9 对 9（要 ≤ 一半）

没有候选单独通过全部判据，按组合规则保持 v5.4；第 3 步的 Tier 2 若通过，铺开照旧在 v5.4 上训。只报：A1、A1′ 吞进去的多是真实动作（累计 5–18° 或 15–21 mm，在物体开始动、停下的地方）。v5.4 在降噪的坐标里没有陷进支撑面，可重定向之后 ep3、ep6、ep7、ep20 的物体静置时陷进支撑面 12–22 mm（ep20 −22.1 mm）；穿透更可能出在重定向那一步，这是推断，未核实，要不要查待定。

被数据否定的方向（4 条）
方向结果来源

用手锚定物体（完美根也不行）0.225oracle_root.txt

Tier 2 denoising

Tier 2 noise is a capped random walk that starts at zero (caps of about 30° and 30 mm). Inside a static segment, every observed increment is a drift increment, and every version from v1 on builds on that fact. The chart shows ADD-AUC of each version against the Tier 1 ground truth.

Reading the chart

The dashed bars, v5.2 and v5.3, use parameters derived from Tier 1, so their compliance is doubtful; they are shown for reference only. The green bar, v5.4, is the submission version: every parameter comes from Tier 2 alone, at a cost of −0.041. See ADR-00002.

10/5: a Tier-2-only evaluation of improvements; v5.4 stays

On 10/4 it was decided to run Tier 2 denoising improvements in parallel with step 3, validated only on Tier 2's own data. Plan agent-doc/plan-tier2-denoise-2026-10-04.md, version 2; execution record agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-05-t2-denoise-eval.md, results results/t2denoise/p1_full.txt (mf4090, started 11:35 on 10/5, 6 minutes). Everything ran under forbid_tier1(): no Tier 1 file was read and nothing was measured on Tier 1, so there is no ADD-AUC against Tier 1 ground truth like the chart above.

The evaluation uses five Tier-2-only measures instead. E1 measures static-detection errors against physical pseudo-labels (an object cannot move while the whole body is far from it); E2a removes one segment's anchor and looks at that segment's tilt residual; E3 adds synthetic drift from the clean-room model to the v5.4 output and measures ADD-AUC against that pseudo ground truth (the only measure that sees yaw and horizontal position); E4 counts floor penetration and motion with nobody acting on the object; E5 checks whether newly static segments exceed the P99 of pure white noise. Each candidate changes one thing. With every switch off, the recomputed v5.4 is bit-identical to runs/recovery/tier2_v54 on all 30 episodes.

CandidateCriteria failedMain numbers (against v5.4)
A1 window coverG1, G2, G3, E561.8% of new static segments (76 / 123) exceed the white-noise P99; core-motion false-static rate 0.0579; E3 main-set mean 0.6568 against 0.6688, worst episode −0.181
A1′ guarded window coverG1, G2, G3, E5E5 22.9% (30 / 131); E3 worst episode −0.131
B1 carry the correctionG2Tilt residual lower on 58% of E2a segments (needs ≥ 60%); G1, G3 and G4 pass
C1 no penetration of support topsC1Frames through a support top while resting: 9 against 9 (needs at most half)

No candidate passes all its criteria on its own, so by the combination rule v5.4 stays; if Tier 2 passes step 3, it scales out on v5.4 as before. Report only: what A1 and A1′ absorb is mostly real motion (5–18° or 15–21 mm in total, where the object starts or stops moving). In the denoising frame v5.4 does not sink into supports, but after retargeting the resting objects of ep3, ep6, ep7 and ep20 sit 12–22 mm inside a support (ep20 −22.1 mm); the penetration more likely comes from the retargeting step. This is an inference, unverified; whether to look into it is to be decided.

Directions ruled out by data (4)
DirectionResultSource
Pure temporal smoothing+0.001smooth_baseline.txt
Anchoring the object to the hand (fails even with a perfect root)0.225oracle_root.txt
Grasp rigidity: propagating with the hand on clean data0.225, 50° / 136 mm at segment endgrasp_rigidity.txt
Fusing per-frame hand incrementsnoise is 2.1× the drift stepincrement_noise.txt

方盒支撑面在 v0.4.0 里被建成边长 1 m 的实心立方体（ep1 vacuum_left_lean_02 的椅背、ep7 iron_round_table_02 的桌面、ep28 squeegee_sweep_away_from_you_03 的两块椅背板）。v0.4.0 scene/support.py:85–86, 114–116 对 USD Cube 只读 size、不读 xformOp:scale，实际建出来半尺寸都是 (0.5, 0.5, 0.5) m（results/ml40/diverge/support_import_v040.txt）；kit 指定的 aee95f2 同样（results/diverge/support_py_versions.txt）。按这个几何，参考物体从第 0 帧起就在方块里：ep7 896 / 896 帧嵌入超过 5 mm，第 0 帧最深约 49 cm（这条未核实，数字直接取自脚本输出）；ep1 519 / 747 帧；ep28 两块分别 608、651 / 988 帧（results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt）。第 1 批里这三条都没有结果：ep1 从 50,001,920 步起每份 checkpoint 都含 NaN、3 项评测报错，ep28 看守在 60,002,304 步停训，ep7 冒烟后跳过上游 8c778f905c（#185，9/29 12:11）已修，Cube 按 size 乘 xformOp:scale 建。ml40 上的补丁副本（V2D_FC_SRC，见现状）建出的 ep7 桌面半尺寸 (0.42, 0.141, 0.005) m（results/ml40/diverge/support_import_p185.txt）。GPU 对照按登记判据的最终判定（14:35，results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt）：H1′ 支持。ep7 冒烟（400 万步、L40S、fp16 存储）用 v0.4.0 时 8 个种子里 5 条出 NaN，用 #185 时 0 / 8（配对种子 43–50），单侧 Fisher p = 0.0128；ep1 截到 6000 万步，v0.4.0 出 NaN、#185 全部有限（L40S、单种子，只作旁证）。能说的范围：v0.4.0 把方盒建成 1 m 立方体是 ep7 冒烟发散的触发，ep1 的单种子旁证同向；ep28 登记为只描述；fp16 存储与 BN 统计量在其中各占多少说不了。10/1 的新 kit 把评测运行时钉在 8c778f9（含 #185），这三条按 #185 的几何两档各重训一条（方盒计划第 2 版已部署）：t1-ep1、t1-ep28、t2-ep1 训完，t2-ep28、t2-ep7 在训；t1-ep7 的正式训练在约 1.4 亿步发散、被看守止损，说明去掉 1 m 立方体以后 ep7 仍会发散，原因没查。详见发散排查

评测与运行环境

情况处理
评测器只要有一个世界被判终止就整条报错：evaluation must disable task failure termination before scoring the full reference。位姿非有限的世界只把位姿清零、打警告，终止判断仍按全部世界算。v0.4.0（harness.py:525–526）与 aee95f2（harness.py:580–581）相同（results/batch1/termination_facts.txt）第 1 批里 ep1 的 3 项评测分别有 2445、2387、2342 个世界（共 4096）位姿非有限，随后都报这个错（results/batch1/ep1_eval_failure_excerpt.txt）；9/29 ep7 冒烟的 256 世界 rollout 里有 172 个（results/ml40/batch1/ep7_smoke_diag_excerpt.txt）。两次都是方盒序列。官方每条只评 1 个世界：那个世界若被判终止，这条交不出轨迹，而每次提交要有全部 30 条。10/1 自行定下：只有数值发散会触发，含 NaN 的 policy 不导出，报这个错就退到同一条 run 里更早的有限 checkpoint；这一问不再写进邮件（决定记录）
真实时长回放时，G1 绑定参考要求每帧 palm 与关节 FK 相差 ≤ 1e-4 m / 1e-4 rad，插值帧过不了全部 30 条重采样后实测最大 4.78 mm / 2.84e-2 rad（ep10 white_desk_lower_to_ground_10）；只放宽这两个校验容差：ep20 用 2 mm / 5e-3 rad，Day 2 统一用 15 mm / 0.086 rad（最大值的 3 倍），远小于 15 cm / 1.5 rad 的跟踪门槛
训练中把 checkpoint 同步上传成 W&B artifact，客户端核验 token 连不上服务器，训练进程直接退出长跑一律 V2D_WANDB_UPLOAD_CKPT=0：指标照常进 W&B，checkpoint 只存本地（snack 第 1 次尝试）
训练链跑着的时候同步代码，会改掉来历、换掉链后面才调用的脚本链开跑写非空的 runs/.chain-active-*，scripts/dev/remote 见到就拒绝同步（只读查询直接 ssh）；链在每条 run 前比对来历，变了就停。链在跑时要在服务器上跑仓内脚本：只用标准库的 Python 用 scripts/dev/pipe-py 经 ssh stdin 送过去执行；要在一条链之后接一个队列，用 stages/policy/queue_after.sh，先复制到 runs/tools/ 再跑（记 md5），它自己也写标记
stages/policy/fc_config_check.sh 走 fc_run.sh 的打印模式，会先建一个空的 runs/logs/<run>.log（fc_run.sh:47）看 run 日志判断开没开跑、挂的哪个导入器时，空日志不算数；stages/policy/diag_importer_gate.py 把还没有训练段开始行的 run 记“未跑”
mf4090 上 400 万 buffer 训练时整卡读数 23.8 GB（卡 24 GB）XLA 没开预分配、按需扩池，整卡读数反映池子大小；放不放得下只看日志里有没有 RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED / out of memory。Day 2 第一次开链的峰值上限与此不一致，见 Day 2
Mac 往两台服务器上传几 MB 的文件会卡住（换不复用的新连接也卡，卡住时复用连接跟着不通）；下载正常必须上传时切成 512 KB 分块逐块传，在服务器上拼回、核 sha256；卡住的复用连接用 ssh -O exit 重置。诊断尽量在服务器上做，只把 results/ 回流到 Mac
在训练用的卡上开查看器会拖慢训练：9/29 10:32–11:46 在 ml40 上开了 3 个 viser、录了 3 条 rerun，ep0 每 1000 万步从 3 分 42 秒变成 5 分，多用约 20 分钟看效果优先录短的 rerun .rrd（每条约 3 分钟 GPU）拿回 Mac 看，或等训完再开 viser；训练中要开就限时（results/ml40/batch1/pace_ml40_a.txt）
旧版留下的 *_g1_support.usda会被优先加载且已不对齐，重定向前删除
mf4090 连 GitHub 时连接被重置在 Mac 上打 git bundle，传过去再 fetch

Special cases

As of 14:40 on 9/30. Two tables: sequences and references, then evaluation and runtime. The box-support row concerns three episodes, ep1, ep7 and ep28, and the submission as well.

Sequences and references

CaseHandling
v0.4.0 builds box supports as solid cubes with 1 m edges (the chair back in ep1 vacuum, the tabletop in ep7 iron, the two chair-back plates in ep28 squeegee). v0.4.0 scene/support.py:85–86, 114–116 reads only size from a USD Cube and ignores xformOp:scale; built for real, all of them come out with half extents (0.5, 0.5, 0.5) m (results/ml40/diverge/support_import_v040.txt). aee95f2, the runtime the kit names, does the same (results/diverge/support_py_versions.txt). With that geometry the reference object sits inside the block from frame 0: ep7 is embedded more than 5 mm in 896 of 896 frames, about 49 cm deep at frame 0 (unverified; the numbers are copied from the script output); ep1 in 519 of 747 frames; ep28 in 608 and 651 of 988 frames for its two plates (results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt). None of the three has a batch 1 result: every ep1 checkpoint from 50,001,920 steps on contains NaN and its 3 evaluations failed, the watcher stopped ep28 at 60,002,304 steps, and ep7 was skipped after its smoke runUpstream 8c778f905c (#185, 12:11 on 9/29) fixes it by building a Cube as size times xformOp:scale. The patched copy on ml40 (V2D_FC_SRC, see Status) builds the ep7 tabletop with half extents (0.42, 0.141, 0.005) m (results/ml40/diverge/support_import_p185.txt). Final verdict of the GPU controls under the registered criteria (14:35, results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt): H1′ is supported. In the ep7 smoke runs (4M steps, L40S, fp16 storage), 5 of 8 seeds hit NaN with v0.4.0 and 0 of 8 with #185 (paired seeds 43–50), one-sided Fisher p = 0.0128; ep1 truncated at 60M steps hits NaN with v0.4.0 and stays finite with #185 (L40S, single seed, supporting evidence only). What this covers: v0.4.0 building the boxes as 1 m cubes is the trigger of the ep7 smoke divergence, and the single-seed ep1 evidence points the same way; ep28 is registered as description only; how much fp16 storage and the BN statistics each contribute is not established. The 10/1 kit pins the evaluation runtime to 8c778f9 (which includes #185), and the three episodes are retrained once per tier on the #185 geometry (box plan v2 deployed): t1-ep1, t1-ep28 and t2-ep1 are trained, t2-ep28 and t2-ep7 are training; the t1-ep7 full run diverged at about 140M steps and was stopped by the watcher, so ep7 still diverges without the 1 m cube, for reasons not yet investigated. On mf4090, runs/flash_chord/t1-ep28-w2048-cap4m-mf4090 still holds the states at 50,001,920 and 60,002,304 steps (the matching policies both contain non-finite parameters) and no DONE; fc_run removed the STOP marker when it exited (fc_run.sh:211–212). Rerunning the same run name directly with fc_run.sh resumes from the NaN state at 60,002,304 steps (fc_run.sh:161–174, agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md), and the batch chain marks it SKIPPED for leftover state without DONE (day2_train_chain.sh:94), so move the folder away before retraining

Expected results are still reported as a range; whether a run diverged is decided by the checkpoint scan (stages/policy/ckpt_nonfinite.py, --watch for a watcher)
ep1 vacuum leaning on a chair back, Tier 1 gap −22.5 mmIt does not rest flat; confirm on replay and leave the data unchanged. The chair back is a box support (see the first row). In the Tier 1 reference the vacuum is more than 5 mm below the floor in 385 of 747 frames (deepest 23.8 mm); ep17 (123 of 440 frames, deepest 16.8 mm) and ep22 (136 of 719 frames, 13.8 mm) go through the floor too, and both trained to the end without diverging (results/diverge/support_penetration{,_floor_controls}.txt)
ep16 spray bottle (spray_bottle_left_desk_ground_02; radius 0.16 m, path 5.27 m, left hand only, 184 / 0 contact frames left / right) trained without NaN, but ends with a mean episode length of only 118.7 and return 15.25, against 374–552 and 68–133 for the other four runs of the segment (results/diverge/train_summary_table_mf4090_b.txt)Evaluation: MP-SR 0, chord 0, SP-SR 1 of 4096, ADD-AUC 0.255 below the do-nothing baseline of 0.266, mean position error 63.7 cm and orientation error 85.4° (results/day2/batch_verdict_mf4090_batch1b.txt, results/batch1/error_table_with_mf4090_b.txt). It fails the criteria; it is not a divergence. It goes into the improvement plans with the other failing sequences (see Next steps). Whether one-handed contact and the long path are why it does not learn has not been checked
ep29 luggage has zero contact frames for both hands (both tiers)The sequence is a left-foot push: in Tier 1 both hands stay more than 10 cm from the object, and the left foot is within 5 cm in 61% of the frames where the object moves. Do not raise the contact threshold; that would turn Tier 2 noise into fake contacts (results/luggage_contact.txt, corrected 9/27). Batch 1 trained it with the unchanged recipe and it passes the criteria: MP-SR 0.174, SP-SR 0.989, ADD-AUC 0.658 (do-nothing baseline 0.231; results/day2/batch_verdict_mf4090_batch1b.txt)
In Tier 2 the ep7 iron and the ep20 rice cooker sink about 2 cm into the tabletop (−20.7 mm and −22.1 mm under v5.4; agent-doc/progress-2026-09-25.md)The no-penetration constraint only covers the floor; check whether training is affected first. In the Tier 1 support file the ep7 tabletop is a box, which v0.4.0 and aee95f2 build as a whole 1 m cube; the support files Tier 2 uses have not been checked. Revisit once the importer question is settled
ep17 traffic cone already 23.5 mm below the floor at frame 0 in the Tier 2 observationAn inherent error between the ground plane and the reconstruction; the no-penetration constraint lifts it about 14 mm. Recorded, not fixed
ep11 has 901 source frames, but the official sample CSV has 900 frames for e000011 (frames 0–899)Training keeps all 901 frames; when writing the packing script, check how the packer treats the extra frame (not tested yet; frame counts from the kit excerpts and the smoke log)
For 16 of the 30 Tier 1 sequences the official support file is an empty Xform (no Cube or Cylinder collider), and FlashCHORD fails to build the sceneTrain these sequences with scene.support=false; the floor stays. All 30 sequences build a scene that way (results/day2/palm_frame_probe_all.txt). Of the other 14, 11 use cylinders and 3 use boxes (ep1, ep7, ep28). On 10/1 it was decided to keep this handling (decision record), and email draft v2 mentions it in one sentence

Evaluation and runtime

CaseHandling
The evaluator raises an error for the whole run as soon as one world is marked terminated: evaluation must disable task failure termination before scoring the full reference. Worlds with non-finite poses only get their poses zeroed and a warning; termination is still checked over all worlds. v0.4.0 (harness.py:525–526) and aee95f2 (harness.py:580–581) are the same (results/batch1/termination_facts.txt)In batch 1 the 3 evaluations of ep1 had 2445, 2387 and 2342 worlds (of 4096) with non-finite poses, and each then raised this error (results/batch1/ep1_eval_failure_excerpt.txt); the 256-world rollout of the ep7 smoke run on 9/29 had 172 (results/ml40/batch1/ep7_smoke_diag_excerpt.txt). Both are box sequences. The official evaluation uses one world per episode: if that world is marked terminated, the episode yields no trajectory, and every submission needs all 30. Decided on 10/1: only a numerical blow-up triggers it, a policy with NaN is never exported, and this error means falling back to an earlier finite checkpoint of the same run; the question is dropped from the email (decision record)
At real-time playback, the G1 binding requires every palm frame to match the joint FK within 1e-4 m / 1e-4 rad, which interpolated frames failMeasured over all 30 resampled sequences, the largest deviation is 4.78 mm / 2.84e-2 rad (ep10 white desk). Only these two check tolerances are relaxed: 2 mm / 5e-3 rad for ep20, and 15 mm / 0.086 rad (3× the maximum) for Day 2, far below the 15 cm / 1.5 rad tracking limits
Uploading a checkpoint as a W&B artifact during training: when the client cannot reach the server to verify the token, the training process exitsLong runs always set V2D_WANDB_UPLOAD_CKPT=0: metrics still go to W&B, checkpoints stay local (first snack attempt)
Syncing code while a training chain runs changes the recorded source and swaps scripts the chain calls laterA chain writes a non-empty runs/.chain-active-* when it starts, and scripts/dev/remote refuses to sync while one exists (read-only queries go over plain ssh); the chain compares the source before every run and stops if it changed. To run a repository script on a server while a chain runs: a stdlib-only Python script goes over ssh stdin with scripts/dev/pipe-py; to queue work behind a chain, copy stages/policy/queue_after.sh to runs/tools/ first (recording its md5) and run it from there, and it writes its own marker
stages/policy/fc_config_check.sh goes through the print mode of fc_run.sh, which creates an empty runs/logs/<run>.log first (fc_run.sh:47)When a run log is used to tell whether a run started or which importer it mounted, an empty log counts for nothing; stages/policy/diag_importer_gate.py marks a run without a training-segment start line as not run
With a 4M buffer on mf4090 the whole card reads 23.8 GB (24 GB card)XLA runs without preallocation and grows its pool on demand, so the whole-card reading reflects the pool size; whether the run fits is decided only by RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED / out of memory in the log. The first Day 2 chain used a peak limit that disagreed with this, see Day 2
Uploads of a few MB from the Mac to either server stall (a fresh, unshared connection stalls too, and the shared connection hangs with it); downloads are fineWhen an upload is unavoidable, send it in 512 KB chunks, join them on the server and check the sha256; reset a hung shared connection with ssh -O exit. Diagnose on the servers where possible and bring only results/ back to the Mac
A viewer on a training card slows training: from 10:32 to 11:46 on 9/29, three viser viewers and three rerun recordings on ml40 took ep0 from 3 min 42 s to 5 min per 10M steps, about 20 minutes lostTo look at a policy, prefer a short rerun .rrd recording (about 3 GPU minutes each) viewed on the Mac, or open viser after training; time-box it if it must run during training (results/ml40/batch1/pace_ml40_a.txt)
Leftover *_g1_support.usda from the old retargeterIt is loaded first and is misaligned; delete it before retargeting
Connections from mf4090 to GitHub get resetPack a git bundle on the Mac, copy it over, then fetch

Next steps and to-dos

As of 07:30 on 10/18 (UTC-6). The order of work was set at 23:50 on 10/1: 1. first finish the submission flow and the exports of all 30 episodes; 2. then pick 2–3 sequences with MP-SR 0 and diagnose whether the failure lies in the approach, the grasp or the tracking; 3. finally try one modified recipe on about 5 sequences and scale it up only if it passes the criteria. It was also decided not to submit anything for now, with maniphys.ai as the team name. On 10/4 it was decided to do step 3 first: try the modified recipe on about 5 sequences, and scale out to 30 only if it passes the registered criteria; Tier 2 denoising improvements run in parallel with step 3, are validated only on Tier 2's own data, and also need a plan first. After the first Tier 1 submission on 10/13 the goal was set: first beat the CHORD baseline, then try to beat Xueyi Wang (FF); the same day it was decided to pause the rest of the Tier 3 training and give the freed GPUs to Tier 1 first. On 10/14 both tiers were ahead of the CHORD baseline and the new goal became first place on every Tier 1 and Tier 2 board (plan agent-doc/plan-first-place-2026-10-14.md); the same day Tier 3 started a training pilot on the dynamics-fixed versions. On 10/16 the Tier 3 intrinsics A/B submission was decided; the same day it was decided to re-evaluate and re-export the existing checkpoints on the new reference and to retrain the episodes that fail on it; r3o stopped after both tiers read “no use”; the same day it was decided that Tier 2 goes in whenever the registered selection finds a better candidate. On 10/17 it was decided to submit Tier 1 c10_1017a and Tier 3 kfix1017, and to run Tier 2 on the other registered eligible episodes. On the evening of 10/17 Tier 2 contact anticipation read “no use”, and the direction is not pursued further in Tier 2; on 10/18 it was decided to sweep the existing ep25 checkpoints first and to add seed 44 for Tier 1 ep14 and ep11. rxzhou owns every item below; anything that uses a GPU or changes the recipe gets a plan before it runs.

训练实验

Day 1 之后做了三次实验、一次查证；官方 kit 发布后 Day 2 改为固定逐帧；第 1 批 21 条 Tier 1 的最后一项评测在 9/30 12:35 结束：通过 9 条、不通过 9 条、无效 3 条（ep7 没有正式训练，ep1 评测失败，ep28 止损后没评）。这三条另开了发散排查：v0.4.0 把它们的方盒支撑面建成 1 m 立方体；GPU 对照 9/30 14:35 跑完，按登记判据的最终判定，这是 ep7 冒烟发散的触发（H1′ 支持，8 个配对种子，单侧 Fisher p = 0.0128），ep1 只有单种子旁证，ep28 只描述。10/1 00:49 起跑 Tier 2 第 1 批（27 条），训练链加了发散检测，10/2 10:09 全部评完，按批量判据通过 6 条，见 Tier 2 第 1 批。10/2 两台先做 8c778f9 兼容性验证（都通过），再重训方盒三条：ep1、ep28 两档都训完、都不通过；两档的 ep7 在 1.33–1.43 亿步发散，查清机理后加了非有限防护，10/2 晚重训的两条都按批量判据通过，见 ep7 防护与重训。10/4 起做第 3 步：变体 A 只改两个配置取值，在每档 5 条 episode 加 1 条对照上试；10/7 凌晨出了最终判定：Tier 2 通过，Tier 1 不通过（S4 的 MPPE），见 第 3 步配方试验。10/8–10/9 在 Tier 1 的 ep0、ep15、ep5 上补跑变体 A 两个种子，只服务于逐条选。每次都先登记判据再跑，结论由脚本按判据给出。核对没过的结论标“未核实”。

  • snack 满配对照官方配方在官方样例上能不能过线不通过（边缘）
  • 回放速度对照1.67 倍速对真实时长，转动落后量有没有差别无差别
  • 官方评测与提交kit 定下的评测口径、提交流程、还缺什么固定逐帧
  • Day 2新序列上的逐帧满配能不能过线，两种回放怎么选2/8 通过
  • 第 1 批批量剩下 21 条 Tier 1 的逐帧满配：ep23、ep24 等 9 条通过，ep1、ep7、ep28 无效9/21 通过
  • 发散排查ep1、ep7、ep28 为什么训出 NaN：v0.4.0 把它们的方盒支撑面建成 1 m 立方体，8 个种子的 GPU 对照判定它是 ep7 冒烟发散的触发H1′ 支持

怎么读这些分数

“不动基线”是物体一直停在第 0 帧参考位姿时的 ADD-AUC，随序列和回放方式变（ep20 是 0.471，snack 样例 0.566，Day 2 的 ep13 只有 0.165），及格线用各自的基线。分数只在同一序列、同一评测回放内可比。

表里的数字逐个对过判定脚本的输出。

Training experiments

After Day 1 rxzhou ran three experiments and one fact-check; after the official kit came out, Day 2 switched to frame-by-frame playback only. The last evaluation of batch 1's 21 Tier 1 runs ended at 12:35 on 9/30: 9 pass, 9 fail, 3 are invalid (ep7 never got its full training run, ep1's evaluation failed, ep28 was stopped and not evaluated). These three got their own divergence investigation: v0.4.0 builds their box supports as 1 m cubes. The GPU controls finished at 14:35 on 9/30, and the final verdict under the registered criteria is that this triggers the divergence of the ep7 smokes (H1′ supported, 8 paired seeds, one-sided Fisher p = 0.0128); ep1 has only single-seed supporting evidence, and ep28 is description only. Tier 2 batch 1 (27 runs) started at 00:49 on 10/1 with divergence detection added to the training chain and was fully evaluated by 10:09 on 10/2, with 6 runs passing the batch criteria; see Tier 2 batch 1. On 10/2 both machines ran the 8c778f9 compatibility check (both pass) and then retrained the three box sequences: ep1 and ep28 finished in both tiers and all fail; the ep7 runs of both tiers diverged between 133M and 143M steps, and once the mechanism was found rxzhou added a non-finite guard, with which both ep7 retrains of 10/2 evening pass the batch criteria; see ep7 guard and retrain. Step 3 started on 10/4: variant A changes two config values and is tried on 5 episodes plus 1 control per tier; the final verdicts came early on 10/7: Tier 2 passes and Tier 1 fails (the MPPE part of S4); see Step 3 recipe trial. On 10/8–10/9 variant A was run with two seeds on Tier 1 ep0, ep15 and ep5, for per-episode selection only. Every experiment registered its criteria before running, and a script produced the verdict from them. Conclusions that did not pass the check are marked unverified.

The divergence investigation is counted from 08:57, when the plan was registered (af261bf) and the ep28 watcher started; see agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md, final verdict results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt (the interim verdict at 4 seeds is kept as results/diverge/diag_verdict_seeds43_46.txt), ep28 stop results/diverge/ep28_nan_watch_log.txt.

How to read these scores

The do-nothing baseline is the ADD-AUC of an object that stays at its frame-0 reference pose. It changes with the sequence and the playback (0.471 for ep20, 0.566 for the snack sample, only 0.165 for Day 2's ep13), and every pass bar uses its own baseline. Scores compare only within one sequence and one evaluation playback.

Each number in the tables was compared with the verdict scripts' output.

任务一览

Track 2 的 30 条 episode，每条一张卡：英文任务名（数据集的序列名去掉日期前缀）、官方英文任务原文、两段视频。左边是 exo 相机里的人类示范，右边是重定向到 G1 之后的参考动作，也就是 policy 要去跟的东西。

  • 人类示范取自数据集的 exo 相机视频（这一路视频三档逐字节相同），经重编码，2 倍速、640 宽；数据集许可 CC BY 4.0。
  • G1 参考用的是 FlashCHORD 回放脚本的运动学参考模式：每一帧直接把机器人和物体放到参考位姿，不跑物理，所以它说明的是“要做成什么样”，不代表 policy 的表现，也不说明这条参考在物理上做得到。支撑面只画了顶面（蓝色），桌子本身不在仿真场景里。ep1、ep7、ep28 的方盒支撑面按上游 #185 的几何画；v0.4.0 会把它们建成 1 m 立方体，见发散排查。
  • 卡片上的判定与 MP-SR、SP-SR 是 Tier 1、逐帧满配、最终 checkpoint、本地 4096 个世界的结果，判定标准见第 1 批批量，不是排行榜分数；来源 results/batch1/error_table_with_mf4090_b.txt 与 results/day2/batch_verdict_*.txt。
  • 素材清单 results/site/media_manifest.txt；生成脚本 stages/evaluate/site_media_exo.sh、stages/policy/site_media_ref.sh、stages/policy/site_ref_replay.py、tools/site_task_gallery.py，计划 agent-doc/plan-site-media-2026-09-30.md。视频进入视口时才加载。

Task gallery

All 30 Track 2 episodes, one card each: the English task name (the dataset sequence name without its date prefix), the official English task text, and two videos. On the left is the human demonstration from the exo camera; on the right is the reference motion after retargeting to the G1, which is what the policy has to track.

  • The human demos come from the dataset's exo camera video (byte-identical across the three tiers), re-encoded at 2× speed and 640 px wide; the dataset is licensed CC BY 4.0.
  • The G1 reference uses the kinematic-reference mode of the FlashCHORD replay script: every frame places the robot and the object directly at the reference pose, with no physics. It shows what the task should look like; it says nothing about how a policy performs or whether the reference is physically feasible. Support surfaces are drawn by their top only (blue); the tables themselves are not part of the simulated scene. The box supports of ep1, ep7 and ep28 use the upstream #185 geometry; v0.4.0 builds them as 1 m cubes, see Divergence investigation.
  • The verdicts, MP-SR and SP-SR on the cards are Tier 1, frame-playback full recipe, final checkpoint, 4096 local worlds; the criteria are on Batch 1. They are not leaderboard scores. Sources: results/batch1/error_table_with_mf4090_b.txt and results/day2/batch_verdict_*.txt.
  • Media list in results/site/media_manifest.txt; generated by stages/evaluate/site_media_exo.sh, stages/policy/site_media_ref.sh, stages/policy/site_ref_replay.py and tools/site_task_gallery.py, plan agent-doc/plan-site-media-2026-09-30.md. Videos load when they scroll into view.
