Day 1b 逐帧诊断

Day 1 的评测只给汇总分数。这一步把三个 policy 的评测 rollout 存下来逐帧拆开：ep20 满配（主）、ep20 100 万 buffer、官方样例 snack 100 万（对照）。9/27 19:53–20:24 在 mf4090 上跑，同一张 4090、4096 世界。七道有效性闸（形状、包装前后一致、重算 ADD-AUC 一致、朝向误差一致、参考的已知答案、逐世界成功率、几何自检）三个 policy 全过。

ep20 满配ep20 100 万snack 100 万
全程 ADD-AUC（不动）0.4315（0.4708）0.4213（0.4708）0.5410（0.5662）
第 1 段贡献差（操作前静止）−0.027−0.033−0.032
第 2 段贡献差（操作窗口）+0.032+0.022+0.006
第 3 段贡献差（操作后）−0.045−0.0380.000
中位净转动比（需要）0.70（0.72）0.76（0.72）0.56（0.55）
操作段 palm 误差中位5.1 cm9.2 cm62.7 cm
参考手在接触帧上离物体（中位）右 5.9 cm右 5.9 cm左 2.6、右 2.8 cm
成功世界（4096 个里）3285620

“贡献差”是这一段对全程 ADD-AUC 的贡献减去“物体不动”在同一段的贡献，三段相加等于全程差；负数表示这一段不如不动。

触发了什么（事先登记的数值条件）

  • T1 触发：非操作段（第 1、3 段）占全程 ADD-AUC 差距的 182%（主）、126%（对照），都 ≥ 50%，按登记归到配方层：查接近与抓取、reset 与 VOC 设置。
  • T2 触发：中位净转动比 0.70，低于第 420 帧不越门槛所需的 0.72，下一步做回放速度对照（已做，见回放速度对照）。
  • T3 未触发：操作段 palm 误差中位 5.1 cm，低于 7.5 cm。
  • T4 触发：参考手在接触帧上离物体 5.9 cm，对照 2.6 cm，差 ≥ 2 cm，下一步查重定向。限定：两条的物体与抓法不同，这个差也可能来自物体形状。

描述（不设阈值）

  • Day 1 撤回的“没跟上转动”确实不成立：ep20 满配 82% 的世界净转过参考的一半，中位转了 70%（第 209 帧到末帧净转 212°，参考 307°）。
  • ep20 满配的失分分三块：操作前就把 rice cooker 预转约 20°（第 209 帧朝向误差中位 20°，第 1 段 −0.027）；操作窗口内比不动好（+0.032）；操作结束后物体停在比参考少转约 75° 的位置（第 552 帧朝向误差中位 74.8°，第 3 段 −0.045）。T1 把第 1、3 段算在一起，其中第 3 段实际是转动不足留下的残差。
  • snack 100 万的失分几乎全在静止前缀：盒子在前 446 帧被碰动（位置误差均值 1.4 cm，不动为 0）。

记录 agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-28-day1b.md；报告 results/day1/diag_*.txt；触发判定 results/day1/diag_triggers.txt。

Day 1b frame-by-frame diagnosis

The Day 1 evaluation only reports aggregates. This step saved the evaluation rollouts of three policies and took them apart frame by frame: full-recipe ep20 (main), ep20 with a 1M buffer, and the official snack sample with a 1M buffer (control). It ran on mf4090 from 19:53 to 20:24 on 9/27, on the same 4090 with 4096 worlds. All three policies passed the seven validity gates (shapes, identical results with and without the wrapper, recomputed ADD-AUC, orientation error, known answers of the reference, per-world success, geometric self-check).

ep20 full recipeep20 1Msnack 1M
Full-run ADD-AUC (do-nothing)0.4315 (0.4708)0.4213 (0.4708)0.5410 (0.5662)
Segment 1 contribution gap (static, before manipulation)−0.027−0.033−0.032
Segment 2 (manipulation window)+0.032+0.022+0.006
Segment 3 (after manipulation)−0.045−0.0380.000
Median net rotation ratio (required)0.70 (0.72)0.76 (0.72)0.56 (0.55)
Median palm error, manipulation segment5.1 cm9.2 cm62.7 cm
Reference hand to object at contact frames (median)right 5.9 cmright 5.9 cmleft 2.6, right 2.8 cm
Successful worlds (of 4096)3285620

A segment's contribution gap is its contribution to the full-run ADD-AUC minus the do-nothing contribution over the same segment; the three add up to the full-run gap, and a negative value means the segment did worse than not moving.

What fired (registered numeric conditions)

  • T1 fired: segments 1 and 3 account for 182% of the ADD-AUC gap for the main policy and 126% for the control, both ≥ 50%, so by the registration this is a recipe-level issue: check approach and grasp, reset and VOC settings.
  • T2 fired: the median net rotation ratio is 0.70, below the 0.72 needed to stay inside the limit at frame 420; the next step was a playback speed comparison (done, see Playback speed comparison).
  • T3 did not fire: the median palm error in the manipulation segment is 5.1 cm, below 7.5 cm.
  • T4 fired: the reference hand is 5.9 cm from the object at contact frames against 2.6 cm for the control, a gap of at least 2 cm; the next step is to check retargeting. Caveat: the two sequences differ in object and grip, so the gap may come from object shape.

Description (no thresholds)

  • The claim withdrawn after Day 1, that the policy does not follow the rotation, is indeed wrong: 82% of full-recipe ep20 worlds turn more than half of the reference's net rotation, with a median of 70% (212° net from frame 209 to the end, against 307° in the reference).
  • The full-recipe ep20 policy loses in three places: it pre-turns the cooker about 20° before manipulation starts (median orientation error 20° at frame 209, segment 1 −0.027); inside the manipulation window it beats doing nothing (+0.032); after manipulation the object rests about 75° short of the reference (median orientation error 74.8° at frame 552, segment 3 −0.045). T1 counts segments 1 and 3 together; segment 3 is really the leftover of the under-rotation.
  • The 1M snack policy loses almost everything in the static prefix: the box gets bumped during the first 446 frames (mean position error 1.4 cm, zero for doing nothing).

Record agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-28-day1b.md; reports results/day1/diag_*.txt; trigger verdict results/day1/diag_triggers.txt.

snack 满配对照

目的是分清 Day 1 的失败出在配方与环境，还是出在 ep20 这条数据。用官方 G1 配方，只给官方样例 parquet，1000 万 buffer、349,999,104 步、种子 42，在 ml40 上训练，4096 世界评测。判据事先登记：chord_sr > 0、maniptrans_sr > 0、ADD-AUC 高于这条序列的不动基线 0.5662；“> 0”只靠 ≤ 3 个世界，或 ADD-AUC 落在门槛 ±0.01 内，另标“边缘”。

第 1 次尝试：中途退出，未判定

9/27 19:56 开始，20:01 写出第一个 checkpoint（10,000,384 步）。20:02 要把它上传成 W&B artifact 时，客户端核验 API token 连不上服务器（AuthenticationError），异常没被接住，训练进程退出。按登记不续训。第 2 次从 0 重来，关掉 checkpoint 上传（V2D_WANDB_UPLOAD_CKPT=0，指标照常进 W&B），判据不变。

第 2 次尝试：不通过（边缘）

9/28 07:32 开始，一段跑完，09:32 评完。

项满配（第 2 次，ml40）100 万（Day 1，mf4090）门槛
chord_sr0.922（3775 / 4096）0> 0 → 过
maniptrans_sr0（0 / 4096）0.00024（1 个世界）> 0 → 不过
ADD-AUC0.56390.5403> 0.5662 → 不过，差 −0.0023
spider_sr_uncentered0.967—只描述
  • 按登记三项要全过，所以不通过；ADD-AUC 落在门槛 ±0.01 内，另标边缘。边缘不走 SOP 的因果分支，下一步按诊断证据定，或补一个种子。
  • 官方配方在官方样例上任务原生成功率 92%，物体位置误差比不动低约 56 cm，ADD-AUC 仍略低于不动。
不下的结论
  • 100 万与满配之差不能单归因于 buffer：两条还换了机器（4090 对 L40S），各只有一个种子（未核实）。
  • 满配这条的失分是否也出在静止前缀（前 440 帧里约 317 帧是上游加的前缀），目前只有 100 万那条的逐帧诊断支持，属推测（证据间接）。
  • 官方样例 parquet 的 fps 列是 200，采集帧率未知，不对它的回放速度下结论。

记录 agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-28-day1b.md 的 A 节；判定 results/day1/verdict_snackbox_cap10m.txt；第 1 次的原始日志摘录 results/ml40/day1/snack_attempt1_*.txt。

Full-recipe snack control

The goal is to tell whether the Day 1 failure comes from the recipe and environment or from the ep20 data. Official G1 recipe, given only the official sample parquet, 10M buffer, 349,999,104 steps, seed 42, trained on ml40 and evaluated with 4096 worlds. Criteria registered in advance: chord_sr > 0, maniptrans_sr > 0, and ADD-AUC above this sequence's do-nothing baseline of 0.5662. A "> 0" that rests on ≤ 3 worlds, or an ADD-AUC within ±0.01 of the bar, is also marked marginal.

Attempt 1: exited midway, no verdict

Started at 19:56 on 9/27 and wrote its first checkpoint (10,000,384 steps) at 20:01. At 20:02, when uploading it as a W&B artifact, the client could not reach the server to verify the API token (AuthenticationError); nothing caught the exception and the training process exited. As registered, it was not resumed. Attempt 2 restarted from zero with checkpoint upload off (V2D_WANDB_UPLOAD_CKPT=0; metrics still go to W&B), with the same criteria.

Attempt 2: fail (marginal)

Started at 07:32 on 9/28, ran in one segment, evaluated by 09:32.

ItemFull recipe (attempt 2, ml40)1M buffer (Day 1, mf4090)Bar
chord_sr0.922 (3775 / 4096)0> 0 → pass
maniptrans_sr0 (0 / 4096)0.00024 (1 world)> 0 → fail
ADD-AUC0.56390.5403> 0.5662 → fail, −0.0023
spider_sr_uncentered0.967—Descriptive
  • As registered, all three items must pass, so this is a fail; ADD-AUC is within ±0.01 of the bar, so it is also marked marginal. A marginal result takes no causal branch of the SOP; the next step follows the diagnostic evidence or adds a seed.
  • On the official sample, the official recipe succeeds in 92% of worlds by the task-native criteria and its object position error is about 56 cm below doing nothing, yet its ADD-AUC is still slightly below doing nothing.
Conclusions not drawn
  • The gap between the 1M and full runs cannot be put on the buffer alone: the two runs also differ in machine (4090 against L40S) and have one seed each (unverified).
  • Whether the full run also loses in the static prefix (about 317 of the first 440 frames are a prefix added upstream) is supported only by the 1M run's diagnosis, so it is a guess (indirect evidence).
  • The sample parquet's fps column says 200 and its capture rate is unknown, so no conclusion is drawn about its playback speed.

Record: section A of agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-28-day1b.md; verdict results/day1/verdict_snackbox_cap10m.txt; log excerpts of attempt 1 in results/ml40/day1/snack_attempt1_*.txt.

回放速度对照

Day 1b 的 T2 触发（转动跟不上）。FlashCHORD 的 G1 配方默认逐帧回放：每个 50 Hz 控制步走一帧 30 fps 的参考，动作按 1.67 倍速播；关掉逐帧就按 fps 重采样成真实时长（ep20 从 553 帧变成 921 帧）。问题是真实时长训练能不能减小转动落后量。计划 agent-doc/plan-playback-2026-09-28.md 第 2 版，9/28 08:41 登记。

设计

  • 满配一对（ml40，1000 万 buffer）与 100 万一对（mf4090，只作描述），两种回放各训一条 ep20。
  • 主指标：关注段末的转动落后量 L，取最后三个 checkpoint 的平均；两臂之差超过事先登记的效应阈值 E 才算有差别。

结果：预算内没有差别

满配一对1.67 倍速真实时长
关注段末落后量 L（三个 checkpoint）64.3 / 70.5 / 69.5，平均 68.1°75.9 / 69.5 / 72.8，平均 72.7°
关注段起点（负 = 提前转）−25.4°−21.4°
新增落后量：起转 / 停顿 / 快转+77.1 / +5.6 / +12.4+77.6 / −27.6 / +44.6
操作段 palm 误差中位4.7 cm4.5 cm
  • Δ = −4.6°。登记的 E = 139.3°，被 100 万真实时长一臂的 checkpoint 波动（69.7°）撑大了，这是登记规则的缺陷；只用满配两臂算，E = 15°，判定不变。
  • 起转段新增的落后量在两种回放下几乎相同（77.1° 对 77.6°），与时间无关，按登记转 T4 与 T1。
  • 100 万真实时长一臂三个 checkpoint 都没有成功的世界，这个组合训不稳。

次要结果：真实时长那条过了 Day 1 判据

ml40 评测，4096 世界1.67 倍速训练，逐帧评真实时长训练，真实时长评
不动基线0.4710.470
ADD-AUC0.4310.547
chord_sr0.790.809
MP-SR（maniptrans_sr）0.00050.887
SP-SR（spider_sr_uncentered）0.00020.844
MPPE5.2 cm3.9 cm
Day 1 判据不通过通过
  • 两条的转动落后量相同，所以 ADD-AUC 的差别不是来自跟上了转动。分段看，真实时长在操作段（+0.066 对 +0.032）和操作后（+0.053 对 −0.044）更好，操作前反而更差（−0.045 对 −0.027）；来源还没查。
  • 它按自己的速度评测。当时官方评测按什么速度回放未知，这个数不能当榜上分数的预测，但让回放速度这个问题的分量大了很多，Day 2 由此而来。9/28 晚 kit 发布后，计分按源帧逐帧对齐，训练改为固定逐帧（见官方评测与提交）。
开跑前挡下的两次失败
  • 真实时长的第一次冒烟 13 秒就失败：G1 绑定参考时逐帧要求 palm 与关节 FK 相差 ≤ 1e-4 m / 1e-4 rad，不区分插值帧。实测重采样后相差 0.61 mm / 2.0e-3 rad，只把这两个校验容差放到 2 mm / 5e-3 rad。
  • 诊断试跑的同卡闸没过（包装前后 ADD-AUC 差 0.0064 > 0.005）：同一 policy 两次不包装的 256 世界评测也差 0.0057，是评测本身不逐位可复现；容差改为 0.005 × √(4096 / 世界数)，4096 世界时不变。

记录 agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-28-playback.md；判定 results/playback/compare.txt、compare_10m_only.txt；过线判定 results/playback/verdict_rt_runs.txt。

Playback speed comparison

Day 1b fired T2 (the rotation lags). FlashCHORD's G1 recipe plays the reference frame by frame by default: every 50 Hz control step advances one frame of the 30 fps reference, so the motion plays at 1.67×. Turning that off resamples by fps to real time (ep20 goes from 553 to 921 frames). The question is whether training at real time reduces the rotation lag. Plan: version 2 of agent-doc/plan-playback-2026-09-28.md, registered at 08:41 on 9/28.

Design

  • A full-recipe pair (ml40, 10M buffer) and a 1M pair (mf4090, descriptive only): one ep20 run per playback.
  • Main metric: the rotation lag L at the end of the window of interest, averaged over the last three checkpoints; the arms differ only if the gap exceeds a registered effect threshold E.

Result: no difference within the budget

Full-recipe pair1.67×Real time
Lag L at window end (three checkpoints)64.3 / 70.5 / 69.5, mean 68.1°75.9 / 69.5 / 72.8, mean 72.7°
Lag at window start (negative = turns early)−25.4°−21.4°
Added lag: start-up / pause / fast turn+77.1 / +5.6 / +12.4+77.6 / −27.6 / +44.6
Median palm error, manipulation segment4.7 cm4.5 cm
  • Δ = −4.6°. The registered E = 139.3° was inflated by the checkpoint spread of the 1M real-time arm (69.7°), a flaw in the registered rule; computed from the two full-recipe arms only, E = 15° and the verdict is the same.
  • The lag added during start-up is almost the same at both speeds (77.1° against 77.6°), so it does not depend on time; as registered, the next steps are T4 and T1.
  • The 1M real-time arm has no successful world at any of its three checkpoints; that combination does not train stably.

Secondary result: the real-time run passes the Day 1 criteria

Evaluated on ml40, 4096 worldsTrained at 1.67×, evaluated frame by frameTrained and evaluated at real time
Do-nothing baseline0.4710.470
ADD-AUC0.4310.547
chord_sr0.790.809
MP-SR (maniptrans_sr)0.00050.887
SP-SR (spider_sr_uncentered)0.00020.844
MPPE5.2 cm3.9 cm
Day 1 criteriaFailPass
  • Both runs lag the rotation by the same amount, so the ADD-AUC gap does not come from following the turn better. By segment, real time does better in the manipulation window (+0.066 against +0.032) and after it (+0.053 against −0.044), and worse before it (−0.045 against −0.027); where the gap comes from is not checked yet.
  • It is evaluated at its own speed. At the time the official evaluation playback was unknown, so this number is no forecast of a leaderboard score. It did make the playback question much weightier, which is why Day 2 exists. Since the kit came out on the evening of 9/28, scoring aligns frame by frame to the source frames and training uses frame-by-frame playback only (see Official evaluation and submission).
Two failures caught before training
  • The first real-time smoke failed after 13 seconds: when binding the reference, G1 requires every palm frame to match the joint FK within 1e-4 m / 1e-4 rad, interpolated frames included. Measured after resampling: 0.61 mm / 2.0e-3 rad. Only these two check tolerances were raised, to 2 mm / 5e-3 rad.
  • The same-card gate of the diagnosis dry run failed (ADD-AUC differs by 0.0064 > 0.005 with and without the wrapper). Two unwrapped 256-world evaluations of the same policy also differ by 0.0057, so the evaluator itself is not bit-reproducible; the tolerance became 0.005 × √(4096 / worlds), unchanged at 4096 worlds.

Record agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-28-playback.md; verdicts results/playback/compare.txt and compare_10m_only.txt; pass check results/playback/verdict_rt_runs.txt.

官方评测与提交

9/28 19:46（kit 压缩包在官网上的修改时间）官方发布 submission kit，定下了评测口径：每条 episode 用一个 checkpoint、以确定性动作跑 1 个世界，导出的轨迹按 30 fps 源帧逐帧计分，每次提交要有全部 30 条 episode。据此训练固定用逐帧回放（9/28 23:05 选定）。10/1 13:43 组委会重新发布 kit，评测运行时从 aee95f2 改钉在 8c778f9，这个版本含方盒支撑面的修复 #185；原定要问组委会的前三问因此由 rxzhou 自行定下（下一节）。截至 10/3 22:15，两档各 30 条都已导出并打成 CSV，上传器预检两档都过，没有提交；ep7 两档用带非有限防护的重训补上（见ep7 防护与重训）。10/3 晚的提交前全面检查两档都过，官方 10/2 的新 kit 不影响这两份 CSV（下一节）。还差的只有待定事项（见下文“还缺什么”）。10/13 17:53–17:56 Tier 1 第一次提交（t1x1013），四项公榜分与打包时本地复算相差都小于 1e-5，Tier 1 的本地分数就是榜上分数；10/14 00:05 Tier 2 第一次提交（b1013），四项公榜分 AUC 0.51379、SP-SR 0.73333、MP-SR 0.53333、MPPE 16.54028 cm（results/submission/submit_b1013.txt）。

10/1 的新 kit：评测运行时钉在 8c778f9

组委会 10/1 13:40 合并上游 #188，13:43 重新发布 kit（sha256 0f2f9f4b…）。README 把 FlashCHORD 运行时钉在 8c778f905c，#188 的说明写明这个版本含 Cube 支撑面的缩放修复，也就是 #185（results/submission/runtime_pin_facts.txt）。

  • v0.4.0 到 8c778f9 共 6 个提交（#180–#185）。FlashCHORD 下改了 11 个文件：评测代码（轨迹导出、来历摘要、两个新指标）、scene/support.py 与测试；依赖与镜像文件没有改动。训练路径上只有 training/flash_sac/evaluation.py 改了一行 import 写法，另加一个默认为 None 的字段（results/submission/eval_runtime_facts.txt）。
  • kit 自带的 flash_chord/scripts/evaluate_policy.py 与三份 Hydra 配置，和 8c778f9 的同名文件逐字节相同。kit 不带运行时本身，要自己装一个能导出排行榜轨迹的 FlashCHORD。
  • 新打包器换算 Track 2 位姿的做法：用第 0 帧求一个刚体变换，把评测器记下的参考对到这一档发布的输入上，逐帧误差都在 1 mm、1e-3 rad 以内才换算，否则整次打包报错（tools/pack_submission.py:78–79, 99, 118）。输入不是发布的那一份时，要用 --frame-references 给出，npz 里写 coordinate_frame=released_track2_world、frame_index [T]、pose_xyzw [T,1,7]。打包器对三档都按干净参考算本地分数，打印成 locally computed 一行。
  • 打包器缺一条计分 episode 就整份拒绝，拒收 environment_valid=false 的世界（读代码，pack_submission.py:274, 314, 399；未核实），并要求所有轨迹的评测脚本与评测代码摘要相同。样例提交 22,989 行、30 条 episode、只有世界 0。

据此定下的事（决定记录 agent-doc/decision-organizer-q1-q3-2026-10-01.md）：

  • 三档的评测与导出都用 8c778f9，“运行时”一问不再问。
  • 评测器把 ReconBody 的终止阈值都改成 1e6，普通的任务失败不会让世界被判终止，只有数值发散会；已落盘的 96 份评测（393,216 个世界）没有一份报这个错。两条提交规矩（10/1 采纳）：含 NaN 的 policy 不拿去导出，训练链 10/1 起已会标 NONFINITE 并跳过评测；提交时某条 episode 报 “must disable task failure termination”，就退到同一条 run 里更早的一份有限 checkpoint，这是唯一允许不用最终 checkpoint 的情形。
  • 只有地面的 16 条继续用 scene.support=false：8c778f9 遇到空的支撑面文件同样报错，关掉支撑面是它自己提供的路径；评测按 checkpoint 里的配置重建场景。
  • ep1、ep7、ep28 两档各重训一条，用 #185 的几何。

v0.4.0 训出的 checkpoint 能否在 8c778f9 里加载：mf4090 的兼容性验证 10/2 06:33–06:43 实测通过，7 行全部符合（agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-02-box6-eval-runtime.md）。用 8c778f9 把 3 份已有 policy（t1-ep8 只有地面、t1-ep6 圆柱支撑面、t2-ep2 是 Tier 2）各导出 1 个世界，t1-ep8 再导出 2 个世界，加载、结构、来历都过，eval_code_sha256、eval_script_sha256 等于 8c778f9 的值。t1-ep6 在两个评测器下 4096 世界的 ADD-AUC 是 0.6335 与 0.6321，差 0.0014，chord_sr 的 |z| 是 1.18，都在登记的门限内。守卫拦下了 ep1 含 NaN 的 policy（退出码 12）；关掉守卫时评测器出错，没有产出导出。单个世界与 4096 世界的差别：t1-ep6 只导 1 个世界时 MP-SR 是 0，同一 checkpoint 4096 世界的 MP-SR 是 0.57。ml40 的兼容性验证 10/2 10:09–10:17 通过，3 行全部符合：t1-ep12、t2-ep0 各导出 1 个世界，加载、结构、来历都过；t1-ep12 在两个评测器下 4096 世界的 ADD-AUC 是 0.6972 与 0.6968，差 0.0004，chord_sr 的 |z| 是 0.47（results/ml40/submission/compat/ml40_*.txt）。两台都过，所以第 1 批的 v0.4.0 checkpoint 继续当候选，提交一律用 8c778f9 副本导出，已有的 4096 世界评测与判定可以沿用。打包在 Mac 上用 uv 跑：服务器镜像里没有 pandas，kit 也不在服务器上。打包预演两轮都过（Tier 1 三条、Tier 2 两条，换算残差在 1e-7 量级；agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-02-submission-exports.md）。只报：同一条轨迹，打包器重算的 ADD-AUC 与评测器不同，ep6 −0.0027、ep12 +0.0218，推测是两边取网格顶点的方式不同（没核实），所以本地 4096 世界的 ADD-AUC 与排行榜口径每条可能差到约 0.02。

Tier 2 打包要加 --frame-references（已生成）

Tier 2 的参考是 v5.4 降噪后的轨迹，与发布的带噪输入差几厘米，不加 --frame-references，打包必然报 “do not differ by one rigid transform”。帧参考按 kit 的 npz 格式由 runs/recovery/tier2_v54 生成：v5.4 存的是 OpenCV 世界系、wxyz 顺序，与发布数据同一约定，换成 xyzw 即可。打包器对 Tier 2 也读干净参考、打印本地分数；rxzhou 的解读是 Tier 2、Tier 3 的本地分数不拿来做任何选择，打包时滤掉那一行（属解读，不是官方原文，待确认）。10/2 已按这个格式生成 30 条（stages/evaluate/make_frame_references.py，只读 Tier 2 的文件），都过了粗核：与发布的带噪输入相比，第 0 帧位置差不超过 20 mm，逐帧角度差的中位数小于 45°（results/submission/frame_refs_t2_mf4090.txt）。

kit 里写定的事

事项内容
评测怎么跑kit 的 evaluate_policy.py 按 checkpoint 自己的配置重建环境与任务（use_checkpoint_config: true），确定性动作
几个世界示例批量评测默认 1 个世界；三个 Tier 都是每条 episode 1 个世界、全部 episode 计分、私有比例 0（没有 holdout）
帧号导出轨迹的 reference_frame = 步数 + 起始帧，time_s = 步数 / 控制频率；kit 按 reference_frame 取帧号
计分格子打包器要求每条计分 episode 的每个参考帧都有一行；官方样例 CSV 只有 1 个世界，ep20 是连续的 553 帧（第 0–552 帧）
运行时要用上游 main 的 FlashCHORD（aee95f2），公开的 v0.4.0 没有轨迹导出
运行时（10/1 新 kit）改钉在 8c778f905c，含 Cube 支撑面的缩放修复（#185）；kit 自带的评测入口与配置与它逐字节相同
成功率口径榜上的 SP-SR、MP-SR 是 episode 的比例，按每条 episode 的平均位置、朝向误差判；各指标在 episode 间平均
完整性每次提交要包含全部计分 episode，每条一个 checkpoint
哈希清单config/tracks.json 里“接受的评测脚本 / 评测代码哈希”目前为空
打包器对照公开参考核对计分 episode、帧与物体，在本地重算 Track 2 指标并与评测器的指标 JSON 比较，把评测器与 checkpoint 的哈希带进 CSV
上传每档有四个 Kaggle 指标比赛，四个用同一个 CSV；上传脚本先校验 CSV 再传给四个
打包器（9/30 新 kit）新 kit 改了三个文件；Track 2 README 写明评测器用的世界系与发布参考不同时，打包器会把位姿换算到发布的世界系

前十行的证据是 results/submission/kit_facts.txt（stages/evaluate/submission_kit_facts.sh 对 9/28 旧 kit 8a169616… 的摘录，逐行带文件与行号）。最后一行出自 results/submission/kit_update_diff.txt。9/30 读新 kit 源码，前十行说的做法都还在，打包器与 Track 2 README 的行号变了；逐行摘录还没按新 kit 重做。kit 本体放在 third_party/v2d_submission_kit/（新版在 update_a2de2aee/），不进 git。

为什么训练固定用逐帧（推断，未核实）

帧号就是步数、计分按源帧对齐，所以只有逐帧回放（一步走一个源帧）能和计分格子一一对上；真实时长训练的 policy 一次评测跑 921 步（ep20），前 553 步会被拿去对完整的 553 帧参考，时间轴错位。这一条是读代码推出的，还没在新评测器上实测。登记的 Day 2 分支两条都不完全吻合，定为按“固定逐帧”处理（见 Day 2）。

9/30 的 kit 更新

9/30 重新下载的 kit sha256 是 a2de2aee…（服务器 Last-Modified 9/29 00:52 UTC-6，即 06:52 GMT），手上旧的是 8a169616…。逐文件比较只有三个文件不同：KIT_MANIFEST.json、tools/pack_submission.py、tracks/track_2/README.md（results/submission/kit_update_diff.txt）。顶层 README.md 没变，评测运行时仍指定 aee95f2。

Track 2 README 第 2 步新加的两句（原文）

“If the evaluator uses a different world frame, the packer converts its poses to the released frame after verifying one rigid transform across the episode. For these single-object episodes, the evaluator may label the object object.”

打包器源码里对应的改动（读新 kit 的 tools/pack_submission.py，行号按新版）：

  • 用第 1 帧的评测器参考位姿和发布参考位姿解出一个旋转加平移，套到整条 episode 的评测器参考上；逐帧残差都在 1 mm、1e-3 rad 以内（78–79 行），才把 rollout 的位姿换到发布的世界系（82–130 行）。超出就把这条 episode 记成问题，整次打包报错退出（350–352 行）。旧版在这种情况下只打印 WARNING，提示分数会和本地算的不一样、去问组委会（旧版 313–322 行）。
  • Track 2 的轨迹是 flash_chord_object_trajectories_v1 格式、却没有记录评测器参考位姿的，新版直接拒收（314–319 行）。
  • 单物体 episode 的物体名全是 object 时，不再因为和参考里的物体名对不上而报错（303–307 行）。
  • KIT_MANIFEST.json 里只有打包器和 Track 2 README 两个文件的 sha256 跟着变了。

对本项目的影响（9/30 当时）：评测口径（1 个世界、逐帧、30 条全交、运行时 aee95f2）不变。aee95f2 评测器导出的轨迹是否用了不同的世界系、会不会触发换算，还没实测；出第一批轨迹后先核对里面有没有评测器参考位姿这一列。10/1 的新 kit 把运行时改成 8c778f9，换算的做法见上文“10/1 的新 kit”。接提交流程前按新 kit 重做 kit 事实摘录（旧摘录 results/submission/kit_facts.txt）。

评测运行时 aee95f2 的方盒支撑面问题

10/1 起评测运行时改为 8c778f9（见上文“10/1 的新 kit”），本节说的问题在评测侧已经不存在。保留本节，作为方盒三条重训的依据。

kit 指定的运行时 aee95f2（kit README.md:34）里，scene/support.py 与 v0.4.0 逐字节相同（md5 95e5e33f…），对 USD Cube 只读 size、不读 xformOp:scale（support.py:85–86, 114–116；results/diverge/support_py_versions.txt）。30 条里只有 ep1（vacuum）、ep7（iron）、ep28（squeegee）用方盒支撑面（results/diverge/support_shapes.txt），在仿真里都被建成边长 1 m 的实心立方体，半尺寸 (0.5, 0.5, 0.5)（results/ml40/diverge/support_import_v040.txt）。按这个几何，参考物体从第 0 帧起就埋在方块里：ep7 的 896 帧全部嵌入超过 5 mm，ep1 是 747 帧里的 519 帧，ep28 对两块方盒分别是 988 帧里的 608、651 帧（results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt；未核实）。

上游 8c778f905c（#185，9/29 12:11 UTC-6）修了这个问题，比 aee95f2 晚 4 个提交（agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md 更正一节）。本仓库打了 #185 补丁的源码副本把 ep7 的桌面建成半尺寸 (0.42, 0.141, 0.005)（results/ml40/diverge/support_import_p185.txt）。这份副本基于 v0.4.0，没有轨迹导出，只能用来训练和做对照。

ml40（L40S）上的对照 9/30 14:35 全部跑完，细节与判据见发散排查。ep7 冒烟 400 万步：v0.4.0 导入的 13 条（R0；fp16 种子 43–50 共 8 条；fp32 种子 43–46 共 4 条）没有一条学起来，8 条出 NaN，另外 5 条停在回合长度约 51、回报约 −3.7；#185 导入的 8 条（fp16 种子 43–50）都没发散，回合长度 243–309、回报 25.6–34.8。ep1 截到 6000 万步（单种子）：v0.4.0 fp16 从 30,001,152 步那份起 NaN，v0.4.0 fp32 从第一份（10,000,384 步）起就 NaN，#185 的 6 份全部有限。

按登记判据 A.3 的最终判定（9/30 14:35）：“v0.4.0 把方盒建成 1 m 立方体是发散的触发”（H1′）得到支持，v0.4.0 fp16 发散 5 / 8 对 #185 fp16 0 / 8（配对种子 43–50），单侧 Fisher p = 0.0128 < 0.05；“fp16 存储是必要条件”（H4）被拒绝。结论的范围：H1′ 是在 ep7 冒烟（400 万步、L40S）上检验的；ep1 只有上面的单种子旁证，方向相同；ep28 登记为只描述。fp16 存储与 BN 统计量在其中各占多少，这组对照说不了（results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt、results/diverge/train_summary_table_ml40_diag_final.txt、results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_L{0,1,3}_ml40.txt）。

评测器遇到坏世界的处理在 aee95f2 与 v0.4.0 里相同：非有限世界只把位姿清零，只要有世界被判终止就报错（results/batch1/termination_facts.txt）。第 1 批里 ep7 冒烟的 256 世界 rollout 有 172 个世界非有限、随后报错（results/ml40/batch1/ep7_smoke_diag_excerpt.txt）；ep1 训练发散，3 份 checkpoint 的评测都报错（results/batch1/ep1_eval_failure_excerpt.txt）。官方只评 1 个世界，这个世界被判终止，这条就交不出轨迹。第 1 批里非有限世界都和这个报错一起出现，所以 rxzhou 推测世界发散也会走到这一步，还没在 aee95f2 上实测。

rxzhou 的理解：排行榜不跑仿真，轨迹由参赛方自己用指定运行时导出，Kaggle 只对 CSV 计分（kit README.md:29–41）；带进 CSV 的 eval_code_sha256 只覆盖 evaluation/ 下的 __init__.py、harness.py、metrics.py、rollout.py（aee95f2 harness.py:123–135），#185 改的 scene/support.py 不在里面。所以换运行时要组委会书面同意，参赛方不能自己决定（计划 agent-doc/plan-ep1-ep7-divergence-2026-09-30.md 附录 A.6）。10/1 的新 kit 已把运行时改成 8c778f9，这一问不用再问。

原定要问组委会的三问（计划附录 A.6；10/1 的新 kit 发布后不再需要问，留作记录）
  • 能否用 aee95f2 + #185（或 ≥ 8c778f9 的运行时）训练并导出轨迹。
  • kit 指定的运行时版本会不会更新。
  • 如果不能换，这三条 episode（在 aee95f2 下物体埋在 1 m 实心立方体里）该怎么提交。

如果只能用 aee95f2，这三条评测时物体就埋在 1 m 实心立方体里；在这种几何上 ep7 的 13 条冒烟没有一条学起来，所以 rxzhou 推测按 aee95f2 这三条交不出有意义的轨迹（推断，没在 aee95f2 上实测；执行记录 agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md 最终判定一节）。

如果允许换，rxzhou 的理解是 30 条都要在同一个运行时上重新导出：打包器注释写明 “one submission is one evaluator build”（新 kit tools/pack_submission.py:365–366，旧版 288–289 行）。#185 只改了 support.py 和它的测试（9/30 查的上游提交文件列表，没有落盘），被哈希的文件不变，所以 rxzhou 理解打包器查不出混用，这一条要靠本项目自己守。ep1、ep7、ep28 怎么训等回复再定（执行记录更正一节）。

10/3 晚的提交前全面检查

计划 agent-doc/plan-submission-check-2026-10-03.md 第 2 版；执行记录 agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-03-submission-check.md。全程只读：没有提交，没有改动要交的两份 CSV，上传器只跑 --dry-run。

编号检查结果
C1 来历每条 episode 的 checkpoint 哈希在 CSV、导出文件、服务器上的 policy 与 4096 世界评测之间一致；run 与步数等于导出清单；评测代码、打包器、上传器的哈希与 kit 文件相同；两档的 checkpoint 互不相交两档都过
C2 结构row_id 与官方样例逐行相同（22,989 行）；比发布数据少的一行是 ep11 第 900 帧，正是物体不可见的帧两档都过
C3 数值全部有限，四元数模长与 1 相差不超过 1e-5两档都过
C4 复算由导出轨迹照打包器的构造复算 CSV，位置最大差约 8.5e-7 m、朝向 1.9e-6 rad（六位小数的舍入量）两档都过
C5 重打包按原参数重打两档，sha256 与原 CSV 逐字节相同两档都过
C6 kit官方 10/2 00:12 又发了一版 kit（b1f8f703），rxzhou 打包用的是 10/1 的 0f2f9f4b。新版只改了说明文档、Track 1 的计分代码与 v2dlb/mhr_submission.py；Track 2 用到的打包器、上传器、评测脚本、样例 CSV 与 config/tracks.json 逐字节相同，两份 CSV 照样成立不过（不影响 CSV）

新 kit 里与本项目有关的只有一处：私有仓库要把 GitHub 账号 Nvidia-V2D-Challenge 邀请为协作者，确保它能看到提交对应的 commit。本仓库现在私有，不做这一步，CSV 里的代码链接组委会打不开；Kaggle 端不会拦，是事后核验时的作废风险。只报：交上去的是 1 个世界的轨迹，Tier 1 按 1 个世界 MP-SR 成功 8 / 30，4096 世界 MP-SR 的 30 条平均 0.265；Tier 1 按 kit 口径的本地分数 AUC 0.3780、SP-SR 0.5333、MP-SR 0.2667、MPPE 25.17 cm，排行榜以 Kaggle 为准。

提交流程

  1. 每条 episode 选一个 checkpoint，用 kit 的 examples/run_policy_evaluation.py（在 8c778f9 运行时里，即 10/1 的新 kit 钉的版本；含 NaN 的 policy 不导出）评 1 个世界，得到 episode_XXXXXX.parquet。
  2. tools/pack_submission.py --track track_2_tier1 --dataset-root … --code-commit <GitHub commit URL>：核对帧与物体、必要时把位姿换算到发布的世界系、本地重算四项指标、带上各哈希，生成 CSV。用 10/1 的新 kit（0f2f9f4b…）；Tier 2 加 --frame-references，因为本项目的参考是 v5.4，不是发布的带噪输入。
  3. submit/v2d_submit.py 校验 CSV 后传到 Kaggle 上这一档的四个指标比赛（同一个 CSV）；上传前要在 Kaggle 上接受这四个比赛的规则。每周 5 次，最后三天不限。

ep20 两条满配各按另一种回放补评一次：逐帧训练的改按真实时长评，chord_sr 0.79 → 0.03、MPPE 5.2 → 12.6 cm；真实时长训练的改按逐帧评，MP-SR 0.887 → 0.012、MPPE 3.9 → 18.6 cm。

撤回与改写的说法
  • “官方评测很可能用 1.67 倍速”：9/28 下午撤回（当时官方没写）；kit 发布后，计分按源帧逐帧对齐，训练相应固定用逐帧，但这是计分格子的结果，不是官方对“回放速度”的原话。
  • “评测若走 Isaac Lab，连仿真器都不同”：FlashCHORD 就是官方提供并推荐的路径，kit 的评测也是 FlashCHORD，撤回。
  • “两种训练换一种回放都崩了”：逐帧训练那条的 MP-SR 在匹配回放下本来就只有 0.0005，改为上面按指标逐项的说法。
  • 9/29 版“还缺什么”把 ep7 的评测器报错单列一项，写成训好的 policy 在 1 个世界里也可能发散：9/30 查到 ep1、ep7、ep28 的方盒支撑面在 v0.4.0 与 aee95f2 里都被建成 1 m 立方体，这一项并进上面的“方盒支撑面问题”。9/30 14:35 按登记判据判定 1 m 立方体是 ep7 冒烟发散的触发（H1′ 支持，单侧 Fisher p = 0.0128，results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt），ep1 只有单种子旁证。

给组委会的邮件草稿第 2 版 agent-doc/organizer-email-draft-2026-10-01.md（10/1 16:30）删去了运行时与终止报错两问，空支撑面降成一句告知，剩 5 问：私有仓库的 commit URL 能否接受、代码能否在封榜后发布；Tier 3 的输入会不会发布；能否重复评测；Kaggle 队伍怎么登记；几条数据规则。10/1 定下先不急。9/28 的邮件与 Discord 草稿（agent-doc/organizer-{email,discord}-draft-2026-09-28.md）已被它取代。参赛说明会 10/7 12:00（UTC-6；官方写的是太平洋时间 11:00）。

Official evaluation and submission

The organizers released the submission kit at 19:46 on 9/28 (the modification time of the kit archive on the official site). It fixes the evaluation: each episode is scored from one checkpoint, one world and deterministic actions, the exported trajectory is scored frame by frame against the 30 fps source frames, and every submission must cover all 30 episodes. Training therefore uses frame-by-frame playback only (chosen at 23:05 on 9/28). At 13:43 on 10/1 the organizers republished the kit and moved the evaluation runtime from aee95f2 to 8c778f9, which includes the box-support fix #185, so the first three questions meant for them were settled without asking (next section). As of 22:15 on 10/3, both tiers are exported, 30 episodes each, and packed into CSVs, and the uploader preflight passes for both; nothing has been submitted. The ep7 of each tier comes from a retrain with the non-finite guard (see ep7 guard and retrain). The pre-submission check on the evening of 10/3 passes for both tiers, and the organizers' kit of 10/2 does not affect the two CSVs (next section). What remains are the pending decisions (see "What is still missing" below). Tier 1 was first submitted at 17:53–17:56 on 10/13 (t1x1013), with all four public scores within 1e-5 of the local recomputation at packing time, so the Tier 1 local score is the board score; Tier 2 was first submitted at 00:05 on 10/14 (b1013), with public scores AUC 0.51379, SP-SR 0.73333, MP-SR 0.53333 and MPPE 16.54028 cm (results/submission/submit_b1013.txt).

The 10/1 kit: evaluation runtime pinned to 8c778f9

The organizers merged upstream #188 at 13:40 on 10/1 and republished the kit at 13:43 (sha256 0f2f9f4b…). The README pins the FlashCHORD runtime to 8c778f905c, and the note on #188 says this version includes the Cube support scaling fix, that is #185 (results/submission/runtime_pin_facts.txt).

  • v0.4.0 to 8c778f9 is 6 commits (#180–#185). Under FlashCHORD, 11 files changed: evaluation code (trajectory export, provenance digests, two new metrics), scene/support.py and tests; no dependency or image file changed. On the training path only training/flash_sac/evaluation.py changed, by one import line and one field that defaults to None (results/submission/eval_runtime_facts.txt).
  • The kit's own flash_chord/scripts/evaluate_policy.py and its three Hydra configs are byte-identical to the same files in 8c778f9. The kit does not ship the runtime itself; participants install a FlashCHORD that can export leaderboard trajectories.
  • How the new packer converts Track 2 poses: it solves one rigid transform from frame 0 that maps the evaluator's recorded reference onto the tier's released input, and converts only if every frame agrees within 1 mm and 1e-3 rad; otherwise the whole packing run fails (tools/pack_submission.py:78–79, 99, 118). When the input is not the released one, it has to be given with --frame-references, as npz files holding coordinate_frame=released_track2_world, frame_index [T] and pose_xyzw [T,1,7]. For all three tiers the packer computes local scores against the clean reference and prints them as a locally computed line.
  • The packer rejects the whole submission if one scored episode is missing and rejects worlds flagged environment_valid=false (from the code, pack_submission.py:274, 314, 399; unverified), and it requires one evaluation-script digest and one evaluation-code digest across all trajectories. The sample submission has 22,989 rows, 30 episodes and world 0 only.

What was decided from this (decision record agent-doc/decision-organizer-q1-q3-2026-10-01.md):

  • All three tiers are evaluated and exported with 8c778f9; the runtime question is dropped.
  • The evaluator sets every ReconBody termination threshold to 1e6, so an ordinary task failure does not mark a world terminated; only a numerical blow-up does. None of the 96 recorded evaluations (393,216 worlds) raised that error. Two submission rules (adopted on 10/1): a policy with NaN is never exported, and since 10/1 the training chain marks such runs NONFINITE and skips their evaluation; if an episode fails with "must disable task failure termination" at submission time, fall back to an earlier finite checkpoint of the same run, the only case in which an episode may use a checkpoint other than the final one.
  • The 16 floor-only sequences keep scene.support=false: 8c778f9 also fails on an empty support file, and turning supports off is the path it provides; evaluation rebuilds the scene from the checkpoint's config.
  • ep1, ep7 and ep28 are retrained once per tier on the #185 geometry.

Can checkpoints trained with v0.4.0 load in 8c778f9? The compatibility check on mf4090 passed this test from 06:33 to 06:43 on 10/2, with all 7 rows as expected (agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-02-box6-eval-runtime.md). 8c778f9 exported 3 existing policies (t1-ep8 floor only, t1-ep6 with a cylinder support, t2-ep2 from Tier 2) on one world each, and t1-ep8 once more on 2 worlds; loading, structure and provenance all pass, with eval_code_sha256 and eval_script_sha256 equal to the 8c778f9 values. Under the two evaluators, t1-ep6 scores an ADD-AUC of 0.6335 and 0.6321 on 4096 worlds, 0.0014 apart, with a chord_sr |z| of 1.18, both within the registered limits. The guard stops ep1's NaN policy (exit 12); with the guard off, the evaluator fails and produces no export. One world against 4096: exported on one world, t1-ep6 has MP-SR 0, while the same checkpoint reaches MP-SR 0.57 on 4096 worlds. ml40's compatibility check passed from 10:09 to 10:17 on 10/2 with all 3 rows as expected: t1-ep12 and t2-ep0 were each exported on one world and pass loading, structure and provenance; under the two evaluators t1-ep12 scores an ADD-AUC of 0.6972 and 0.6968 on 4096 worlds, 0.0004 apart, with a chord_sr |z| of 0.47 (results/ml40/submission/compat/ml40_*.txt). With both machines passing, the v0.4.0 checkpoints of batch 1 stay candidates, every submission export uses the 8c778f9 copy, and the existing 4096-world evaluations and verdicts carry over. Packing runs on the Mac with uv, since the server image lacks pandas and the kit is not on the servers. Both packing rehearsals passed (3 Tier 1 and 2 Tier 2 episodes, conversion residuals around 1e-7; agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-02-submission-exports.md). Reported only: on the same trajectory the packer's recomputed ADD-AUC differs from the evaluator's, by −0.0027 for ep6 and +0.0218 for ep12, presumably because the two take mesh vertices differently (not verified), so a local 4096-world ADD-AUC may differ from the leaderboard's by up to about 0.02 per episode.

Tier 2 packing needs --frame-references (generated)

The Tier 2 references are the v5.4 denoised trajectories, several centimetres away from the released noisy input, so packing without --frame-references is bound to fail with "do not differ by one rigid transform". The frame references are generated in the kit's npz format from runs/recovery/tier2_v54: v5.4 is stored in the OpenCV world frame with wxyz quaternions, the same convention as the released data, so only the quaternion order changes to xyzw. The packer also reads the clean reference for Tier 2 and prints local scores; rxzhou's reading is that Tier 2 and Tier 3 local scores are never used to choose anything, and packing drops that line (an interpretation, not official wording, awaiting confirmation). On 10/2 the 30 files were generated in this format (stages/evaluate/make_frame_references.py, which reads only Tier 2 files), and all pass the sanity checks: against the released noisy input, the frame-0 position differs by at most 20 mm and the median per-frame angle difference is under 45° (results/submission/frame_refs_t2_mf4090.txt).

What the kit fixes

ItemContent
How evaluation runsThe kit's evaluate_policy.py rebuilds the environment and task from the checkpoint's own config (use_checkpoint_config: true) with deterministic actions
WorldsThe example batch runner uses one world by default; all three tiers use one world per episode, score every episode and have a private fraction of 0 (no holdout)
Frame numbersIn the exported trajectory reference_frame = step + start frame and time_s = step / control rate; the kit takes the frame number from reference_frame
Scoring gridThe packer needs a row for every reference frame of every scored episode; the official sample CSV has one world and 553 contiguous frames (0–552) for ep20
RuntimeThe FlashCHORD runtime from upstream main (aee95f2) is required; the public v0.4.0 has no trajectory export
Runtime (10/1 kit)Now pinned to 8c778f905c, which includes the Cube support scaling fix (#185); the kit's evaluation entry point and configs are byte-identical to it
Success ratesLeaderboard SP-SR and MP-SR are fractions of episodes, judged by each episode's mean position and orientation errors; every metric is averaged across episodes
CompletenessEvery submission must include all scored episodes, one checkpoint each
Hash listsThe lists of accepted evaluator script and code hashes in config/tracks.json are empty for now
PackerChecks scored episodes, frames and objects against the public reference, recomputes the Track 2 metrics locally and compares them with the evaluator's metrics JSON, and carries evaluator and checkpoint hashes into the CSV
UploadEach tier has four Kaggle metric competitions that take the same CSV; the uploader validates the CSV, then sends it to all four
Packer (9/30 kit)The new kit changes three files; the Track 2 README now says that when the evaluator uses a different world frame, the packer converts its poses into the released frame

Evidence for the first ten rows: results/submission/kit_facts.txt (excerpts of the old 9/28 kit 8a169616… by stages/evaluate/submission_kit_facts.sh, each line tagged with file and line). The last row comes from results/submission/kit_update_diff.txt. Reading the new kit's source on 9/30, everything the first ten rows describe is still there, but line numbers in the packer and the Track 2 README have moved; the line-by-line excerpt has not been redone for the new kit. The kit lives in third_party/v2d_submission_kit/ (the new one under update_a2de2aee/), outside git.

Why training uses frame-by-frame playback (inference, unverified)

Frame number equals step and scoring is aligned to source frames, so only frame-by-frame playback (one source frame per step) lines up with the scoring grid one to one; a real-time-trained policy runs 921 steps on ep20, and its first 553 steps would be scored against the full 553-frame reference, out of step. This was inferred from the code; it has not been tested on the new evaluator. Neither registered Day 2 branch fitted exactly, and it was decided to treat it as "fixed frame-by-frame" (see Day 2).

The 9/30 kit update

The kit downloaded again on 9/30 has sha256 a2de2aee… (server Last-Modified 00:52 UTC-6 on 9/29, 06:52 GMT); the copy in use was 8a169616…. A file-by-file comparison finds three changed files: KIT_MANIFEST.json, tools/pack_submission.py and tracks/track_2/README.md (results/submission/kit_update_diff.txt). The top-level README.md is unchanged and still names aee95f2 as the evaluation runtime.

The two sentences added to step 2 of the Track 2 README (verbatim)

"If the evaluator uses a different world frame, the packer converts its poses to the released frame after verifying one rigid transform across the episode. For these single-object episodes, the evaluator may label the object object."

What changed in the packer source (read from the new kit's tools/pack_submission.py; line numbers refer to the new file):

  • It solves one rotation and offset from the first frame of the evaluator's reference pose and the released reference pose, applies it to the evaluator reference over the whole episode, and converts the rollout poses into the released frame (lines 82–130) only if every frame's residual is within 1 mm and 1e-3 rad (lines 78–79). Otherwise the episode is recorded as a problem and the whole packing run exits with an error (lines 350–352). In the same situation the old packer only printed a WARNING saying the score would not match local numbers and to raise it with the organizers (old lines 313–322).
  • A Track 2 trajectory in the flash_chord_object_trajectories_v1 format that does not record the evaluator's reference poses is now rejected (lines 314–319).
  • For a single-object episode, object names that are all object no longer fail the check against the reference's object names (lines 303–307).
  • In KIT_MANIFEST.json, only the sha256 entries of the packer and the Track 2 README changed.

What this meant for the project on 9/30: the scoring rules (one world, frame by frame, all 30 episodes, runtime aee95f2) are unchanged. Whether trajectories exported by the aee95f2 evaluator use a different world frame, and so trigger the conversion, has not been tested; once the first trajectories exist, check that they carry the evaluator reference poses. The 10/1 kit moved the runtime to 8c778f9; how the conversion works is described in "The 10/1 kit" above. Redo the kit fact excerpt against the new kit before building the submission flow (the old excerpt is results/submission/kit_facts.txt).

Box supports in the aee95f2 evaluation runtime

Since 10/1 the evaluation runtime is 8c778f9 (see "The 10/1 kit" above), so the problem described here no longer exists on the evaluation side. The section stays as the reason for retraining the three box sequences.

In aee95f2, the runtime the kit names (kit README.md:34), scene/support.py is byte-identical to v0.4.0 (md5 95e5e33f…): for a USD Cube it reads size and ignores xformOp:scale (support.py:85–86, 114–116; results/diverge/support_py_versions.txt). Only three of the 30 sequences use box supports, ep1 (vacuum), ep7 (iron) and ep28 (squeegee) (results/diverge/support_shapes.txt), and in simulation each box becomes a solid 1 m cube with half extents (0.5, 0.5, 0.5) (results/ml40/diverge/support_import_v040.txt). With that geometry the reference object sits inside the cube from frame 0: in ep7 all 896 frames are embedded by more than 5 mm, in ep1 519 of 747 frames, and in ep28 608 and 651 of 988 frames for its two boxes (results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt; unverified).

Upstream 8c778f905c (#185, 12:11 UTC-6 on 9/29) fixed this, 4 commits after aee95f2 (agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md, correction section). The repository's #185-patched source copy builds the ep7 tabletop with half extents (0.42, 0.141, 0.005) (results/ml40/diverge/support_import_p185.txt). That copy is based on v0.4.0 and has no trajectory export, so it is only good for training and controls.

The controls on ml40 (L40S) all finished by 14:35 on 9/30; details and criteria are in Divergence investigation. In 4M-step ep7 smokes, none of the 13 runs with the v0.4.0 importer (R0, 8 fp16 runs with seeds 43–50 and 4 fp32 runs with seeds 43–46) learned anything: 8 produced NaN and the other 5 stalled at an episode length of about 51 with a return of about −3.7. The 8 runs with the #185 importer (fp16, seeds 43–50) did not diverge and reached episode lengths of 243–309 and returns of 25.6–34.8. With ep1 cut at 60M steps (one seed), v0.4.0 in fp16 is NaN from the 30,001,152-step checkpoint on, v0.4.0 in fp32 is NaN from its first checkpoint (10,000,384 steps), and all 6 #185 checkpoints are finite.

Final verdict under the registered criteria A.3 (14:35 on 9/30): the hypothesis that v0.4.0 building the boxes as 1 m cubes triggers the divergence (H1′) is supported, with v0.4.0 fp16 diverging in 5 of 8 runs against 0 of 8 for #185 fp16 (paired seeds 43–50), one-sided Fisher p = 0.0128 < 0.05. The hypothesis that fp16 storage is necessary (H4) is rejected. Scope: H1′ was tested on ep7 smokes (4M steps, L40S); ep1 has only the single-seed evidence above, pointing the same way; ep28 was registered as description only. These controls cannot say how much fp16 storage and the BN statistics each contribute (results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt, results/diverge/train_summary_table_ml40_diag_final.txt, results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_L{0,1,3}_ml40.txt).

aee95f2 and v0.4.0 treat bad worlds the same way: a non-finite world only has its poses zeroed, and any world flagged as terminated makes the evaluator raise an error (results/batch1/termination_facts.txt). In batch 1, 172 of the 256 worlds in the ep7 smoke rollout went non-finite before the error (results/ml40/batch1/ep7_smoke_diag_excerpt.txt), and ep1 diverged in training, with all three checkpoint evaluations raising the error (results/batch1/ep1_eval_failure_excerpt.txt). The official evaluation runs one world; if that world is flagged as terminated, the episode yields no trajectory. In batch 1 the non-finite worlds always came with this error, so rxzhou infers that a world that blows up ends the same way; this has not been tested on aee95f2.

rxzhou's reading: the leaderboard runs no simulation. Participants export the trajectories themselves with the named runtime and Kaggle only scores the CSV (kit README.md:29–41). The eval_code_sha256 carried into the CSV covers only __init__.py, harness.py, metrics.py and rollout.py under evaluation/ (aee95f2 harness.py:123–135), and scene/support.py, the file #185 changes, is not among them. Switching runtimes therefore needs the organizers' written approval; it is not the participants' call (plan agent-doc/plan-ep1-ep7-divergence-2026-09-30.md, appendix A.6). The 10/1 kit moved the runtime to 8c778f9, so this no longer needs asking.

The three questions meant for the organizers (plan appendix A.6; no longer needed after the 10/1 kit, kept for the record)
  • May participants train and export trajectories with aee95f2 + #185 (or a runtime at or after 8c778f9)?
  • Will the runtime version named in the kit be updated?
  • If not, how should these three episodes be submitted, given that under aee95f2 the object is buried in a solid 1 m block?

If only aee95f2 is allowed, the object in these three episodes sits inside a solid 1 m block during evaluation, and none of the 13 ep7 smokes on that geometry learned anything, so rxzhou infers that these three cannot yield meaningful trajectories under aee95f2 (inference, not tested on aee95f2; execution record agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md, final verdict section).

If a switch is allowed, rxzhou's reading is that all 30 episodes must be re-exported on the same runtime: the packer's comment says "one submission is one evaluator build" (new kit tools/pack_submission.py:365–366, old lines 288–289). #185 changes only support.py and its test (from the upstream commit's file list, checked on 9/30 and not recorded in the repo), so the hashed files stay the same; as rxzhou reads it, the packer cannot detect a mix, and the project has to enforce this itself. How to train ep1, ep7 and ep28 waits for the answer (execution record, correction section).

Pre-submission check on the evening of 10/3

Plan agent-doc/plan-submission-check-2026-10-03.md, version 2; execution record agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-03-submission-check.md. Read-only throughout: nothing was submitted, the two CSVs were not changed, and the uploader ran with --dry-run only.

The new one changes only documentation, the Track 1 scoring code and v2dlb/mhr_submission.py; the packer, uploader, evaluation script, sample CSV and config/tracks.json that Track 2 uses are byte-identical, so both CSVs standFails (CSVs unaffected)

Only one thing in the new kit concerns this project: a private repository has to invite the GitHub account Nvidia-V2D-Challenge as a collaborator so that it can see the submitted commit. This repository is private; without this step the organizers cannot open the code link in the CSV. Kaggle does not block the upload, but it is a disqualification risk at verification. Report only: what is submitted is a one-world trajectory, and Tier 1 succeeds on MP-SR in 1 world for 8 of 30 episodes, while the 30-episode mean of the 4096-world MP-SR is 0.265; Tier 1's local scores under the kit's definitions are AUC 0.3780, SP-SR 0.5333, MP-SR 0.2667 and MPPE 25.17 cm, and the leaderboard is whatever Kaggle computes.

Submission flow

  1. Pick one checkpoint per episode and evaluate it with the kit's examples/run_policy_evaluation.py (inside 8c778f9, the runtime the 10/1 kit pins; a policy with NaN is never exported) on one world, producing episode_XXXXXX.parquet.
  2. tools/pack_submission.py --track track_2_tier1 --dataset-root … --code-commit <GitHub commit URL> checks frames and objects, converts poses into the released frame when needed, recomputes the four metrics locally, attaches the hashes and writes the CSV. Use the 10/1 kit (0f2f9f4b…); for Tier 2 add --frame-references, because the project's references are v5.4, not the released noisy input.
  3. submit/v2d_submit.py validates the CSV and uploads it to the tier's four metric competitions on Kaggle (one CSV); the rules of those four competitions must be accepted on Kaggle first. Five submissions per week, unlimited in the last three days.

Both full-recipe ep20 policies were also evaluated under the other playback: at real time the frame-trained policy's chord_sr goes 0.79 → 0.03 and MPPE 5.2 → 12.6 cm; frame by frame the real-time-trained policy's MP-SR goes 0.887 → 0.012 and MPPE 3.9 → 18.6 cm.

Withdrawn and rewritten statements
  • "The official evaluation most likely uses 1.67×": withdrawn on the afternoon of 9/28 (nothing official then). Since the kit, scoring aligns to source frames frame by frame and training uses frame-by-frame playback accordingly, but that follows from the scoring grid; the organizers have not said anything about playback speed as such.
  • "If the evaluation runs on Isaac Lab, even the simulator differs": FlashCHORD is the officially provided and recommended path, and the kit's evaluation is FlashCHORD. Withdrawn.
  • "Both policies collapse under the other playback": the frame-trained policy's MP-SR was only 0.0005 under its own playback to begin with; replaced by the per-metric statement above.
  • The 9/29 version of "What is still missing" listed the ep7 evaluator error as its own item and warned that a trained policy might blow up its single official world too. On 9/30 rxzhou found that v0.4.0 and aee95f2 both build the box supports of ep1, ep7 and ep28 as 1 m cubes, so that item is folded into the box-support section above. At 14:35 on 9/30 the registered criteria found the 1 m cube to be the trigger of the ep7 smoke divergence (H1′ supported, one-sided Fisher p = 0.0128, results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt); ep1 has only single-seed evidence.

Email draft v2 to the organizers, agent-doc/organizer-email-draft-2026-10-01.md (16:30 on 10/1), drops the runtime and termination questions and turns empty supports into a one-sentence note, leaving 5 questions: whether a commit URL in a private repository is acceptable and whether the code may be released after the freeze; whether a Tier 3 input will be released; whether evaluations may be repeated; how Kaggle teams should register; and a few data rules. On 10/1 it was decided that it can wait. It replaces the 9/28 email and Discord drafts (agent-doc/organizer-{email,discord}-draft-2026-09-28.md). The participant webinar is on 10/7 at 12:00 UTC-6 (11:00 Pacific in the announcement).

Day 2

原问题是“官方回放未知时训练用哪种回放更稳”。跑到一半官方 kit 发布，计分按源帧逐帧对齐，定为按“固定逐帧”处理：取消剩下的真实时长臂，改跑新序列的逐帧满配。新序列的逐帧 policy 8 条里 2 条过 Day 1 判据，其余 6 条都卡在 MP-SR 为 0。

计划与判据登记于 67f1d86（9/28 16:30）；执行记录 agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-28-day2.md（Day 2、Day 2b）与 2026-09-29-mf4090-night1.md（昨晚 mf4090 三条）。判定由脚本机械给出（stages/evaluate/batch_verdict.py、day2_compare.py）。

登记的判定

  • Q1（训练用哪种回放）：选 B 后两臂齐全的只有 ep26，有效计票序列不足 2 条，按规则不下结论；训练回放由官方计分格子定为逐帧。ep26 上按逐帧评，逐帧训练远好于真实时长训练（MP-SR 0.956 对 0.006）。
  • Q2（新序列上能不能过线）：逐帧格子 ml40 上 1/5 通过，连同 mf4090 昨晚 3 条是 2/8。登记规则是“两种训练都 ≤ 1/3 → 批量前先做 T1 与 T4”；真实时长一臂只剩 ep26 一格且通过，字面不触发。只看逐帧已经 ≤ 1/3，定为批量与 T1 / T4 诊断并行。
  • Q3（mf4090 的 400 万 buffer）：真实时长一臂两颗种子都不过，按规则不可用；第一次实测的种子噪声 gap −0.010、MPPE −2.2 cm。逐帧一臂只报数：ep20 上 400 万与 ml40 满配相近，ep27 是写这段时（9/29 早上）最好的一条，第 1 批按此让 mf4090 用 400 万分担。
  • Q4（只有地面的序列）：ep26、ep10、ep4 关掉支撑面后都建场景成功、正常训完。
两处执行偏差（都在对应结果出来之前登记）
  • mf4090 冒烟的显存上限：开链参数 23000 MiB 没写进计划，与登记的判据（只看 OOM 字样）不一致，三条冒烟被误判跳过；改成整卡总量后重核全过、重开。
  • 9/28 23:05 按 kit 改为固定逐帧：强制同步 ml40 改写来历，原训练链在 ep13 逐帧跑完后按设计停下，Day 2b 接着跑 ep10、ep4、ep2 的逐帧。

Day 2

The question was which training playback is safer when the official playback is unknown. Midway the official kit came out, scoring frame by frame against source frames, and it was decided to treat it as "fixed frame-by-frame": the remaining real-time arms were cancelled and replaced with frame-playback runs on new sequences. Of the eight new frame-playback policies, two pass the Day 1 criteria; the other six all stall at MP-SR 0.

Plan and criteria registered in 67f1d86 (16:30 on 9/28); execution records agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-28-day2.md (Day 2, Day 2b) and 2026-09-29-mf4090-night1.md (last night's three mf4090 runs). Verdicts are produced mechanically by scripts (stages/evaluate/batch_verdict.py, day2_compare.py).

What happened

9/28 16:33–17:20Preflight passed (identity check, ADD-AUC difference 0.003); all six ml40 smokes passed. The mf4090 smokes were wrongly failed by an unregistered memory limit, rechecked against the registered criterion and relaunched at 16:58 (execution deviation, marked as such)
9/28 19:46–22:30The official kit came out; checking it confirmed that scoring aligns frame by frame to source frames (see Official evaluation and submission)
9/28 23:05Option B chosen: after ep13 frame finished, cancel the ep13 and ep10 real-time arms and run the next middle-tier (ep4) and long-tier (ep2) picks frame by frame; at 23:39 the original training chain stopped at its source check, as designed
9/29 00:24–07:45mf4090 ran three more frame-playback runs with a 4M buffer: ep21, ep27, ep3
9/29 08:27Everything evaluated: 12 Day 2 evaluations, 9 Day 2b and 9 on mf4090, all successful

Every evaluation ran the source frame count and had no non-finite world.

Registered verdicts

  • Q1 (which playback to train with): after choice B only ep26 has both arms, fewer than 2 valid voting sequences, so by the rule there is no conclusion; the official scoring grid fixes frame playback instead. On ep26 under frame evaluation, frame training is far better than real-time training (MP-SR 0.956 against 0.006).
  • Q2 (do new sequences pass): 1 of 5 frame cells passes on ml40, 2 of 8 with last night's three mf4090 runs. The registered rule is "both trainings ≤ 1/3 → do T1 and T4 before the batch"; the real-time arm has a single cell (ep26), which passes, so literally it does not fire. Frame alone is already ≤ 1/3, and it was decided to run the batch and the T1 / T4 diagnosis in parallel.
  • Q3 (mf4090 with a 4M buffer): neither real-time seed passes, so it is not usable by the rule; the first measured seed noise is gap −0.010 and MPPE −2.2 cm. The frame arm is reported only: on ep20 the 4M run is close to the ml40 full recipe, and ep27 was the best policy when this was written (morning of 9/29), so batch 1 let mf4090 share the work with a 4M buffer.
  • Q4 (floor-only sequences): ep26, ep10 and ep4 built their scenes and trained normally with the support surface off.
Two execution deviations (each logged before the results it could affect)
  • The mf4090 smoke memory limit: the launch argument of 23000 MiB was not in the plan and disagreed with the registered criterion (OOM messages only), so three smokes were wrongly skipped; with the card total as the limit all three passed and the chains restarted.
  • At 23:05 on 9/28, the switch to fixed frame-by-frame after the kit: a forced sync of ml40 changed the recorded source, the original training chain stopped after ep13 frame as designed, and Day 2b then trained ep10, ep4 and ep2 frame by frame.

ep1 vacuum_left_lean_02mf4090 · A无效训练 checkpoint 从 50,001,920 步起含 NaN；3 项评测都跑完 747 步，各有 2342–2445 个世界位姿非有限，随后评测器报错（发散排查）
ep7 iron_round_table_02ml40 · A无效冒烟后 256 世界的诊断 rollout 有 172 个世界非有限、评测器报错，按登记跳过，没有正式训练（发散排查）
ep28 squeegee_sweep_away_from_you_03mf4090 · B无效50,001,920 与 60,002,304 步两份 checkpoint 连续非有限，看守 9/30 09:30 停训，不评（发散排查）

判定与数字取自 results/day2/batch_verdict_ml40_batch1a.txt（通过 2、不通过 2、无效 1）、batch_verdict_mf4090_batch1a.txt（2、2、1）、batch_verdict_ml40_batch1b.txt（1、4、0）、batch_verdict_mf4090_batch1b.txt（4、1、1）。ep0 按世界算 MP-SR 0.438，但 ADD-AUC 低于不动基线，按判据不通过；ep5 的 MP-SR 只靠 5 个世界；ep11 判为“不通过（边缘）”，chord_sr 只靠 2 个世界。ep1 的评测报错摘录 results/batch1/ep1_eval_failure_excerpt.txt，NaN 扫描 results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_ep1.txt。

ep7：冒烟后评测器报错，桌面在 v0.4.0 里是 1 m 立方体

ep7（iron_round_table_02）的冒烟训练通过，之后 256 世界的诊断 rollout 报 172 个世界位姿非有限，接着官方评测器报错（harness.py:526；摘录 results/ml40/batch1/ep7_smoke_diag_excerpt.txt），按登记跳过。现已查明：v0.4.0 的导入器对 USD Cube 只读 size、不读 xformOp:scale，把这条的桌面建成边长 1 m 的实心立方体（results/ml40/diverge/support_import_v040.txt）；按这个几何，iron 896 帧全部埋在立方体里，第 0 帧深约 49 cm（results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt；数字直接取自脚本输出，未核实）。ep1、ep28 同样是方盒支撑面。排查经过与 GPU 对照见发散排查。

27 条放在一起看

本批有判定的 18 条，加上 Day 2、Day 2b 与 night1 的 9 条（ep2、ep3、ep4、ep10、ep13、ep20、ep21、ep26、ep27），都是逐帧满配、最终 checkpoint（results/batch1/error_table_with_mf4090_b.txt，脚本 stages/evaluate/batch_error_table.py）。下面是事后描述，不作检验。

5MP-SR ≥ 0.95：ep12 0.992、ep8 0.979、ep23 0.975、ep24 0.962、ep26 0.956
2MP-SR 0.5–0.95：ep22 0.764、ep6 0.562
6MP-SR 在 0 与 0.5 之间：ep0 0.438、ep27 0.321、ep29 0.174、ep18 0.152、ep20 0.016、ep5 0.001
14MP-SR 恰好为 0

SP-SR ≥ 0.7 的有 15 条。按秩相关（Spearman，n = 27）看各项序列特征与 MP-SR 的关系：路程 −0.69、总转角 −0.65、运动占比 −0.50、单段最大转角 −0.03、物体半径 +0.28。难的序列是“一共要走多远、转多少”大的那些，跟某一下转得多猛关系不大（rxzhou 的理解）。

失败原因（事后分析，推断为主）

门槛出自评测器源码（results/batch1/metric_threshold_facts.txt）：一个世界先对帧取平均误差，MP 要位置 < 3 cm 且朝向 < 30°，SP 要位置 ≤ 10 cm 且朝向 ≤ 0.5 rad（28.6°）。下面的“平均位置 / 平均朝向”是再对 4096 个世界取平均，看典型世界卡在哪一项。

  • ml40 B 段的四条不通过分三种（agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-batch1b-failure-analysis.md）。VOC 是给物体的虚拟辅助力，课程里从 1 分段降到 0，评测时没有。
    • 朝向跟不住：ep15、ep17 物体一直在手里（chord 0.982、0.831），但在 VOC 撤掉的过程中，训练时的物体朝向误差一路涨，ep15 从 0.11 到 0.53–0.55 rad，ep17 从 0.11 到 0.61–0.64 rad；最终平均朝向 30.4°、42.0°。
    • 有辅助也学不会：ep19 在 VOC 全开的第 0 段参考进度只有 0.32，最好到 0.61，撤掉后回到 0.54。
    • 辅助一撤就掉：ep25 的参考进度从第 1 段的 0.70 降到第 8 段的 0.22，物体终止比例从 25% 升到 78%；最终平均位置 114.0 cm，推测是掉了。
  • mf4090 B 段的 ep16（spray bottle，半径 0.16 m，路程 5.27 m，参考里只有左手接触，184 / 0 帧）：训练的 W&B 摘要没有 NaN，但最终平均回合长度 118.7、回报 15.25，同段另外四条是 374–552 与 68–133（results/diverge/train_summary_table_mf4090_b.txt）。评测 chord 0、SP-SR 只有 1 个世界，平均位置 63.7 cm、平均朝向 85.4°，ADD-AUC 0.255 低于不动基线 0.266。推测训练阶段就没学起来，原因未查。
  • ep29、ep18 卡在 3 cm 的位置线：平均位置 3.5 cm、4.1 cm，平均朝向 5.8°、11.1°，都在 SP 门槛以内（SP-SR 0.989、0.966），MP-SR 只有 0.174、0.152。误差表把 ep22（3.7 cm）、ep6（3.3 cm）也标为卡在位置。ep29 的参考里双手都没有接触帧（0 / 0），序列名的意思是用左脚推 rolling luggage。
  • 分析文档按 22 条归纳：MP-SR ≥ 0.95 的 4 条（ep12、ep8、ep23、ep26）都是在地面上推或转的大件，解读里的共同点是用双手。新加的 ep24（watering can）MP-SR 0.962，参考里只有右手接触（0 / 519 帧），半径 0.19 m 落在文档里“小件”的 0.13–0.29 m 范围内，这条归纳要改。

分析文档列的改法（VOC 衰减更慢、加大朝向奖励、训练更长）都没试过，偏离官方配方，要另写计划登记；这些 Tier 1 结果也不能拿去调 Tier 2 / Tier 3 的方法。

每条的用时差别很大

ep1 第一个 1000 万步 4 分 53 秒（含编译），之后到 4000 万步每 1000 万步 3 分 17 秒到 3 分 20 秒，到 5000 万步那一段 6 分 22 秒，之后 7 分 17 秒到 7 分 25 秒直到训完；同一台上的 ep6、ep8 是 3 分 38 秒左右（results/batch1/pace_mf4090_a_2044.txt）。变慢的起点与 NaN 重合：第一份含 NaN 的 checkpoint 是 50,001,920 步（results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_ep1.txt），本地 W&B 历史里 NaN 首次出现在 42,444,800 步（results/diverge/wandb_local_history.txt），都落在第一段变慢的区间里。ml40 上的截断复现同样如此：L0（ep1、v0.4.0 导入器）第一份 NaN 在 30,001,152 步，每 1000 万步（不含第一段的编译，L1 同）从 3 分 18 秒变成 6 分 33 秒，之后 7 分 23 秒到 7 分 27 秒；L1（同样设置、换成 #185 的导入器）6 份 checkpoint 全部有限，一直是 3 分 9 秒到 3 分 10 秒（results/diverge/pace_trunc60m_ml40.txt、ckpt_nonfinite_L0_ml40.txt、ckpt_nonfinite_L1_ml40.txt）。这里只记重合，因果没查；之前写的“课程第一阶段之后接触计算变重”这条推测撤回。ml40 上 ep0 多用了约 20 分钟：9/29 10:32–11:46 同卡开着 3 个 viser 查看器、录了 3 条 rerun，这段每 1000 万步从 3 分 42 秒变成 5 分（results/ml40/batch1/pace_ml40_a.txt）。

节奏由 stages/policy/run_pace.sh 按 checkpoint 落盘时间算出，这个脚本训练期间也能在 Mac 上跑（经 stdin 传到训练机，不需要同步仓库）。

两条的参考动作对比（G1 运动学回放，2 倍速）：通过的 ep12 black_platform_turn_05 是在地面上转一个大件；彻底失败的 ep25 cyan_water_bottle_rotate_01 是手里连续转一个小瓶子。

  • 判定 results/day2/batch_verdict_{ml40,mf4090}_batch1{a,b}.txt；27 条误差表 results/batch1/error_table_with_mf4090_b.txt；训练摘要 results/diverge/train_summary_table_mf4090_b.txt。

Batch 1

Frame-playback full-recipe runs for the remaining 21 Tier 1 episodes, split across both machines in segments A and B. Started at 09:29 on 9/29; the last evaluation finished around 12:35 on 9/30. Under the Day 1 criteria 9 pass, 9 fail and 3 are invalid. The three invalid ones, ep1, ep7 and ep28, all use box support surfaces, and v0.4.0 builds all three as solid 1 m cubes with the reference object buried inside for most frames (script output in results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt, unverified); how their divergence relates to this geometry is covered in Divergence investigation.

Plan agent-doc/plan-batch1-tier1-2026-09-29.md (registered in 6e7ae5c); execution record agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-29-batch1.md. The queues come from stages/policy/make_batch_queue.py, and stages/policy/precheck_expect.sh ran the chain's assertions on all 42 expanded configs of both segments before launch (all passed). The verdict uses the final checkpoint only, and non_finite_worlds must be empty.

"Pass" here means the Day 1 criteria in stages/evaluate/batch_verdict.py: the final checkpoint, evaluated with matching playback on 4096 worlds, has chord_sr > 0, maniptrans_sr (MP-SR) > 0, and ADD-AUC above the sequence's frame-playback do-nothing baseline. MP-SR and SP-SR in the tables are success fractions over 4096 worlds; the leaderboard evaluates one world per episode, see Official evaluation and submission.

Verdicts and numbers from results/day2/batch_verdict_ml40_batch1a.txt (2 pass, 2 fail, 1 invalid), batch_verdict_mf4090_batch1a.txt (2, 2, 1), batch_verdict_ml40_batch1b.txt (1, 4, 0) and batch_verdict_mf4090_batch1b.txt (4, 1, 1). ep0 has a per-world MP-SR of 0.438 but its ADD-AUC is below the do-nothing baseline, so it fails the criteria; ep5's MP-SR rests on 5 worlds; ep11 is "fail (marginal)", its chord_sr resting on 2 worlds. ep1's evaluation error excerpt is in results/batch1/ep1_eval_failure_excerpt.txt and its NaN scan in results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_ep1.txt.

ep7: evaluator error after the smoke; v0.4.0 builds its tabletop as a 1 m cube

ep7 (iron_round_table_02) passed its smoke training, but its 256-world diagnostic rollout reported 172 worlds with non-finite poses and the official evaluator then raised an error (harness.py:526; excerpt in results/ml40/batch1/ep7_smoke_diag_excerpt.txt), so it was skipped as registered. Why the scene is wrong is now known: v0.4.0's importer reads only size from a USD Cube and ignores xformOp:scale, so this episode's tabletop becomes a solid cube 1 m on a side (results/ml40/diverge/support_import_v040.txt). With that geometry the iron is inside the cube in all 896 frames, about 49 cm deep at frame 0 (results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt; the numbers are taken directly from the script output, unverified). ep1 and ep28 also use box supports. The investigation and the GPU controls are in Divergence investigation.

All 27 together

The 18 runs with a verdict from this batch plus 9 from Day 2, Day 2b and night1 (ep2, ep3, ep4, ep10, ep13, ep20, ep21, ep26, ep27), all frame-playback full recipe at the final checkpoint (results/batch1/error_table_with_mf4090_b.txt, script stages/evaluate/batch_error_table.py). What follows is a post-hoc description, not a test.

5MP-SR ≥ 0.95: ep12 0.992, ep8 0.979, ep23 0.975, ep24 0.962, ep26 0.956
2MP-SR 0.5–0.95: ep22 0.764, ep6 0.562
6MP-SR between 0 and 0.5: ep0 0.438, ep27 0.321, ep29 0.174, ep18 0.152, ep20 0.016, ep5 0.001
14MP-SR exactly 0

15 runs have SP-SR ≥ 0.7. Spearman rank correlations (n = 27) of sequence features with MP-SR: path length −0.69, total rotation −0.65, moving fraction −0.50, largest single rotation −0.03, object radius +0.28. The hard sequences are the ones that travel far and turn a lot in total; how sharply any single turn goes matters little (rxzhou's reading).

Why runs fail (post-hoc, mostly inference)

The thresholds come from the evaluator source (results/batch1/metric_threshold_facts.txt): each world's errors are first averaged over frames; MP needs position < 3 cm and orientation < 30°, SP needs position ≤ 10 cm and orientation ≤ 0.5 rad (28.6°). "Mean position / orientation" below is additionally averaged over the 4096 worlds, to show where a typical world falls short.

  • The four ml40 segment B failures fall into three kinds (agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-batch1b-failure-analysis.md). VOC is a virtual assist force on the object; the curriculum lowers it from 1 to 0 in stages, and evaluation has none.
    • Orientation drifts: in ep15 and ep17 the object stays in hand (chord 0.982 and 0.831), but as VOC is removed the training orientation error keeps rising, from 0.11 to 0.53–0.55 rad for ep15 and from 0.11 to 0.61–0.64 rad for ep17; final mean orientation 30.4° and 42.0°.
    • Not learned even with assistance: ep19 reaches only 0.32 reference progress in stage 0 with full VOC, peaks at 0.61, and falls back to 0.54 once VOC is gone.
    • Drops as soon as assistance goes: ep25's reference progress falls from 0.70 in stage 1 to 0.22 in stage 8 while the object termination fraction rises from 25% to 78%; its final mean position error is 114.0 cm, which suggests the object was dropped.
  • ep16 in mf4090 segment B (spray bottle, radius 0.16 m, path 5.27 m, left-hand contact only in the reference, 184 / 0 frames): its W&B summary has no NaN, but the final mean episode length is 118.7 and return 15.25, against 374–552 and 68–133 for the other four runs in the segment (results/diverge/train_summary_table_mf4090_b.txt). The evaluation has chord 0, SP-SR from a single world, mean position 63.7 cm and orientation 85.4°, and ADD-AUC 0.255 below the do-nothing 0.266. rxzhou's guess is that it never learned during training; the cause has not been looked into.
  • ep29 and ep18 stall at the 3 cm position line: mean position 3.5 cm and 4.1 cm, mean orientation 5.8° and 11.1°, both inside the SP thresholds (SP-SR 0.989 and 0.966), yet MP-SR only 0.174 and 0.152. The error table also marks ep22 (3.7 cm) and ep6 (3.3 cm) as held back by position. ep29's reference has no hand contact frames at all (0 / 0); the sequence name says the luggage is pushed with the left foot.
  • With 22 runs, the analysis generalized that the 4 runs with MP-SR ≥ 0.95 (ep12, ep8, ep23, ep26) are all large objects pushed or turned on the floor, and its reading named two-handed contact as the common factor. The newly added ep24 (watering can) reaches MP-SR 0.962 with right-hand contact only in the reference (0 / 519 frames), and its radius of 0.19 m falls in the 0.13–0.29 m range the document calls small, so that generalization needs revising.

The changes listed in the analysis (slower VOC decay, a larger orientation reward, longer training) are all untried and depart from the official recipe, so they need their own registered plan; these Tier 1 results must not be used to tune Tier 2 / Tier 3 methods.

Run times vary a lot by sequence

ep1's first 10M steps took 4 min 53 s (including compilation), then it ran at 3 min 17 s to 3 min 20 s per 10M steps up to 40M steps, took 6 min 22 s for the stretch ending at 50M, and then 7 min 17 s to 7 min 25 s until the end; ep6 and ep8 on the same machine ran at about 3 min 38 s (results/batch1/pace_mf4090_a_2044.txt). The slowdown starts where NaN appears: the first checkpoint with NaN is the one at 50,001,920 steps (results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_ep1.txt), and the local W&B history first shows NaN at 42,444,800 steps (results/diverge/wandb_local_history.txt), both inside the first slow stretch. The truncated reproduction on ml40 shows the same: in L0 (ep1, v0.4.0 importer) the first NaN checkpoint is at 30,001,152 steps and the pace per 10M steps (leaving out the first stretch, which includes compilation; likewise for L1) goes from 3 min 18 s to 6 min 33 s, then 7 min 23 s to 7 min 27 s; in L1 (same settings with the #185 importer) all 6 checkpoints are finite and the pace stays at 3 min 9 s to 3 min 10 s (results/diverge/pace_trunc60m_ml40.txt, ckpt_nonfinite_L0_ml40.txt, ckpt_nonfinite_L1_ml40.txt). This records only that the two coincide; cause and effect have not been checked. The earlier guess that contact computation gets heavier after the first curriculum stage is withdrawn. On ml40, ep0 lost about 20 minutes: from 10:32 to 11:46 on 9/29 three viser viewers and three rerun recordings ran on the same card, and the pace went from 3 min 42 s to 5 min per 10M steps (results/ml40/batch1/pace_ml40_a.txt).

The pace comes from checkpoint write times via stages/policy/run_pace.sh, which also works from the Mac during training (the script is piped to the training machine, so no repository sync is needed).

The two reference motions side by side (G1 kinematic playback, 2× speed): ep12 black_platform_turn_05, which passes, turns a large object on the floor; ep25 cyan_water_bottle_rotate_01, which fails completely, spins a small bottle in the hand.

截至 2026-09-30 14:40（UTC-6）· 最终判定

ep1、ep7、ep28 发散排查

第 1 批里用方盒支撑面的三条 Tier 1 序列都训出了 NaN：ep1、ep28 在正式训练的 4000–5000 万步之间，ep7 在冒烟的约 241 万步，三条的批量判定都是无效。已经核实的是场景建错了：FlashCHORD v0.4.0 把这三条的方盒支撑面建成边长 1 m 的实心立方体。按这个几何用脚本重算，参考物体从第 0 帧起就埋在里面（脚本输出，未核实）。上游 #185 已修，kit 指定的评测运行时 aee95f2 没有这个修复。ml40 上的 GPU 对照 14:35 全部跑完。按登记判据的最终判定：“1 m 立方体是发散的触发”（H1′）得到支持，ep7 冒烟的 8 个配对种子里 v0.4.0 发散 5 条、#185 发散 0 条，单侧 Fisher p = 0.0128；H3（随机数值不稳）和 H4（fp16 存储是必要条件）被拒绝；ep1 的截断复现里，fp32 存储的 L3 也发散了。结论的范围：H1′ 是在 ep7 冒烟（400 万步、L40S）上检验的，ep1 只有单种子旁证、方向相同，ep28 登记为只描述。

10/2 补记：#185 几何下 ep7 的正式训练也发散了

方盒重训 t1-ep7（#185 补丁副本、mf4090）冒烟通过，正式训练在 130,004,992 步时 policy 还全部有限，140,005,376 步起含 NaN（5892 个，与 9/30 发散的 checkpoint 个数相同），看守 10/2 09:52 止损。本页的判定只覆盖 400 万步的冒烟：1 m 立方体是冒烟发散的触发，但去掉它以后，ep7 在约 1.4 亿步仍会发散，原因当时没查（results/box/t1ep7_divergence_mf4090.txt、agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-02-box6-eval-runtime.md）。t2-ep7 也在约 1.43 亿步发散（10/2 13:21 止损）。两条的机理、非有限防护与重训见 ep7 防护与重训。

计划 agent-doc/plan-ep1-ep7-divergence-2026-09-30.md（CPU 部分第 2 版 af261bf；附录 A 的 GPU 对照第 3 版 10:35 因方盒被建成 1 m 立方体而停用，改到第 5 版后 11:07 登记于 1867ffd）；执行记录 agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md；结果在 results/diverge/ 与 results/ml40/diverge/。本页把三种说法分开写：“判定”是登记判据经 stages/policy/diag_verdict.py 给出的；“描述”是看到结果后的读数，不计入判据；推断都标成推断或未核实。

ep1 变慢与出 NaN 在时间上重合，原先的推测撤回

ep1 在 mf4090 上前 4000 万步每 1000 万步 3:17–3:20（第一段 4:53 含编译），到 50,001,920 步那一段 6:22，之后 7:17–7:25（results/batch1/pace_mf4090_a.txt）。ml40 上截断复现的 L0 也一样：3:18 之后，到第一份 NaN（30,001,152 步）那一段 6:33，之后 7:23–7:27；同一序列换 #185 导入的 L1 一直是 3:09–3:10（results/diverge/pace_trunc60m_ml40.txt）。目前只能说变慢与出 NaN 重合，原因没查。9/29 页面上“课程第一阶段之后接触计算变重”的推测撤回。

导入器把方盒建成 1 m 立方体（已核实）

v0.4.0 的 scene/support.py 对 USD Cube 只读 size、不读 xformOp:scale：85–86 行只取一个边长，114–116 行按这个边长建正方体（results/diverge/support_py_versions.txt）。三条序列的 USDA 都用 scale 标尺寸。用部署的导入器（md5 95e5e33f…）实际建一次，方盒的半尺寸全是 (0.5, 0.5, 0.5)，同一次检查里的圆柱对照正常（results/ml40/diverge/support_import_v040.txt）。

序列标注尺寸v0.4.0 建成#185 建成（半尺寸，m）v0.4.0 几何下物体嵌入 > 5 mm 的帧第 0 帧有符号距离
ep7 tabletop84 × 28.2 × 1 cm100 × 100 × 100 cm(0.42, 0.141, 0.005)896 / 896−490.4 mm（最深 −495.1）
ep1 chair_back2 × 12 × 27 cm100 × 100 × 100 cm(0.01, 0.06, 0.135)519 / 747−463.2 mm
ep28 两块 chair_back_top各 10 × 13 × 2 cm各 100 × 100 × 100 cm各 (0.05, 0.065, 0.01)608、651 / 988−476.5、−410.3 mm

标注尺寸取自 USDA（results/diverge/support_shapes.txt），#185 的尺寸取自 results/ml40/diverge/support_import_p185.txt。嵌入按物体网格抽样顶点到方盒的最小有符号距离算，负数表示在盒内（stages/retarget/support_penetration.py --cube-as-v040，results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt）。后两列是脚本输出；ep7 这一行的论断未核实。

  • 上游 8c778f905c（#185，“Honor authored scale for Cube support geometry”，9/29 12:11 UTC-6）改成按 size × scale 建盒。kit 指定的 aee95f2 比它早 4 个提交，support.py 与 v0.4.0 逐字节相同（md5 95e5e33f…），所以在官方运行时里这三条同样是 1 m 立方体。
  • 本仓库的补丁 third_party/patches/flash_chord-v0.4.0-cube-support-scale-8c778f9.patch 只取上游 support.py 的改动；stages/policy/make_patched_fc_src.sh 生成打过补丁的源码副本（support.py md5 4d6eab11…，与上游 8c778f9 相同）。队列行写 V2D_FC_SRC=<副本> 时，scripts/dev/in-flash-chord 把副本挂到容器的 /workspace 并往 stderr 打一行挂载说明；写空的 V2D_FC_SRC= 就挂原来 third_party/video_to_data 里的上游 v0.4.0 源码（scripts/dev/in-flash-chord 第 9 行）。
  • 按 #185 的几何，参考本身的情况：ep7 iron_round_table_02 对 1 cm 桌面最多嵌入 0.5 mm；ep1 vacuum_left_lean_02 对椅背最多嵌入 4.1 mm；ep28 squeegee_sweep_away_from_you_03 在 239、209 帧里有顶点从两块 2 cm 板的另一侧穿出（results/diverge/support_penetration.txt）。ep1 另有 385 / 747 帧低于地面超过 5 mm（最深 23.8 mm），ep17、ep22 也穿地（123 / 440、136 / 719 帧），两条都训完没事（support_penetration_floor_controls.txt）。
  • 第 1 批里这三条的训练与评测都跑在 v0.4.0 的几何上，它们的无效判定说明不了 policy 在正确几何上能学成什么样。

同一条 ep7 iron_round_table_02 参考在两种几何下的样子（G1 运动学回放，2 倍速，不跑物理）：左边是 v0.4.0 的导入，桌面被建成 1 m 实心立方体，iron 从第 0 帧起就在里面；右边是 #185 的导入，桌面按标注尺寸建成薄板。

放大：一处出事，整条训练停下（读代码）

以下读自上游 v0.4.0 的代码，逐行带文件与行号落盘在 results/diverge/code_facts.txt（执行记录 C4）。

  • BatchNorm 的滑动均值与方差按 0.01 做指数平均，更新处没有非有限过滤（network.py:113–149）。一个含 NaN 的批次进来，统计量就一直是 NaN。
  • 梯度非有限时，_apply_adam_if_finite 只跳过参数与 Adam 动量（文档串原文 “Skip only parameters and Adam moments; callers retain all other attempted-update state”），同一步算出的 actor、critic、target 的 batch_stats 照写（learner.py:625, 643, 759, 776, 786）。
  • 每遇到一次非有限梯度，loss scale 乘 0.5（update.py:59, 76，配置 loss_scale_backoff_factor: 0.5）。W&B 本地记录里 ep7 冒烟的 loss scale 在一行之内从 262144 掉到 6.1e-05，ep1 从 4.19e6 掉到 2.1e-22，下一行是 0（results/diverge/wandb_local_history.txt）。
  • G1 配方把回放缓冲里的观测存成 float16（g1_recon_body_flash_sac.yaml:76，训练默认 float32），计算用混合精度、compute_dtype: float16。

塌掉后的 5892 个 NaN 全在 state.0021–state.0030（成对的 256、1024 与 486 维统计量）。486 维的输入归一化统计量逐块看：位置、速度、位置差与目标历史整块 NaN（手在物体系、目标在物体系两块只有位置部分 NaN）；所有 6D 朝向块、只取自参考的未来根位置与参考相位都有限（results/diverge/obs_block_nan_map.txt）。

这部分能说明什么（推断）

代码解释了“一个批次出事，整条 run 就停下”：统计量变 NaN 以后所有前向输出都是 NaN，参数跳步护不住统计量。它没说明最先是什么出事。上午的推断是 fp16 存储把过大的位置、速度存成 inf；GPU 对照里 fp32 存储的 R2 照样发散，H4 被拒绝。R2 两条发散 run 的 NaN 下标与 ep1（fp16）逐位相同（results/diverge/obs_block_nan_map_R2_s4{3,4}.txt），按 A.3 归入“溢出发生在存储之外”一支，只描述。

GPU 对照（ml40 L40S，9/30 11:08 起）

按附录 A 第 5 版。每组只和它的对照差一个因素：R3′ 对 R1 只差导入器，R2 对 R1 只差观测存储精度，L1 对 L0 只差导入器，L3 对 L0 只差存储精度；配置逐键核对，意外差异 0（results/ml40/diverge/cfgcheck_diag_ml40_*.txt）。

组序列导入器观测存储种子长度队列

除 R0 外 buffer 都是 400 万；冒烟是 4,096,000 步。截断按配置做：总步数 60,002,304，学习率调度仍按全程 683,592 次更新。队列 ① 11:08–11:41（9 条）、② 11:41–11:57、③ 11:57–12:03、④ 12:03–14:06（L0 到 12:41，L1 到 13:02，L2 到 13:23，L3 到 14:06）、⑤ 14:06–14:35（8 条）。原来的 ep1、ep28 是在 mf4090 的 RTX 4090 上训的，L 组都在 L40S 上，所以 L 组的结论一律写成“L40S、单种子、与……一致”。

结果（全部 27 条）

“发散”按 A.3：冒烟看 W&B 摘要有无 NaN、最终 checkpoint 有无非有限参数；截断的 run 看任何一份 checkpoint。发散的冒烟最终 checkpoint 都是 5892 个 NaN。节奏的第一段含编译，不列。来源：results/diverge/train_summary_table_ml40_diag_final.txt、ckpt_nonfinite_diag_smokes_ml40.txt、ckpt_nonfinite_ext4750_ml40.txt、ckpt_nonfinite_L{0,1,2,3}_ml40.txt、pace_trunc60m_ml40.txt。

按登记判据 A.3 的最终判定

判定文件 results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt（14:35，stages/policy/diag_verdict.py，输入是全部结果与导入器最终核对），执行记录末尾有“最终判定”一节。13:04 的中途判定（results/diverge/diag_verdict_seeds43_46.txt）在 4 个种子时给出 H1′ 的 p = 0.2143，按登记补跑 ⑤ 后再检验了一次；下表的判定栏以最终判定为准。

假设登记的检验读数判定
H3：与场景和精度无关的随机数值不稳R1 ≥ 2 / 4 发散 → 拒绝R1 5 / 8（种子 43–46 时 2 / 4 已拒绝）拒绝
H4：fp16 观测存储是“一处出事、整条训练死掉”的必要条件R2 ≥ 2 / 4 → 拒绝R2 2 / 4（种子 43–46；R1 对 R2 单侧 Fisher p = 0.7571；⑥ 没跑）拒绝
H1′：v0.4.0 的 1 m 立方体是三条序列发散的触发R1 对 R3′ 单侧 Fisher p < 0.05 → 支持；4 个种子时 p ≥ 0.05 → 补到 8 个再检验，仍 ≥ 0.05 → 不支持ep7 冒烟：R1 5 / 8 对 R3′ 0 / 8（配对种子 43–50），p = 0.0128；只检验了 ep7，范围见下支持
否定结论：仅修导入挡不住发散R3′ ≥ 2 / 4（或 ≥ 3 / 8）R3′ 0 / 8不成立
  • R0 发散，原 ep7 冒烟可以复现。
  • H1′ 的次要读数：R3′ 8 / 8 的回合 ≥ 64、回报 ≥ 2.37，回到非方盒冒烟的范围。
  • S：ep1 无 NaN、回合 73.0、回报 3.29，与非方盒冒烟一致（登记界限 ≥ 64、≥ 2.37）。ep28 登记为只描述、不判假设：按 #185 的几何，它的参考仍穿过两块薄板，分不开“修导入不够”与“参考穿板”。
  • L 组（L40S、单种子，只作旁证）：L0 发散、L1 不发散，与 H1′ 一致。L3 与 L0 只差存储精度，也发散，按 A.3 记为“fp32 单独不够”，与 H4 被拒绝同向。L2 登记为只描述。次要读数（第 1 段 35–60M 的平均回合长度，results/diverge/wandb_by_stage_L.txt）：L0 3.56、L1 204、L2 318、L3 3.55；登记的参照是 ml40 B 段 5 条第 1 段最短 209，塌掉的 run 约 5–6。
结论的范围

H1′ 是在 ep7 冒烟（400 万步、L40S）上检验的；ep1 只有 L 组的单种子旁证；ep28 登记为只描述。能说的是“v0.4.0 把方盒建成 1 m 立方体是 ep7 冒烟发散的触发，ep1 的单种子旁证同向”。说不了的是 fp16 存储与 BN 统计量在其中各占多少：H4 被拒绝只说明 fp16 存储不是必要条件。

描述（不计入判据）

v0.4.0 的 13 条 ep7 冒烟（R0、R1 8 条、R2 4 条）一条都没学起来：8 条 NaN；另外 5 条没出 NaN，最终回合都停在约 51、回报约 −3.7。#185 的 8 条回合 243–309、回报 25.6–34.8。原 ep7 冒烟（种子 42）在塌掉的前一行（2,355,200 步）回合也是约 50、回报 −4.11（wandb_local_history.txt）。登记的“发散”只数 NaN，那 5 条按判据算未发散，所以 H1′ 的 Fisher 检验比回合长度上的差别弱。回合停在约 51 的原因没查，把它归给 1 m 立方体只是推断。

冒烟基线也是同一个方向：31 条逐帧满配冒烟里，方盒三条的最终回合最短（ep7 5.0 且 NaN、ep1 53.0、ep28 57.4），其余 28 条都 ≥ 64.0（results/diverge/train_summary_table_{mf4090,ml40}.txt）；换 #185 后 ep1 73.0、ep28 87.0。

执行偏差：导入器核对看的文件

A.3 规定 #185 的 run 日志里必须有 in-flash-chord 的挂载行、v0.4.0 的必须没有，但写的文件是 runs/logs/<run>.s1.log，写错了：fc_run.sh:194 把 in-flash-chord 的输出写进 runs/logs/<run>.log，.s1.log 是 docker logs，只有容器里的输出，挂载行都是 0。判据的意思不变，改在 <run>.log 上核对（stages/policy/diag_importer_gate.py，两份都数）。14:35 的最终核对覆盖五个队列，27 条全部有效，#185 的每条恰有 1 行指向补丁副本，v0.4.0 的每条 0 行（results/diverge/importer_gate_ml40_final.txt；13:02 的中途核对见 importer_gate_ml40.txt）。

对提交的影响

  • 官方每条 episode 只评 1 个世界，每次提交要有全部 30 条（见官方评测与提交）。那 1 个世界被判终止，评测器就报错，这条交不出轨迹；非有限的世界本身只被剔除并警告（aee95f2 rollout.py:429–432、harness.py:580–581，results/batch1/termination_facts.txt）。第 1 批里非有限世界都和这个报错一起出现，所以推测发散也会走到这一步，还没在 aee95f2 上实测。
  • kit 指定的运行时是 aee95f2，9/30 重新下载的 kit（sha256 a2de2aee…）仍写这个提交（kit README.md:34）。在 aee95f2 里，这三条的物体从第 0 帧起埋在 1 m 的实心方块里。
  • 推断（没在 aee95f2 上实测）：如果只能用 aee95f2，这三条评测时就是这种几何；v0.4.0 的 support.py 与 aee95f2 相同，在这种几何上 ep7 的 13 条冒烟没有一条学起来，所以推测按 aee95f2 交不出有意义的轨迹。
  • 评测摘要 eval_code_sha256 只覆盖 evaluation/{__init__,harness,metrics,rollout}.py（aee95f2 harness.py:123–135），#185 改的 scene/support.py 不在里面。rxzhou 的理解：换导入器不能自己定，要组委会书面许可。
  • 打包器要求所有轨迹的评测摘要一致（原文 “One submission is one evaluator build.”，旧 kit tools/pack_submission.py:286–300，新 kit 同一检查在 363–377 行）。rxzhou 的理解：若允许用 #185，30 条都要在同一个运行时上重新导出。
原定要问组委会的三问（计划附录 A.6；10/1 的新 kit 把运行时钉在 8c778f9 后不再需要问，留作记录）
  • 能否用 aee95f2 + #185（或 ≥ 8c778f9 的运行时）训练与导出轨迹；
  • kit 指定的版本会不会更新；
  • 若不能，这三条 episode（在 aee95f2 下物体埋在 1 m 实心立方体里）该怎么提交。

接下来

  • ep1、ep7、ep28 用 #185 的导入器重训：10/1 的新 kit 把评测运行时钉在 8c778f9，这个版本已含 #185（决定记录 agent-doc/decision-organizer-q1-q3-2026-10-01.md；方盒计划第 2 版已部署，mf4090 10/2 06:43 开训；ep7 的第一次正式训练在约 1.4 亿步发散，见本页开头的补记）。训练链的发散检测（冒烟判据加“W&B 摘要无 NaN、最后一份 checkpoint 全部有限”，正式训练加看守）10/1 已做进链里，随 Tier 2 第 1 批上线。ml40 的诊断队列与两台的第 1 批都已结束。

As of 14:40 on 2026-09-30 (UTC-6) · final verdict

Divergence in ep1, ep7 and ep28

The three Tier 1 sequences in batch 1 that use box support surfaces all trained into NaN: ep1 and ep28 between 40M and 50M steps of their full runs, ep7 at about 2.41M steps of its smoke, and all three are invalid in the batch verdicts. What is verified is that the scene is built wrong: FlashCHORD v0.4.0 builds these box supports as solid 1 m cubes. Recomputed by script on that geometry, the reference object sits inside the cube from frame 0 (script output, unverified). Upstream #185 fixes this; aee95f2, the evaluation runtime the kit pins, does not have the fix. The GPU controls on ml40 all finished at 14:35, and the final verdict under the registered criteria supports H1′, that the 1 m cube triggers the divergence: over 8 paired seeds of the ep7 smoke, 5 diverged on v0.4.0 and 0 on #185, one-sided Fisher p = 0.0128. H3 (random numerical instability) and H4 (fp16 storage is necessary) are rejected, and in the truncated ep1 reruns L3, with fp32 storage, diverged as well. The scope is narrow: H1′ was tested on ep7 smokes (4M steps, L40S); ep1 has only single-seed supporting evidence pointing the same way; ep28 was registered as description only.

Added 10/2: the full ep7 run on the #185 geometry diverged too

The box retrain t1-ep7 (#185-patched copy, mf4090) passed its smoke, and its full run was all finite at 130,004,992 steps and had NaN from 140,005,376 steps on (5892 values, the same count as the checkpoints that diverged on 9/30); the watcher stopped it at 09:52 on 10/2. The verdict on this page covers only the 4M-step smokes: the 1 m cube triggers the smoke divergence, but without it ep7 still diverges at about 140M steps, for reasons not investigated at the time (results/box/t1ep7_divergence_mf4090.txt, agent-doc/experiments/2026-10-02-box6-eval-runtime.md). t2-ep7 diverged too, at about 143M steps (stopped at 13:21 on 10/2). The mechanism for both, the non-finite guard and the retrains are on ep7 guard and retrain.

Plan agent-doc/plan-ep1-ep7-divergence-2026-09-30.md (CPU part in version 2, af261bf; version 3 of the GPU controls in appendix A was dropped at 10:35 because v0.4.0 builds the boxes as 1 m cubes, and version 5 was registered in 1867ffd at 11:07); execution record agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md; results in results/diverge/ and results/ml40/diverge/. This page keeps three kinds of statement apart: a verdict comes from the registered criteria via stages/policy/diag_verdict.py; a description is a reading taken after the results and does not count toward any criterion; inferences are marked as inferences or as unverified.
