  • RuntimeWhat the Mac, the two training machines and the two images do2 machines
  • Dependencies and gapsExternal dependency versions and what is still missing5 gaps

数据流

三档走同一条链。实线框已跑通，虚线框未开始或受阻，绿框是训练所在环节。

挑战数据每档一个目录
→
Tier 1直接用
Tier 2v5.4 降噪 · 守卫
Tier 3视频重建 · 未开始
→
转换challenge_to_soma
→
重定向soma_to_g1 + 骨长
→
对齐检查间隙 < 20 mm
→
FlashCHORD 训练
→
评测
→
提交kit 已到 · 未接上
  1. 转换：challenge_to_soma.py 把挑战 parquet 转成官方重定向的输入目录。人体带逐帧骨长参数，丢掉会让关节整体偏 13.5 mm。
  2. Tier 2 降噪：export_tier2.py v5.4，进程一启动就禁止读取 Tier 1，每条序列产出一个位姿文件。
  3. 重定向与对齐检查：batch.sh 在一次性容器里跑官方 soma_to_g1，再查物体与地面、桌面的间隙。
  4. 训练：fc_train.sh，FlashCHORD G1 FlashSAC，全程 3.5 亿环境步；replay buffer 容量默认 1000 万，要 48 GB 显存。
  5. 评测：evaluate_policy.py 输出 ADD-AUC、MPPE 与成功率。评测 JSON 里的成功率字段 spider_sr_uncentered、maniptrans_sr、chord_sr 是 4096 个世界里的比例；kit 计分用的 spider_sr、maniptrans_sr 是 episode 的比例（SP-SR、MP-SR）。本项目的评测以策略自己的参考轨迹为对照：Tier 1 的物体参考就是干净轨迹，Tier 2 的是降噪后的。
  6. 提交：kit 的 examples/run_policy_evaluation.py（8c778f9 运行时、1 个世界）出轨迹，tools/pack_submission.py 打包，submit/v2d_submit.py 传到 Kaggle 上这一档的四个比赛。每周 5 次，最后三天不限。见官方评测与提交。

Pipeline

All three tiers go through the same chain. Solid boxes run today, dashed boxes are not started or blocked, and the green box is where training happens.

Challenge dataone folder per tier
→
Tier 1used as is
Tier 2v5.4 denoise · guard
Tier 3video rebuild · not started
→
Convertchallenge_to_soma
→
Retargetsoma_to_g1 + bone length
→
Alignment checkgap < 20 mm
→
FlashCHORD training
→
Evaluation
→
Submissionkit in hand · not wired
  1. Convert: challenge_to_soma.py turns a challenge parquet into the input folder of the official retargeter. The body keeps its per-frame bone-length parameters; dropping them shifts every joint by 13.5 mm.
  2. Denoise Tier 2: export_tier2.py v5.4 blocks all Tier 1 reads as soon as the process starts, and writes one pose file per sequence.
  3. Retarget and check alignment: batch.sh runs the official soma_to_g1 in a throwaway container, then measures the gap between the object and the floor or tabletop.
  4. Train: fc_train.sh, FlashCHORD G1 FlashSAC, 350 million environment steps per full run. The default replay buffer size is 10 million, which needs a 48 GB GPU.
  5. Evaluate: evaluate_policy.py reports ADD-AUC, MPPE and success rates. The success-rate fields in the evaluation JSON are spider_sr_uncentered, maniptrans_sr and chord_sr, each a fraction of 4096 worlds; the kit scores spider_sr and maniptrans_sr as fractions of episodes (SP-SR, MP-SR). The project's evaluation compares against the policy's own reference: for Tier 1 the object reference is the clean trajectory, for Tier 2 it is the denoised one.
  6. Submit: the kit's examples/run_policy_evaluation.py (8c778f9 runtime, one world) produces the trajectories, tools/pack_submission.py packs them, and submit/v2d_submit.py uploads to the tier's four Kaggle competitions. Up to 5 submissions per week, unlimited in the final three days. See Official evaluation and submission.

stages/policyFlashCHORD 与 Isaac Lab 的训练入口；可断点续训的 fc_run.sh（只留最近 2 份 state）、配置正向核对、冒烟判据、训练链；批量队列生成（make_batch_queue.py）、开跑前预跑链的核对（precheck_expect.sh）、按 checkpoint 看训练节奏（run_pace.sh，也认队列日志）、手写队列（fc_queue.sh）与等另一条链结束再跑的队列（queue_after.sh）；9/30 为发散排查加了 NaN 与回合长度扫描（train_nan_scan.py）、checkpoint 非有限扫描与看守（ckpt_nonfinite.py --watch）、本地 .wandb 解析（wandb_local_history.py）、#185 补丁源码副本（make_patched_fc_src.sh）与导入器实建检查（support_import_check.py）、导入器核对（diag_importer_gate.py）、按登记判据出判定（diag_verdict.py）。用法见批量训练与监控与停止可用
stages/evaluate评测仍用 FlashCHORD 自带评测器；本仓加了按判据下结论、逐帧诊断、“物体不动”基线、换一种回放评测、Day 2 判定与评测链、批量判定（batch_verdict.py）、kit 原文摘录（submission_kit_facts.sh）、policy 查看器（policy_viewer.py，viser / rerun）、多条误差汇总（batch_error_table.py，9/30）有合成测试
scripts/dev多主机同步与远端执行（V2D_ENV，训练链在跑时拒绝同步）、record（结果落盘带命令、md5、来历）、结果与 policy 回流、两个一次性容器入口（in-flash-chord 可用 V2D_FC_SRC 换挂源码副本）、镜像构建；9/30 加了 pipe-py（只用标准库的仓内脚本经 ssh stdin 在训练机上执行，不同步，链在跑时也能用）可用

Modules

LocationContentsState

运行环境

下文说"mf4090""ml40"指机器，"4090""L40S"只指显卡本身。

位置用途状态
Mac 工作树编辑代码与文档；scripts/dev/remote 同步到训练机后执行，V2D_ENV=ml40 切到 ml40。results/ 单向回流到 Mac：mf4090 回到 results/，其他主机回到 results/<主机>/可用
mf4090（实验室共用，9/28 起只有 rxzhou 在用；1× RTX 4090 24 GB，22 核）数据转换、降噪、重定向；Isaac Lab。FlashCHORD 放不下 1000 万 buffer；Day 1 的 100 万那条明显更差（两条同时换了 GPU 与机器，差别不能单独归因于 buffer）。400 万 buffer 跑逐帧满配可用（ep27 过线，ep20 与 ml40 满配相近），跑真实时长不可用（Day 2 Q3）；整卡读数 23.8 GB，至今没有 OOM。第 1 批分担一半 Tier 1：没发散的 6 条一条 1 h 57 min 到 2 h 14 min；ep1 从出 NaN 的那一段起变慢，用了 4 h 02 min（results/batch1/pace_mf4090_{a_2044,b}.txt）可用
ml40（实验室共用，9/28 起只有 rxzhou 在用；1× L40S 48 GB，空闲 45,458 MiB，28 核，111 GiB 内存）FlashCHORD 满配训练与评测；满配一条 2 h 03 min 到 2 h 31 min（第 1 批 8 条，随序列变，ep0 那条开着查看器；results/ml40/batch1/pace_ml40_{a_2044,b}.txt），峰值显存 40,273 MiB（Day 1）可用
镜像 robotic-groundingIsaac Lab 2.3.2 + 官方 toolkit：重定向与 Isaac Lab 训练（只在 mf4090）可用
镜像 flash_chord（22 GB）FlashCHORD：FlashSAC 训练与评测；两台都有。ml40 上的已随存储迁到 /lab_data，迁移后用 64 世界复核评测，ADD-AUC 与迁移前差 0.00015。FlashCHORD 源码默认从仓库的 third_party/video_to_data（v0.4.0）挂进容器；9/30 起 ml40 上另有一份打了上游 #185 补丁的源码副本，队列行写 V2D_FC_SRC=<副本> 时换挂它，只用于发散排查（见批量训练）可用

容器一律一次性启动、整仓挂 /repo，换机器要用相同布局，原因见 ADR-00003。

看 policy 效果：stages/evaluate/policy_viewer.py 包装官方 view_policy.py。viser 在服务器上开，经 ssh -L 转发到 Mac 的 localhost；rerun 录成 .rrd 拿回 Mac，用同版本（rerun-sdk 0.27.3）打开。在训练用的卡上开会拖慢训练，见特殊情况。

Runtime

Below, "mf4090" and "ml40" name the machines; "4090" and "L40S" refer only to the GPUs.

WhereUsed forState
Mac working treeEditing code and docs; scripts/dev/remote syncs to a training machine and runs there, V2D_ENV=ml40 targets ml40. results/ flows one way back to the Mac: mf4090 into results/, other hosts into results/<host>/Ready
mf4090 (lab machine, only rxzhou uses it since 9/28; 1× RTX 4090 24 GB, 22 cores)Conversion, denoising, retargeting; Isaac Lab. FlashCHORD cannot hold the 10M buffer; the Day 1 run with a 1M buffer was clearly worse (the two runs also differ in GPU and machine, so the gap is not attributable to the buffer alone). A 4M buffer works for full-recipe frame-playback runs (ep27 passes; ep20 is close to the ml40 full recipe) but not for real-time playback (Day 2 Q3); the whole card reads 23.8 GB, with no OOM so far. It took half of the Tier 1 work in batch 1: 1 h 57 min to 2 h 14 min for each of the 6 runs that did not diverge; ep1 slowed down from the interval where its NaN appeared and took 4 h 02 min (results/batch1/pace_mf4090_{a_2044,b}.txt)Ready
ml40 (lab machine, only rxzhou uses it since 9/28; 1× L40S 48 GB, 45,458 MiB free, 28 cores, 111 GiB RAM)Full-recipe FlashCHORD training and evaluation; one full run takes 2 h 03 min to 2 h 31 min depending on the sequence (8 runs in batch 1; the ep0 run had viewers open; results/ml40/batch1/pace_ml40_{a_2044,b}.txt), with a 40,273 MiB memory peak (Day 1)Ready
Image robotic-groundingIsaac Lab 2.3.2 plus the official toolkit: retargeting and Isaac Lab training (mf4090 only)Ready
Image flash_chord (22 GB)FlashCHORD: FlashSAC training and evaluation; on both machines. On ml40 it moved to /lab_data with the storage; a 64-world re-evaluation after the migration differs from the earlier one by 0.00015 ADD-AUC. The FlashCHORD source is mounted into the container from the repository's third_party/video_to_data (v0.4.0) by default; since 9/30 ml40 also has a source copy with the upstream #185 patch, mounted instead when a queue line sets V2D_FC_SRC=<copy>, used only for the divergence investigation (see Batch training)Ready

Containers are always throwaway and mount the whole repository at /repo, so every machine needs the same layout; ADR-00003 explains why.

To look at a policy: stages/evaluate/policy_viewer.py wraps the official view_policy.py. viser runs on the server and is forwarded to the Mac's localhost with ssh -L; rerun records an .rrd that is opened on the Mac with the same version (rerun-sdk 0.27.3). A viewer on a training card slows training; see Special cases.

已知缺口

  • 官方评测已随 kit 发布；10/1 的 kit 把运行时钉在 8c778f9（含 #185）。两台的 8c778f9 副本 10/1 晚已建、已核对；兼容性验证两台都通过（mf4090 10/2 06:43、ml40 10:17），v0.4.0 训出的 checkpoint 能在里面加载、导出。官方 1 个世界的分数与 4096 世界的均值可以差很多：t1-ep6 只导 1 个世界时 MP-SR 为 0，同一 checkpoint 4096 世界是 0.57。见官方评测与提交。
  • 选 checkpoint 的规则、批量导出（stages/evaluate/export_chain.sh）与 Mac 上的打包（stages/evaluate/pack_tier.sh）10/2 已写、已部署（提交流程计划第 2 版）；还没打出第一份 CSV。
  • runs/ 运行记录的字段已在批量训练列出，还没写进契约。
  • Tier 3 整条链路未开始。
  • 契约表全空；现有测试写明"暂无契约 ID"。

Known gaps

  • The official evaluation came with the kit; the 10/1 kit pins the runtime to 8c778f9 (with #185). The 8c778f9 copies on both machines were built and checked on the evening of 10/1; the compatibility check passed on both machines (mf4090 at 06:43 on 10/2, ml40 at 10:17), so checkpoints trained with v0.4.0 load and export in it. The official one-world score can be far from the 4096-world mean: exported on one world, t1-ep6 has MP-SR 0, against 0.57 on 4096 worlds for the same checkpoint. See Official evaluation and submission.
  • The checkpoint rule, the batch export (stages/evaluate/export_chain.sh) and the packing on the Mac (stages/evaluate/pack_tier.sh) were written and deployed on 10/2 (submission flow plan v2); no CSV has been produced yet.
  • The run-record fields are listed in Batch training but not yet in a contract.
  • The whole Tier 3 pipeline has not started.
  • The contract tables are empty; existing tests state that they have no contract ID yet.

架构决定

三份都是 Proposed，待团队确认。决定变了就改正文，末尾留一行变更记录。原文在 docs/adr/。

  • ADR-00001 训练栈用 FlashCHORD：Isaac Lab 每个策略 18.6 小时，装不进 5 周≈2 h 对 18.6 h
  • ADR-00002 档位隔离净室参数加运行时守卫，Tier 2/3 不碰 Tier 1−0.041
  • ADR-00003 交接与运行只经文件交接；多台训练机；ml40 就地重建镜像4 条交接边

Architecture decisions

All three are Proposed and awaiting team review. When a decision changes, the text is updated and a line is added to its change log. The originals are in docs/adr/.

  • ADR-00001 Training stackUse FlashCHORD: at 18.6 hours per policy, Isaac Lab does not fit in 5 weeks≈2 h vs 18.6 h
  • ADR-00002 Tier isolationClean-room parameters plus a runtime guard; Tier 2/3 never touch Tier 1−0.041
  • ADR-00003 Handoff and runtimeFile-only handoff, multiple training machines, image rebuilt on ml404 handoff edges

ADR-00001 训练栈用 FlashCHORD

Status: ProposedDate: 2026-09-25，2026-09-26 更新数字docs/adr/00001-training-stack.md

决定因素是时间：最坏情况下有 90 个策略，Isaac Lab 每个要 18.6 小时，装不进 5 周。代价是依赖 48 GB 卡，以及官方评测栈与播放速度未知。Isaac Lab 保留作备选。

FlashCHORD FlashSACIsaac Lab ReconBody
单策略时长（4090，按稳态吞吐推算）≈ 1.9–2.2 h（replay buffer 100 万）≈ 18.6 h
吞吐≈ 50,500 环境步/秒 · 2048 个并行环境3.34 s/iter · 4096 个并行环境
90 个策略≈ 170–200 卡时≈ 1,700 卡时
4090 能否满配不能：replay buffer 1000 万超过 XLA 显存上限能：4096 个并行环境仅 9.3 GB
评测自带，直接出比赛指标指标只进 TensorBoard
默认播放速度逐帧，30 fps 参考按 1.67 倍速播放按 fps 重采样
未证实

表里的时长来自 4090，且 replay buffer 缩到 100 万。满配 1000 万在 L40S 上按账算放得下，但没跑过，时长和显存余量都以 ml40 冒烟实测为准。播放速度是否与官方评测一致也未知，第一个全程训练的策略出来后，同一条序列在两种速度下各训一次对照。

9/28 进展（ADR 正文未改）：满配在 ml40 上实测 2 h 10 min，显存峰值 40,273 MiB。两种速度的对照已做，转动落后量没有差别，但真实时长那条按自己的参考过了 Day 1 判据；官方评测口径仍未知。见回放速度对照。

9/29 进展（ADR 正文待改）：官方 kit 的评测就是 FlashCHORD（要上游 aee95f2 的运行时），按源帧逐帧计分，训练固定用逐帧回放；正文里“官方评测栈与播放速度未知”这项代价已不成立。见官方评测与提交。

ADR-00001 Train with FlashCHORD

Status: ProposedDate: 2026-09-25, numbers updated 2026-09-26docs/adr/00001-training-stack.md

Time decides it. In the worst case the project needs 90 policies, and at 18.6 hours each, Isaac Lab does not fit in 5 weeks. The cost is a dependency on a 48 GB card, and the organizers have not said which stack or playback speed they evaluate with. Isaac Lab stays as the fallback.

FlashCHORD FlashSACIsaac Lab ReconBody
Time per policy (4090, extrapolated from steady throughput)≈ 1.9–2.2 h (1-million replay buffer)≈ 18.6 h
Throughput≈ 50,500 env steps/s · 2048 parallel envs3.34 s/iter · 4096 parallel envs
90 policies≈ 170–200 GPU-hours≈ 1,700 GPU-hours
Full recipe on a 4090No: a 10-million replay buffer exceeds the XLA memory limitYes: 4096 parallel envs use only 9.3 GB
EvaluationBuilt in, reports challenge metricsMetrics go only to TensorBoard
Default playbackOne frame per step, so a 30 fps reference plays at 1.67×Resampled by fps
Unverified

The times in the table come from the 4090 with the replay buffer cut to 1 million. The full 10-million buffer should fit on the L40S by rxzhou's accounting, but it has never run; time and memory headroom come from the ml40 smoke run. Whether the playback speed matches the official evaluation is also unknown: once the first fully trained policy exists, the same sequence is trained at both speeds for comparison.

Progress on 9/28 (ADR text unchanged): the full recipe measured 2 h 10 min on ml40 with a 40,273 MiB memory peak. The two-speed comparison is done: the rotation lag does not differ, but the real-time run passes the Day 1 criteria on its own reference; the official evaluation setting is still unknown. See Playback speed comparison.

Progress on 9/29 (ADR text still to be revised): the official kit evaluates with FlashCHORD (on the upstream aee95f2 runtime) and scores frame by frame against the source frames, so training uses frame-by-frame playback only; the cost in the text, that the official stack and playback speed are unknown, no longer applies. See Official evaluation and submission.

ADR-00002 档位隔离：净室参数 + 运行时守卫

Status: ProposedDate: 2026-09-25docs/adr/00002-tier-isolation.md

规则原文要求 Tier 2/3 的方法用 “anything other than the clean trajectory captions provided in Tier 1”，也就是不能用 Tier 1 的干净轨迹。下表各做法的取舍是 rxzhou 的解读，不是组委会的裁定。

做法判断
Tier 2 推理只读 Tier 2合规
v5.4：参数只由 Tier 2 与物理常识推出提交用
用 Tier 1 − Tier 2 估噪声上限与步长，做成门限（v5.3）不用
在 Tier 1 轨迹生成的噪声上调静止阈值（v5.2）不用
用 Tier 1 训出的权重热启动 Tier 2/3 策略不做
方法定型后只拿 Tier 1 测一次、不据此回调邮件确认

原文用的是 "encouraged"，不是 "must not"；把它当硬规则执行是 rxzhou 选的最严解读。官方对外部数据、跨 track 数据都还没有条文（规则草案 PR #164 标为 "Awaiting confirmation"）。

决定：以 0.041 的 ADD-AUC 换掉 Tier 2 整档被判违规的风险。官方若允许开发期用 Tier 1，换一个目录即可切回 v5.3。

守卫怎么实现

libs/v2d_infra/tier_guard.py 在提交管线进程启动时调用，拦截 open、np.load、pq.read_table、pq.ParquetFile、glob 五条读取路径，读 Tier 1 目录直接抛错。守卫下重导 v5.3，与改前 30/30 逐位一致，证明导出全程没读 Tier 1。

ADR-00002 Tier isolation: clean-room parameters plus a runtime guard

Status: ProposedDate: 2026-09-25docs/adr/00002-tier-isolation.md

The rule requires Tier 2/3 methods to use “anything other than the clean trajectory captions provided in Tier 1”, i.e. no Tier 1 clean trajectories. The decisions in the table are rxzhou's own reading, not an organizer ruling.

PracticeDecision
Tier 2 inference reads only Tier 2Compliant
v5.4: parameters from Tier 2 and physics aloneSubmission
Noise caps and step size estimated from Tier 1 − Tier 2, used as gates (v5.3)Not used
Static threshold tuned on noise generated from Tier 1 trajectories (v5.2)Not used
Warm-starting Tier 2/3 policies from Tier 1 weightsNot done
Measuring once against Tier 1 after the method is frozen, without tuning on itAsk by email

The wording is "encouraged", not "must not"; treating it as a hard rule is rxzhou's own strictest reading. The organizers have no rule yet on external data or cross-track data (draft rules PR #164 marks it "Awaiting confirmation").

Decision: give up 0.041 ADD-AUC to remove the risk of the whole Tier 2 entry being ruled invalid. If the organizers allow Tier 1 use during development, switching back to v5.3 only means pointing to a different folder.

How the guard works

libs/v2d_infra/tier_guard.py is called when a submission-pipeline process starts. It intercepts five read paths (open, np.load, pq.read_table, pq.ParquetFile, glob) and raises on any read of the Tier 1 folder. Re-exporting v5.3 under the guard matched the previous output bit for bit on 30 of 30 sequences, which shows the export never reads Tier 1.

ADR-00003 环节交接与运行方式

Status: ProposedDate: 2026-09-25，2026-09-26 修订docs/adr/00003-stage-handoff-and-runtime.md
边产物位置
recovery → retarget每条序列一个 npz：物体位姿（OpenCV 系、全长）、可见性、方法版本与参数runs/recovery/tier2_v5X/
retarget 输入官方目录结构 + v2d_meta.json（档位、源帧率、位姿来源）runs/motion_src/tierN/
retarget → policy官方 motion_v1 parquet，每档一个 motion rootruns/motion/tierN/
policy → evaluatecheckpoint + 评测 JSONruns/flash_chord/<run>/

上游 toolkit 以固定版本的本地副本接入，源码不改，行为改动在本仓包装；mf4090 连 GitHub 受限，升级靠 git bundle。容器一律一次性启动，整仓挂 /repo，因为 parquet 里的网格路径写的是 /repo/...；默认不占 GPU。

多台训练机（9/26 新增）
  • .env 放共用变量与凭据，.env.<名字> 只覆盖主机与路径，用 V2D_ENV=<名字> 选择。名字写错直接报错，不回落到默认主机，因为同步带 --delete。
  • 回流按主机分目录：runs/<主机>/、results/<主机>/；只拉 policy 与日志，不拉 state。
  • ml40 的镜像就地重建，不从 mf4090 搬：经 Mac 中转只有 0.23–1.2 MB/s；registry mirror 上的 CUDA base 与 mf4090 上 flash_chord 镜像的前 11 层 diff_id 相同。构建走直连模式，不读 .env、不传代理。

ADR-00003 Stage handoff and runtime

Status: ProposedDate: 2026-09-25, revised 2026-09-26docs/adr/00003-stage-handoff-and-runtime.md
EdgeArtifactLocation
recovery → retargetOne npz per sequence: object pose (OpenCV frame, full length), visibility, method version and parametersruns/recovery/tier2_v5X/
retarget inputOfficial folder layout plus v2d_meta.json (tier, source fps, pose source)runs/motion_src/tierN/
retarget → policyOfficial motion_v1 parquet, one motion root per tierruns/motion/tierN/
policy → evaluateCheckpoint plus evaluation JSONruns/flash_chord/<run>/

The upstream toolkit is a pinned local copy with its source left unmodified; behavior changes live in wrappers in this repository. mf4090 cannot reach GitHub reliably, so upgrades go through a git bundle. Containers are always throwaway and mount the whole repository at /repo, because mesh paths inside the parquet files point to /repo/...; they use no GPU unless asked.

Multiple training machines (added 9/26)
  • .env holds shared variables and credentials; .env.<name> overrides only the host and path and is selected with V2D_ENV=<name>. A misspelled name stops with an error instead of falling back to the default host, because the sync uses --delete.
  • Pull-backs land in per-host folders, runs/<host>/ and results/<host>/; only policies and logs come back, never state files.
  • The ml40 image is rebuilt in place instead of copied from mf4090: relaying through the Mac runs at 0.23–1.2 MB/s, and the CUDA base on the registry mirror has the same diff_ids as the first 11 layers of the flash_chord image on mf4090. The build runs in direct mode, reading no .env and passing no proxy.

L40S 训练 SOP

目标：在 ml40 上跑通 FlashCHORD 满配训练，先确认单个策略的实际时长和成功率，再批量训练 Tier 1 与 Tier 2 各 30 条。

第 1 批已结束

Tier 1 第 1 批 21 条于 9/29 09:29 开跑，最后一份判定于 9/30 12:59 落盘：通过 9、不通过 9、无效 3（ep1、ep7、ep28，都是方盒支撑面的序列）。“通过”指批量判定脚本（stages/evaluate/batch_verdict.py）按 Day 1 判据给的结论，不是排行榜成功率。见第 1 批批量与发散排查。补跑这三条与 Tier 2 的批量计划都还没写。

1 · 9/26–9/27前置条件完成镜像先建在系统盘；存储迁移 9/27 完成 2 · 9/27部署完成镜像验收、同步、W&B、续训实测 3 · 9/27–9/29验证：Day 1 → Day 2完成新序列逐帧 2/8 过线；按 kit 固定逐帧 4 · 9/29 起批量训练进行中Tier 1 第 1 批结束；Tier 2 第 1 批 27 条训完、评完；方盒 3 条两档重训，3 条训完、2 条在训，t1-ep7 发散

跑起来之后

  • 批量训练第 1 批的做法、手写队列、排后续队列、换导入器与核对6 节
  • 监控与停止看哪些信号，出现了怎么处理；链在跑时怎么查；发散看守12 条规则
  • 已知风险方盒建成 1 m 立方体、一处出事整条训练死掉、摘要看不出卡住，以及之前记下的 11 条14 条

排期：没发散的满配正式训练，ml40 的 8 条用时 2 h 03 min 到 2 h 31 min（ep0 那条开着查看器）、mf4090 的 6 条用时 1 h 57 min 到 2 h 14 min，随序列变（秒数舍去；results/ml40/batch1/pace_ml40_{a_2044,b}.txt、results/batch1/pace_mf4090_{a_2044,b}.txt）。ep1 用了 4 h 02 min：从出 NaN 的那一段起变慢（那一段 6 分 22 秒，之后每 1000 万步 7 分 17 秒到 7 分 25 秒），不算在上面的区间里。

L40S training SOP

Goal: run full-recipe FlashCHORD training on ml40, measure the real time and success rate of one policy first, then batch-train the 30 Tier 1 and 30 Tier 2 sequences.

Batch 1 is finished

The 21 runs of Tier 1 batch 1 started at 09:29 on 9/29, and the last verdict was written at 12:59 on 9/30: 9 pass, 9 fail, 3 invalid (ep1, ep7 and ep28, the three sequences with box supports). "Pass" is the batch verdict script (stages/evaluate/batch_verdict.py) applying the Day 1 criteria, not a leaderboard success rate. See Batch 1 and Divergence investigation. No plan is written yet for rerunning those three or for the Tier 2 batch.

1 · 9/26–9/27PrerequisitesDoneimage built on the root disk first; storage migration done 9/27 2 · 9/27DeploymentDoneimage check, sync, W&B, resume test 3 · 9/27–9/29Validation: Day 1 → Day 2Done2 of 8 new-sequence frame runs pass; frame playback fixed by the kit 4 · From 9/29Batch trainingRunningTier 1 batch 1 done; all 27 Tier 2 batch 1 runs trained and evaluated; the 3 box-support sequences are retrained in both tiers: 3 runs trained, 2 training, t1-ep7 diverged

Once it runs

  • Batch trainingHow batch 1 ran, hand-written queues, chaining queues, swapping and checking the importer6 sections
  • Monitoring and stopsWhich signals to watch and what to do when they fire; checking while a chain runs; the divergence watcher12 rules
  • Known risksBox supports built as 1 m cubes, one bad batch kills a whole run, summaries that hide stuck runs, plus the 11 recorded earlier14 risks

Schedule: full-recipe runs that did not diverge took 2 h 03 min to 2 h 31 min on ml40 (8 runs; ep0 had viewers open) and 1 h 57 min to 2 h 14 min on mf4090 (6 runs), depending on the sequence (seconds dropped; results/ml40/batch1/pace_ml40_{a_2044,b}.txt, results/batch1/pace_mf4090_{a_2044,b}.txt). ep1 took 4 h 02 min: it slowed down from the interval in which it went NaN (6 min 22 s for that interval, then 7 min 17 s to 7 min 25 s per 10M steps), so it is left out of those ranges.

If the migration is late (decided)

The flash_chord image is built on the root disk first and training goes ahead; the docker storage migration happens between two trainings (ADR-00003 revision). The fallback of filling in with a 1M buffer on mf4090 was ruled out by Day 1: that produced a clearly worse policy (the two runs also differ in GPU and machine, so the gap is not attributable to the buffer alone).

Three points to make to the admin
  1. Docker here uses the containerd image store (docker info shows driver-type io.containerd.snapshotter.v1), so the root in /etc/containerd/config.toml has to move as well; keep the nvidia runtime and registry-mirrors in daemon.json.
  2. Create /lab_data/v2d (mfshared, 2775).
  3. Stopping docker kills training containers, so the migration window has to come before training starts.

部署

存储迁移完成之后按顺序做。任何一步不符就停下，回报再定。

9/27 实际

镜像先在系统盘上建好（迁移之前），第 2–7 步都已做完。存储迁移之后的复核也已通过：Docker Root Dir 是 /lab_data/docker、镜像 ID 不变、容器里能看到 L40S。

  1. 验收迁移：df / 可用约 90 GB，docker info 的 Runtimes 含 nvidia，4 个 v2d_* 镜像都在，/lab_data/v2d 可写。
  2. 重建镜像：在 ml40 上 V2D_BUILD_DIRECT=1 scripts/dev/build-flash-chord（约 22 GB）。核对前 11 层 diff_id 与 mf4090 上的镜像相同，记下镜像 ID 与依赖 freeze。为什么不从 mf4090 搬，见 ADR-00003。
  3. 同步代码：Mac 上仓库根先 V2D_ENV=ml40 V2D_DRYRUN=1 scripts/dev/remote 看清单（删除项应为 0），再 V2D_ENV=ml40 scripts/dev/remote，推完从 Mac 侧做 sha256 核对。远端有非空的 runs/.chain-active-* 时它拒绝同步，见监控与停止。
  4. 上游 toolkit：ml40 上已是 v0.4.0（33129dd），与 mf4090 逐文件一致，不用再拉。v0.4.0 的 scene/support.py 把方盒支撑面建成 1 m 立方体（见已知风险）；诊断用的 #185 补丁副本在 ml40 的 /lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/fc_src/v040_p185，生成与挂载见批量训练。
  5. 训练输入：runs/motion、runs/motion_src、runs/recovery 已逐字节一致（9/26 核对，0 差异）；开训前再比一次清单，mf4090 上重新导出过才补传。
  6. 确认 Tier 2 用 v5.4：runs/motion_src/tier2/*/v2d_meta.json 里 object_pose_source 指向 tier2_v54。
  7. 确认卡空闲：nvidia-smi --query-compute-apps 与 docker ps 都为空，而且在约好的时段内。

Deployment

In order, after the storage migration. If any step does not match, stop and report before deciding.

What happened on 9/27

The image was built on the root disk first (before the migration), and steps 2–7 are done. The post-migration recheck passed too: the Docker Root Dir is /lab_data/docker, the image ID is unchanged and containers see the L40S.

  1. Check the migration: about 90 GB free on /, the nvidia runtime listed under Runtimes in docker info, all 4 v2d_* images present, /lab_data/v2d writable.
  2. Rebuild the image: on ml40, V2D_BUILD_DIRECT=1 scripts/dev/build-flash-chord (about 22 GB). Check that the first 11 diff_ids match the image on mf4090, and record the image ID and the dependency freeze. ADR-00003 explains why it is not copied from mf4090.
  3. Sync code: from the repository root on the Mac, first V2D_ENV=ml40 V2D_DRYRUN=1 scripts/dev/remote to see the list (it should delete nothing), then V2D_ENV=ml40 scripts/dev/remote, then check sha256 from the Mac. It refuses to sync while the server has a non-empty runs/.chain-active-*; see Monitoring and stops.
  4. Upstream toolkit: already v0.4.0 (33129dd) on ml40 and identical to mf4090 file for file; nothing to fetch. The v0.4.0 scene/support.py builds box supports as 1 m cubes (see Known risks); the #185-patched copy used for diagnosis is at /lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/fc_src/v040_p185 on ml40, and Batch training covers how to build and mount it.
  5. Training inputs: runs/motion, runs/motion_src and runs/recovery are already byte-identical (checked 9/26, zero differences). Compare the lists once more before training; resend only if mf4090 re-exported something.
  6. Confirm Tier 2 uses v5.4: object_pose_source in runs/motion_src/tier2/*/v2d_meta.json points to tier2_v54.
  7. Confirm the card is free: both nvidia-smi --query-compute-apps and docker ps are empty, and it is inside the agreed time slot.

Day 1 验证

四次运行按顺序跑，序列都是 Tier 1 ep20（rice_cooker）。通过条件事先定好，跑完不再改。

顺序运行要点通过条件
1满配冒烟fc_train.sh <ep20> 2048 4096000 …，replay buffer 容量保持默认 1000 万；显存每 200 ms 采一次exit 0；日志有 compiled FlashSAC JAX executables，跑过 409,600 步进入更新；无 OOM、base limit 报错；整卡峰值超过 44,000 MiB 就改用 800 万。另记峰值、稳态吞吐、XLA backend will use up to 行、onnxruntime provider
2ep20 全程fc_train.sh <ep20> 2048 349999104 /repo/runs/flash_chord/t1_ep20_w2048_cap10m_ml40跑到 100%，拿到 policy_349999104.safetensors；记录真实时长
3官方 snack_box 对照同样配方，parquet 用官方自带样例官方样例能训出来，用来区分配方问题与数据问题
4评测evaluate_policy.py evaluation.checkpoint=…chord_sr、maniptrans_sr 都 > 0；ADD-AUC 高于冒烟时的 0.46，具体阈值开跑前写进运行记录

结果（9/27，UTC-6）

顺序状态结果
1通过整卡峰值 40,273 MiB，稳态 50,580 步/秒；正式跑保持默认 1000 万 buffer
2完成ml40 12:44–14:54，2 h 10 min，一次跑完。mf4090 另跑了一条 100 万 buffer 版本（12:35–14:41）
3未通过（边缘）100 万 buffer 版本（mf4090，16:39 跑完）不通过：chord_sr 0（0/4096），ADD-AUC 0.540 低于本序列的不动基线 0.566，几乎所有世界在 pelvis 上越线。满配版本 9/28 在 ml40 上跑完（第 1 次因 W&B 上传中途退出，第 2 次一段跑完），也不通过：chord_sr 0.922、maniptrans_sr 0、ADD-AUC 0.5639 对 0.5662，边缘。见 snack 满配对照
4未通过登记的门槛是 ADD-AUC > 0.4708（物体完全不动，评测前修正；原写 0.465）。两条 ep20 的 chord_sr、maniptrans_sr 都 > 0，ADD-AUC 0.431 / 0.422，两套门槛都没过
ml40 上评测，4096 世界物体不动1000 万 buffer100 万 buffer
ADD-AUC0.4710.4310.422
chord_sr（任务原生）—0.790.017
maniptrans_sr00.00050.0044
MPPE3.3 cm5.2 cm10.0 cm
物体位置误差1.7 cm4.1 cm9.0 cm
物体朝向误差49.2°42.2°46.1°
  • 两个 policy 都把 rice cooker 推离了原位（位置误差比不动多 2.4 / 7.3 cm）。全程平均朝向误差只比不动少 7° / 3°，但这不等于没转：任务原生判据下不越物体门槛的世界（满配约 80%）必须在第 359 帧把物体转离初始朝向至少约 94°。ADD-AUC 丢在哪一段，由 Day 1b 诊断核实。
  • 1000 万 buffer 的 run 明显好于 100 万的，训练曲线从课程第 0 阶段（VOC=1）就分开（单种子）（两条同时换了 GPU 与机器，差别不能单独归因于 buffer）。100 万 buffer 不再用来衡量配方。
  • ADD-AUC 很大程度上奖励静止段的“不动”：snack_box 前 440 / 800 帧静止，物体完全不动就能拿 0.566，比 ep20 的 0.471 还高。及格线要用每条序列自己的不动基线，不能跨序列套固定值。
  • 评测 JSON 的 chord_sr 按任务原生门槛（pelvis、palm、object）算，失败原因看 reference_tracking.failure_cause_fraction；termination_cause_fraction 是旧口径，别混读。
  • 记录：agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-28-day1.md。表里每个数都由 scripts/experiments/2026-09-28-day1.sh 重新生成到 results/day1/，判定由 stages/evaluate/verdict.py 计算。
Day 1 之后

ep20 与 snack_box 都通过：开始批量。snack_box 通过、ep20 不通过：问题在本项目的数据，逐项排查参考轨迹。两者都不通过：先查配方与环境，不开批量。现状：ep20（1.67 倍速）未通过，满配 snack_box 也不通过（边缘），按这条规则不开批量。之后按诊断证据走：Day 1b 逐帧诊断 → 回放速度对照 → Day 2，见训练实验。Day 2 之后 rxzhou 选了批量与 T1 / T4 诊断并行，第 1 批于 9/29 09:29 开跑、9/30 12:35 评完，见第 1 批批量。

冒烟命令（在 ml40 仓目录下执行）
P='/repo/runs/motion/tier1/whole_body/soma/sequence_id=2026-06-08_16-57-34_rice_cooker_clockwise_04/robot_name=g1/data.parquet'
timeout 1800 nvidia-smi --query-gpu=timestamp,memory.used,utilization.gpu \
  --format=csv,noheader -lms 200 > runs/logs/fc_smoke_ml40_cap10m.gpu &
timeout 1800 nvidia-smi --query-compute-apps=pid,used_memory \
  --format=csv,noheader -lms 200 > runs/logs/fc_smoke_ml40_cap10m.apps &
timeout 1700 env V2D_GPU=1 scripts/dev/in-flash-chord \
  "TF_CPP_MIN_LOG_LEVEL=0 bash /repo/stages/policy/fc_train.sh '$P' 2048 4096000 /repo/runs/flash_chord/smoke_ml40_t1_ep20_w2048_cap10m" \
  > runs/logs/fc_smoke_ml40_cap10m.log 2>&1; echo exit=$?
grep -E 'exceeds the base limit|RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED|out of memory' runs/logs/fc_smoke_ml40_cap10m.log   # 应无输出
pgrep -af '[n]vidia-smi --query'   # 应无输出

.apps 文件按进程记显存，用来分清是不是别人占了卡。稳态吞吐用后半段 progress 行回归，不用日志里的累计均值（含编译时间）。

Day 1 validation

Four runs, in order, all on Tier 1 ep20 (rice_cooker). Pass conditions are fixed in advance and not changed after the runs.

OrderRunKey settingsPass condition
1Full-buffer smokefc_train.sh <ep20> 2048 4096000 …, replay buffer size left at the default 10 million; GPU memory sampled every 200 msExit 0; the log shows compiled FlashSAC JAX executables and passes 409,600 steps into updates; no OOM or base-limit error; if the whole-card peak exceeds 44,000 MiB, use 8 million. Also record the peak, the steady throughput, the XLA backend will use up to line and the onnxruntime provider
