  • Both policies push the cooker away from its place (position error 2.4 and 7.3 cm above the do-nothing case). Their mean orientation error is only 7° and 3° below it, but that does not mean they do not turn it: worlds that never cross the task-native object limit (about 80% for the 10M run) must have turned the cooker at least about 94° from its start by frame 359. Where ADD-AUC is lost is checked by the Day 1b diagnosis.
  • The 10M-buffer run is clearly better than the 1M one, and the training curves separate already in curriculum stage 0 (VOC = 1), single seed (the two runs also differ in GPU and machine, so the gap is not attributable to the buffer alone). The 1M buffer is no longer used to measure the recipe.
  • ADD-AUC largely rewards doing nothing during static segments: snack_box is static for 440 of its 800 frames, so an object that never moves scores 0.566, higher than ep20's 0.471. Pass bars must come from each sequence's own do-nothing baseline, not a fixed value across sequences.
  • The evaluation JSON's chord_sr uses the task-native limits (pelvis, palm, object); read failure causes from reference_tracking.failure_cause_fraction. termination_cause_fraction is a legacy measure; do not mix the two.
  • Record: agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-28-day1.md. Every number here is regenerated into results/day1/ by scripts/experiments/2026-09-28-day1.sh, the verdict is computed by stages/evaluate/verdict.py.
After Day 1

Both ep20 and snack_box pass: start the batch. snack_box passes and ep20 fails: the problem is in the project's data, so inspect the reference step by step. Both fail: check the recipe and environment first, and hold the batch. Now: ep20 (at 1.67×) failed and the full-recipe snack_box fails too (marginal), so by this rule the batch stays on hold. What followed went by the diagnostic evidence: the Day 1b diagnosis, then the playback speed comparison, then Day 2; see Training experiments. After Day 2 rxzhou chose to run the batch in parallel with the T1 / T4 diagnosis, and batch 1 ran from 09:29 on 9/29 until its last evaluation at 12:35 on 9/30; see Batch 1.

Smoke command (run from the repository folder on ml40)
P='/repo/runs/motion/tier1/whole_body/soma/sequence_id=2026-06-08_16-57-34_rice_cooker_clockwise_04/robot_name=g1/data.parquet'
timeout 1800 nvidia-smi --query-gpu=timestamp,memory.used,utilization.gpu \
  --format=csv,noheader -lms 200 > runs/logs/fc_smoke_ml40_cap10m.gpu &
timeout 1800 nvidia-smi --query-compute-apps=pid,used_memory \
  --format=csv,noheader -lms 200 > runs/logs/fc_smoke_ml40_cap10m.apps &
timeout 1700 env V2D_GPU=1 scripts/dev/in-flash-chord \
  "TF_CPP_MIN_LOG_LEVEL=0 bash /repo/stages/policy/fc_train.sh '$P' 2048 4096000 /repo/runs/flash_chord/smoke_ml40_t1_ep20_w2048_cap10m" \
  > runs/logs/fc_smoke_ml40_cap10m.log 2>&1; echo exit=$?
grep -E 'exceeds the base limit|RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED|out of memory' runs/logs/fc_smoke_ml40_cap10m.log   # should print nothing
pgrep -af '[n]vidia-smi --query'   # should print nothing

The .apps file records memory per process, which tells this project's run apart from another user's. Take the steady throughput from a regression over the second half of the progress lines, not the cumulative average in the log, which includes compile time.

批量训练

第 1 批的做法沿用，Tier 2 第 1 批起加了几样

10/1 起的 Tier 2 第 1 批（计划 agent-doc/plan-t2-batch-2026-10-01.md）沿用下面这套做法，另加了发散看守、B 段门槛、开链前环境核对等，见本页最后一节“训练链的新开关”。第 1 批：计划 agent-doc/plan-batch1-tier1-2026-09-29.md（登记于 6e7ae5c）：逐帧回放；16 条只有地面的序列加 scene.support=false；ml40 用 1000 万 buffer、mf4090 用 400 万；每台 A、B 两段，A 段评完后 B 段自动开跑。队列用 stages/policy/make_batch_queue.py 生成（不覆盖已有文件），开跑前用 stages/policy/precheck_expect.sh 预跑链的正向核对。Day 2 的训练链与评测链原样复用。结果 9 通过、9 不通过、3 无效，见第 1 批批量。

  • 两台各排一个队列，先 Tier 1，再 Tier 2；每条一个输出目录 runs/flash_chord/t{档}-ep{序号}-w2048-cap{10m|4m}-{机器}/。
  • 每段评完跑 stages/evaluate/batch_verdict.py <期望表> <结果目录>：按 Day 1 判据看最终 checkpoint；有效性闸是 3 个评测都在、步数等于源帧数、不动基线的帧数对得上、没有非有限世界、来历干净、3 个 checkpoint 各不相同；差距在 ±0.01 以内或只靠 ≤ 3 个世界的记为边缘。Tier 2 第 1 批只评最终 checkpoint，判定加 --steps 349999104；这时没有走势，也不查“3 个 checkpoint 各不相同”。
  • 每次运行落盘一份记录：仓库 commit、上游 commit、镜像 ID 与 base 层、parquet 的 sha256、完整配置（--cfg job --resolve 的输出）、seed、GPU 型号、开始与结束时间、评测 JSON。
  • 每份 checkpoint 约 165 MB（policy 4.9 MB + state 160 MB）。fc_run.sh 默认只留最近 2 份 state（V2D_KEEP_STATES），policy 全留，一条全程约 0.5 GB。训练完用 V2D_ENV=ml40 scripts/dev/pull-runs <run> 把 policy 和日志拉回本地；state 只用于续训，留在训练机上。Tier 2 第 1 批的正式训练设 V2D_KEEP_STATES=1，只留最新一份 state，一条约 0.35 GB。
  • 长跑关掉 W&B 的 checkpoint 上传（V2D_WANDB_UPLOAD_CKPT=0），见特殊情况。
  • 10/1 起批量链自带发散检测：冒烟判据查最新 policy 与 W&B 摘要，正式训练经 fc_run_watched.sh 挂看守，见监控与停止。
  • 不做跨档热启动：Tier 2 的策略从头训练，理由见 ADR-00002。

手写队列（诊断、截断复现、补跑）

make_batch_queue.py 只生成 t1-ep…（默认）或 t2-ep…（--tier 2）的 run 名，种子写死 42，precheck_expect.sh 与 day2_train_chain.sh 断言正式步数 349,999,104，走批量链会在冒烟后接着开 3.5 亿步的正式训练（计划 agent-doc/plan-ep1-ep7-divergence-2026-09-30.md 的“运行方式”）。所以诊断队列手写，用 stages/policy/fc_queue.sh 跑。

  1. 写 stages/policy/queues/<机器>_<名字>.txt，每行 [KEY=VAL ...] <run> <容器内 parquet> <world_count> <total_steps> [hydra 覆盖...]，# 之后是注释。run 名带 diag- 前缀；换种子写 reset.seed=N training.seed=N；行首写 V2D_WANDB_PROJECT=v2d-selftest、V2D_WANDB_UPLOAD_CKPT=0，以及显式的 V2D_FC_SRC（见下一节）。
  2. 链没在跑时同步，然后在训练机仓库根跑 fc_config_check.sh（结果在 runs/cfgcheck/）。
  3. 在 tmux 里跑 fc_queue.sh。它不写 runs/.chain-active-*，要自己在前后放、删一个非空的标记，否则挡不住同步，queue_after.sh 也等不到它。
  4. 截断复现按配置截断：总步数 60,002,304，加 training.optimization.learning_rate_schedule_updates=683592（349,999,104 ÷ 2048 × 4），学习率曲线与正式训练相同，正常跑完写 DONE（stages/policy/queues/diag_ml40_trunc60m.txt）。
命令（ml40，仓库根）
cd /lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge
stages/policy/fc_config_check.sh stages/policy/queues/diag_ml40_trunc60m.txt    # 最后一行应是“== 配置核对通过”
tmux new-session -d -s v2d-diag -c "$PWD" \
  'echo "diag $(cat .v2d-source-commit) $(date)" > runs/.chain-active-diag
   stages/policy/fc_queue.sh stages/policy/queues/diag_ml40_trunc60m.txt >> runs/logs/diag_ml40_trunc60m.log 2>&1
   rm -f runs/.chain-active-diag'

队列中途停下（某行失败或被 fc_stop 停掉）时日志里有“队列在 <run> 停下（rc=…）”；重跑同一条 fc_queue.sh 命令即从断点继续，有 DONE 的行跳过。

等上一条链结束再跑：queue_after.sh

stages/policy/queue_after.sh <等的标记名> <本链标记名> <队列文件> <日志> 开始就写自己的 runs/.chain-active-<本链标记名>（等待期间同步也被挡住），每分钟看一次 runs/.chain-active-<等的标记名>，最多等 6 小时；标记消失后再等卡空（最多 5 分钟），然后调 fc_queue.sh，结束时删掉自己的标记，日志末行“<队列> 结束，退出码 N”。批量链的标记名是它的日志名（day2_train_chain.sh:24），手写队列的标记名是你自己放的那个。

链在跑时仓库不能同步，服务器副本里可能还没有这个脚本，所以先复制到 runs/tools/ 再跑（runs/ 不在同步范围），复制时记下 md5。队列文件也要已经在服务器上；新写的队列同样复制过去，把路径换成它。9/30 的 ⑤ 就是这样排的（tmux v2d-diag5，md5 e412b5d7…）：④ 的最后一条 L3 14:06:10 跑完，④ 结束后 6 秒 ⑤ 开跑，14:06–14:35 跑完 8 条，退出码 0（agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md 的“最终判定”一节；L3 的完成时间见 results/diverge/pace_trunc60m_ml40.txt）。

命令（先 Mac，再 ml40）
# Mac，仓库根：复制（不经 scripts/dev/remote），记下 md5
ssh ml40 'mkdir -p /lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge/runs/tools'
scp stages/policy/queue_after.sh ml40:/lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge/runs/tools/
md5 -q stages/policy/queue_after.sh

# ml40，仓库根：md5 对上后放进 tmux
cd /lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge
md5sum runs/tools/queue_after.sh
tmux new-session -d -s v2d-diag5 -c "$PWD" 'bash runs/tools/queue_after.sh diag diag5 stages/policy/queues/diag_ml40_ext4750.txt runs/logs/diag_ml40_ext4750.log'

复制用 scp 只是一种写法，仓库里没有规定；⑤ 那次是在 Mac 上用 ssh ml40 "cd <仓库根> && mkdir -p runs/tools && cat > runs/tools/queue_after.sh" < stages/policy/queue_after.sh 传过去的（执行记录只记了 md5）。要紧的是两边 md5 一致，并把 md5 写进执行记录。

换导入器：V2D_FC_SRC 与 #185 补丁副本

scripts/dev/in-flash-chord 默认把仓库里的 third_party/video_to_data/robotic_grounding/flash_chord（v0.4.0，33129dd）挂到容器的 /workspace。设 V2D_FC_SRC=<目录> 只把这一处换成补丁副本，素材目录仍取原 checkout。副本里必须有 SOURCE_V2D.txt 和 src/flash_chord，否则 in-flash-chord 以退出码 2 退出；挂上时往 stderr 打一行 in-flash-chord: /workspace ← <副本真实路径>（…）。

副本由 stages/policy/make_patched_fc_src.sh <目标目录> 生成：third_party/video_to_data 不在 33129dd 就拒绝；只复制 flash_chord（不含 outputs/、logs/），打 third_party/patches/flash_chord-v0.4.0-cube-support-scale-8c778f9.patch，核对打完的 support.py 与上游 8c778f905c 的 sha256 相同，写 SOURCE_V2D.txt；目标已存在就退出，原 checkout 不动。ml40 上的副本 /lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/fc_src/v040_p185 把 ep7 的桌面建成半尺寸 (0.42, 0.141, 0.005)（results/ml40/diverge/{make_patched_fc_src,support_import_p185}.txt）。

  • 队列行：用 #185 的写 V2D_FC_SRC=/lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/fc_src/v040_p185，用 v0.4.0 的写空的 V2D_FC_SRC=，免得 shell 里残留的值混进来。没写这一项的行，导入器核对记无效。
  • fc_config_check.sh 调 in-flash-chord 时不带队列行里的变量，所以它看不到导入器，只证明配置相同。
  • 第 1 批的正式训练都是 v0.4.0。10/1 的新 kit 把评测运行时钉在 8c778f9（含 #185），ep1、ep7、ep28 两档按 #185 的几何重训：训练挂补丁副本，评测用 8c778f9 副本（方盒计划 agent-doc/plan-box6-and-eval-runtime-2026-10-01.md，第 2 版，已部署，mf4090 10/2 06:43 开训），见已知风险。
命令（ml40，仓库根；最后一步在 Mac）
cd /lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge
# 生成副本，成功时最后一行是 PATCHED_FC_SRC_OK <目录>
scripts/dev/record diverge/make_patched_fc_src stages/policy/make_patched_fc_src.sh /lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/fc_src/v040_p185
# 用这份导入器真正建一次支撑面（容器内、只用 CPU），看方盒的半尺寸
V2D_FC_SRC=/lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/fc_src/v040_p185 scripts/dev/record diverge/support_import_p185 \
  scripts/dev/in-flash-chord "python /repo/stages/policy/support_import_check.py /repo/runs/motion/tier1/whole_body/reconstructed_stage/2026-05-29_15-20-43_iron_round_table_02_support.usda"

# Mac，仓库根：结果拉回 results/ml40/
V2D_ENV=ml40 scripts/dev/pull-results

scripts/dev/record 同名结果会被新结果替换；已提交的名字（如上面两个）重跑时换个名字。

核对每条 run 实际挂的导入器

stages/policy/diag_importer_gate.py <队列文件>... 逐行读队列：写了非空 V2D_FC_SRC 的 run，runs/logs/<run>.log 里至少要有一行挂载说明，而且每行都指向这个副本；写空值的 run 一行都不能有。日志里没有“fc_run <run> 第 1 段”开始行的记“未跑”，有 DONE 却没有开始行的记无效；有无效时退出码 1。

要看的是 <run>.log：fc_run.sh:193–194 把训练段那次 in-flash-chord 的 stderr 写进它；<run>.s1.log 是 docker logs，只有容器里的输出，不会有挂载说明（计划附录 A.3 原文写成 .s1.log，是写错了文件）。9/30 诊断的 5 个队列 27 条全部有效，没有无效或未跑的（results/diverge/importer_gate_ml40_final.txt，14:35）。

命令（Mac，仓库根）
V2D_ENV=ml40 scripts/dev/pipe-py stages/policy/diag_importer_gate.py \
  stages/policy/queues/diag_ml40_ep7.txt stages/policy/queues/diag_ml40_ep7_fp32.txt \
  stages/policy/queues/diag_ml40_p185_smoke.txt stages/policy/queues/diag_ml40_trunc60m.txt \
  stages/policy/queues/diag_ml40_ext4750.txt

要落盘就在前面加 scripts/dev/record diverge/<名字>。队列文件从服务器上的仓库副本读。

fc_config_check 会先建出 run 目录和空日志

fc_config_check.sh 走 fc_run.sh 的打印模式（V2D_PRINT_CMD=1），但在打印之前 fc_run.sh 已经建好 runs/flash_chord/<run>/（里面只有 .lock，fc_run.sh:41、46），并 touch 出空的 runs/logs/<run>.log（fc_run.sh:47）。所以 <run>.log 存在不说明这条开跑过，要看里面有没有“fc_run <run> 第 1 段”这一行。

重训之前先挪走旧目录

  • 同名目录里有 DONE，fc_run.sh 直接跳过（fc_run.sh:44）。ep1 正式训练跑满了 3.5 亿步、带着 NaN 写了 DONE（训练链只在有 DONE 时记“完成”，见 results/batch1/pace_mf4090_a.txt），重训要换 run 名或先挪走目录。
  • 被 fc_stop 停掉的 ep28，目录 runs/flash_chord/t1-ep28-w2048-cap4m-mf4090 里留着 50,001,920、60,002,304 步的 state（对应的 policy 都含非有限参数），没有 DONE；STOP 标记在 fc_run 退出时已删掉（fc_run.sh:211–212）。用 fc_run.sh 或手写队列重跑同名 run，会从 60,002,304 步那份 NaN state 续训（fc_run.sh:161–174）；批量链遇到则记 SKIPPED（day2_train_chain.sh:94，续训不恢复 replay）。重训前先挪走这个目录（agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md 的“ep28 看守”一节）。
  • ep7 在 ml40 上只有冒烟，同样先挪走 runs/flash_chord/{smoke-,}t1-ep7-w2048-cap10m-ml40 或换 run 名。

训练链的新开关（10/1 起）

这些开关随 Tier 2 第 1 批上线。默认值不变的，旧队列照样能跑。

名字作用怎么用
stages/policy/fc_run_watched.sh正式训练的包装：后台起 ckpt_nonfinite.py --watch（每 120 秒扫新写完的 policy，按步数连续 2 份非有限就 fc_stop；--parent 绑在包装上），前台跑 fc_run.sh，训完等 12 秒再扫最新 policy训练链的正式阶段自动调用。退出码 9：看守止损；10：训完但最新 policy 非有限，写 NONFINITE，评测链不评；11：看守没起来，不开训练。看守日志 runs/logs/<run>.nanwatch.log
stages/policy/chain_after.sh等上一段评完、上一段完成至少 N 条，再开下一段训练链；不够就在下一段的日志里写原因与 DAY2_TRAIN_FAILEDchain_after.sh <上一段训练日志> <上一段评测日志> <N> <期望表> <冒烟队列> <正式队列> <显存上限> <日志名>，放 tmux
stages/policy/chain_preflight.sh开链前核对：来历干净、GPU 无进程、没有长链标记、环境里没有 V2D_FC_SRC、镜像指纹、上游 HEAD、上游已跟踪文件无改动、support.py 的 md5仓库根执行，全过打 PREFLIGHT_OK；用 scripts/dev/record 落盘
stages/policy/precheck_expect.sh正向核对，现在还断言 parquet 在 runs/motion/tier<档位>/ 下（档位取 run 名的 t<档位>- 前缀）训练链第 1 步直接调用它；开链前对每一段先跑一次
V2D_EVAL_STEPS评测链评哪几个步数（默认最后三份）Tier 2 第 1 批设 349999104
V2D_PRUNE_SMOKE_STATE=1冒烟判过、基线算完就删它的 state，步数记进 STATE_PRUNEDmf4090 开；smoke_check.sh 重判时认 STATE_PRUNED
V2D_MIN_FREE_MB每条冒烟与正式开跑前的可用磁盘下限，不够就停整条链默认 2048，mf4090 设 1200
V2D_FC_SRC 守卫环境或队列行里有 V2D_FC_SRC 就停链，除非 V2D_ALLOW_FC_SRC=1要换导入器的诊断队列显式放行
fc_config_check.sh 多队列一次接受多份队列，同一 parquet 的官方配方只展开一次冒烟与正式队列一起给
make_batch_queue.py --tier 2生成 Tier 2 队列：帧数取 Tier 2 的重定向汇总，支撑面取两台 support_kinds.py 的结果（必须一致），带 scale 的方盒拒绝排队Mac 上跑

Batch training

The batch 1 procedure carries over, with additions from Tier 2 batch 1

Tier 2 batch 1, from 10/1 (plan agent-doc/plan-t2-batch-2026-10-01.md), follows the procedure below and adds a divergence watcher, a gate for segment B, a pre-launch environment check and more; see the last section of this page, "New switches in the training chain". Batch 1: plan agent-doc/plan-batch1-tier1-2026-09-29.md (registered in 6e7ae5c): frame-by-frame playback; the 16 floor-only sequences get scene.support=false; ml40 uses a 10M buffer and mf4090 a 4M buffer; each machine runs segments A and B, and B starts on its own once A is evaluated. stages/policy/make_batch_queue.py generates the queues (never overwriting existing files), and stages/policy/precheck_expect.sh dry-runs the chain's checks before launch. The Day 2 training and evaluation chains carry over unchanged. Result: 9 pass, 9 fail, 3 invalid; see Batch 1.

  • One queue per machine, Tier 1 first, then Tier 2; one output folder per run, runs/flash_chord/t{tier}-ep{id}-w2048-cap{10m|4m}-{machine}/.
  • After each segment, run stages/evaluate/batch_verdict.py <expectation table> <results folder>: the Day 1 criteria on the final checkpoint, with validity gates (all 3 evaluations present, step count equal to the source frames, matching do-nothing baseline frames, no non-finite worlds, a clean source, 3 distinct checkpoints); a margin within ±0.01 or a pass carried by ≤ 3 worlds is flagged as marginal. Tier 2 batch 1 evaluates only the final checkpoint and runs the verdict with --steps 349999104; there is then no trend and no "distinct checkpoints" gate.
  • Every run writes a record: repository commit, upstream commit, image ID and base layers, parquet sha256, full config (output of --cfg job --resolve), seed, GPU model, start and end time, evaluation JSON.
  • Each checkpoint is about 165 MB (policy 4.9 MB + state 160 MB). fc_run.sh keeps only the latest 2 states by default (V2D_KEEP_STATES) and every policy, about 0.5 GB per full run. After training, V2D_ENV=ml40 scripts/dev/pull-runs <run> brings the policies and logs back; state files are only for resuming and stay on the training machine. Tier 2 batch 1 full runs set V2D_KEEP_STATES=1 and keep only the latest state, about 0.35 GB per run.
  • Long runs turn off W&B checkpoint upload (V2D_WANDB_UPLOAD_CKPT=0); see Special cases.
  • From 10/1 the batch chain detects divergence itself: the smoke criteria check the latest policy and the W&B summary, and full runs go through fc_run_watched.sh with a watcher; see Monitoring and stops.
  • No cross-tier warm start: Tier 2 policies train from scratch, as ADR-00002 explains.

Hand-written queues (diagnosis, truncated reproductions, reruns)

make_batch_queue.py only produces t1-ep… run names (default) or t2-ep… (--tier 2), with seed 42 hard-coded, and precheck_expect.sh and day2_train_chain.sh assert the full 349,999,104 steps, so going through the batch chain starts a 350M-step run right after the smoke (the "how to run" part of agent-doc/plan-ep1-ep7-divergence-2026-09-30.md). Diagnostic queues are therefore written by hand and run with stages/policy/fc_queue.sh.

  1. Write stages/policy/queues/<machine>_<name>.txt, one line per run: [KEY=VAL ...] <run> <parquet inside the container> <world_count> <total_steps> [hydra overrides...]; anything after # is a comment. Prefix run names with diag-; set other seeds with reset.seed=N training.seed=N; start each line with V2D_WANDB_PROJECT=v2d-selftest, V2D_WANDB_UPLOAD_CKPT=0 and an explicit V2D_FC_SRC (next section).
  2. Sync while no chain is running, then run fc_config_check.sh from the repository root on the training machine (output in runs/cfgcheck/).
  3. Run fc_queue.sh in tmux. It does not write runs/.chain-active-*, so place a non-empty marker before it and remove it afterwards; otherwise nothing blocks a sync, and queue_after.sh has nothing to wait for.
  4. Truncated reproductions truncate by config: 60,002,304 total steps plus training.optimization.learning_rate_schedule_updates=683592 (349,999,104 ÷ 2048 × 4), so the learning-rate curve matches the full run and the run writes DONE normally (stages/policy/queues/diag_ml40_trunc60m.txt).
Commands (ml40, repository root)
cd /lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge
stages/policy/fc_config_check.sh stages/policy/queues/diag_ml40_trunc60m.txt    # last line should read "== 配置核对通过"
tmux new-session -d -s v2d-diag -c "$PWD" \
  'echo "diag $(cat .v2d-source-commit) $(date)" > runs/.chain-active-diag
   stages/policy/fc_queue.sh stages/policy/queues/diag_ml40_trunc60m.txt >> runs/logs/diag_ml40_trunc60m.log 2>&1
   rm -f runs/.chain-active-diag'

If the queue stops partway (a line fails or is stopped with fc_stop), the log says it stopped at that run (队列在 <run> 停下（rc=…）); rerunning the same fc_queue.sh command resumes from there and skips lines that have DONE.

Starting a queue after another chain: queue_after.sh

stages/policy/queue_after.sh <marker to wait for> <own marker> <queue file> <log> writes its own runs/.chain-active-<own marker> at once (so syncing is blocked while it waits), checks every minute for runs/.chain-active-<marker to wait for> for up to 6 hours, then waits up to 5 minutes for the card to be free, calls fc_queue.sh, removes its own marker at the end and logs a last line with the queue's exit code. A batch chain's marker name is its log name (day2_train_chain.sh:24); a hand-written queue's marker is whatever you placed.

While a chain runs the repository cannot be synced, so the server copy may not have this script yet. Copy it into runs/tools/ first (runs/ is outside the sync) and note its md5. The queue file must already be on the server too; copy a newly written queue the same way and point to that path. Queue ⑤ on 9/30 was scheduled like this (tmux v2d-diag5, md5 e412b5d7…): L3, the last run of ④, finished at 14:06:10; ⑤ started 6 seconds after ④ ended, ran its 8 runs from 14:06 to 14:35 and exited with code 0 (the "final verdict" section of agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md; L3's finish time is in results/diverge/pace_trunc60m_ml40.txt).

Commands (Mac first, then ml40)
# Mac, repository root: copy without scripts/dev/remote and note the md5
ssh ml40 'mkdir -p /lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge/runs/tools'
scp stages/policy/queue_after.sh ml40:/lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge/runs/tools/
md5 -q stages/policy/queue_after.sh

# ml40, repository root: once the md5 matches, start it in tmux
cd /lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge
md5sum runs/tools/queue_after.sh
tmux new-session -d -s v2d-diag5 -c "$PWD" 'bash runs/tools/queue_after.sh diag diag5 stages/policy/queues/diag_ml40_ext4750.txt runs/logs/diag_ml40_ext4750.log'

scp is just one way to copy, and the repository does not prescribe one; for queue ⑤ the file went over from the Mac with ssh ml40 "cd <repo root> && mkdir -p runs/tools && cat > runs/tools/queue_after.sh" < stages/policy/queue_after.sh (the execution record notes only the md5). What matters is that the md5 matches on both ends and goes into the run record.

Swapping the importer: V2D_FC_SRC and the #185 copy

By default scripts/dev/in-flash-chord mounts the repository's third_party/video_to_data/robotic_grounding/flash_chord (v0.4.0, 33129dd) at /workspace in the container. Setting V2D_FC_SRC=<folder> swaps only that mount for a patched copy; assets still come from the original checkout. The copy must contain SOURCE_V2D.txt and src/flash_chord, or in-flash-chord exits with code 2; when it mounts one, it prints in-flash-chord: /workspace ← <real path of the copy>（…） to stderr.

stages/policy/make_patched_fc_src.sh <target folder> builds the copy: it refuses unless third_party/video_to_data is at 33129dd, copies only flash_chord (without outputs/ and logs/), applies third_party/patches/flash_chord-v0.4.0-cube-support-scale-8c778f9.patch, checks that the patched support.py has the same sha256 as upstream 8c778f905c, and writes SOURCE_V2D.txt; it stops if the target exists and leaves the original checkout alone. The copy on ml40, /lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/fc_src/v040_p185, builds ep7's tabletop with half extents (0.42, 0.141, 0.005) (results/ml40/diverge/{make_patched_fc_src,support_import_p185}.txt).

  • Queue lines: a #185 run writes V2D_FC_SRC=/lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/fc_src/v040_p185, a v0.4.0 run writes an empty V2D_FC_SRC= so that a value left in the shell cannot leak in. The importer check marks lines without it as invalid.
  • fc_config_check.sh calls in-flash-chord without the queue line's variables, so it cannot see the importer; it only shows that the configs match.
  • All batch 1 full runs used v0.4.0. The 10/1 kit pins the evaluation runtime to 8c778f9 (with #185), and ep1, ep7 and ep28 are retrained in both tiers on the #185 geometry: training mounts the patched copy and evaluation the 8c778f9 copy (box plan agent-doc/plan-box6-and-eval-runtime-2026-10-01.md, v2, deployed; mf4090 started at 06:43 on 10/2), see Known risks.
Commands (ml40, repository root; the last one on the Mac)
cd /lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge
# Build the copy; on success the last line is PATCHED_FC_SRC_OK <folder>
scripts/dev/record diverge/make_patched_fc_src stages/policy/make_patched_fc_src.sh /lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/fc_src/v040_p185
# Build the supports once with this importer (in the container, CPU only) and read the box half extents
V2D_FC_SRC=/lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/fc_src/v040_p185 scripts/dev/record diverge/support_import_p185 \
  scripts/dev/in-flash-chord "python /repo/stages/policy/support_import_check.py /repo/runs/motion/tier1/whole_body/reconstructed_stage/2026-05-29_15-20-43_iron_round_table_02_support.usda"

# Mac, repository root: pull the results into results/ml40/
V2D_ENV=ml40 scripts/dev/pull-results

scripts/dev/record replaces a result of the same name; pick a new name when rerunning under one that is already committed, such as the two above.

Checking which importer each run actually mounted

stages/policy/diag_importer_gate.py <queue file>... reads the queues line by line: a run with a non-empty V2D_FC_SRC needs at least one mount line in runs/logs/<run>.log, all pointing to that copy; a run with the empty value must have none. A run whose log has no "fc_run <run> 第 1 段" start line counts as not run yet, and one with DONE but no start line counts as invalid; any invalid run gives exit code 1.

The file to read is <run>.log: fc_run.sh:193–194 writes the stderr of the training segment's in-flash-chord call into it, while <run>.s1.log holds docker logs, only the output from inside the container, and never has the mount line (appendix A.3 named .s1.log, which was the wrong file). All 27 runs in the five diagnostic queues of 9/30 are valid, with none invalid or not run (results/diverge/importer_gate_ml40_final.txt, 14:35).

Command (Mac, repository root)
V2D_ENV=ml40 scripts/dev/pipe-py stages/policy/diag_importer_gate.py \
  stages/policy/queues/diag_ml40_ep7.txt stages/policy/queues/diag_ml40_ep7_fp32.txt \
  stages/policy/queues/diag_ml40_p185_smoke.txt stages/policy/queues/diag_ml40_trunc60m.txt \
  stages/policy/queues/diag_ml40_ext4750.txt

Put scripts/dev/record diverge/<name> in front to save the output. The queue files are read from the server's copy of the repository.

fc_config_check creates the run folder and an empty log

fc_config_check.sh uses fc_run.sh in print mode (V2D_PRINT_CMD=1), but before printing, fc_run.sh has already created runs/flash_chord/<run>/ (holding only .lock, fc_run.sh:41, 46) and touched an empty runs/logs/<run>.log (fc_run.sh:47). An existing <run>.log therefore does not mean the run started; look for the "fc_run <run> 第 1 段" line in it.

Move old folders aside before retraining

  • fc_run.sh skips a run whose folder has DONE (fc_run.sh:44). The full ep1 run went all 350M steps and wrote DONE with NaN weights (the training chain logs "完成" only when DONE exists; see results/batch1/pace_mf4090_a.txt), so a retrain needs a new run name or the folder moved aside.
  • ep28 was stopped with fc_stop. Its folder runs/flash_chord/t1-ep28-w2048-cap4m-mf4090 still holds the states at 50,001,920 and 60,002,304 steps (both matching policies have non-finite parameters) and no DONE; the STOP marker was removed when fc_run exited (fc_run.sh:211–212). Rerunning the same run name through fc_run.sh or a hand-written queue resumes from the NaN state at 60,002,304 steps (fc_run.sh:161–174), while the batch chain marks it SKIPPED (day2_train_chain.sh:94; resuming does not restore the replay buffer). Move the folder aside before retraining (the "ep28 看守" section of agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md).
  • ep7 only has a smoke on ml40; move runs/flash_chord/{smoke-,}t1-ep7-w2048-cap10m-ml40 aside or rename the run as well.

New switches in the training chain (from 10/1)

They went live with Tier 2 batch 1. Defaults are unchanged, so old queues still run.

NameWhat it doesHow to use it
stages/policy/fc_run_watched.shWrapper for full runs: starts ckpt_nonfinite.py --watch in the background (every 120 seconds it scans newly written policies and calls fc_stop after 2 consecutive non-finite ones by step; --parent ties it to the wrapper), runs fc_run.sh in the foreground, and after training waits 12 seconds and scans the latest policyCalled by the full-run stage of the training chain. Exit 9: stopped by the watcher; 10: finished with a non-finite latest policy, writes NONFINITE and the evaluation chain skips it; 11: the watcher did not start, so training does not start. Watcher log in runs/logs/<run>.nanwatch.log
stages/policy/chain_after.shStarts the next training chain only after the previous segment is evaluated and completed at least N runs; otherwise writes the reason and DAY2_TRAIN_FAILED to the next segment's logchain_after.sh <previous train log> <previous eval log> <N> <expectation table> <smoke queue> <full queue> <memory limit> <log name>, in tmux
stages/policy/chain_preflight.shPre-launch check: clean source, no GPU processes, no chain markers, no V2D_FC_SRC in the environment, image fingerprint, upstream HEAD, no changes to tracked upstream files, md5 of support.pyRun from the repository root; prints PREFLIGHT_OK when everything passes; record it with scripts/dev/record
stages/policy/precheck_expect.shThe positive config check, which now also asserts that the parquet sits under runs/motion/tier<tier>/ (tier taken from the run name's t<tier>- prefix)The training chain's step 1 calls it; run it once per segment before launch
V2D_EVAL_STEPSWhich steps the evaluation chain evaluates (default: the last three)Tier 2 batch 1 sets 349999104
V2D_PRUNE_SMOKE_STATE=1Deletes a smoke's state once it has passed and its baseline is computed, recording the step in STATE_PRUNEDOn for mf4090; smoke_check.sh accepts STATE_PRUNED when re-judging
V2D_MIN_FREE_MBMinimum free disk before each smoke and full run; below it the whole chain stopsDefault 2048; mf4090 uses 1200
V2D_FC_SRC guardStops the chain if V2D_FC_SRC appears in the environment or a queue line, unless V2D_ALLOW_FC_SRC=1Diagnostic queues that swap the importer allow it explicitly
fc_config_check.sh with several queuesTakes several queue files in one call and expands the official recipe once per parquetPass the smoke and full queues together
make_batch_queue.py --tier 2Generates Tier 2 queues: frame counts from the Tier 2 retargeting summary, supports from both machines' support_kinds.py output (which must agree), and refuses scaled boxesRun on the Mac

监控与停止

10/1 起训练链自带发散检测，Tier 2 第 1 批起用。冒烟判据加了两项：最新 policy 不能有非有限参数；W&B 摘要里动作均值、熵、回报不能是 nan，loss_scale 不能为 0。正式训练经 fc_run_watched.sh 挂看守，按步数连续 2 份 checkpoint 含非有限参数就 fc_stop。此前的第 1 批没有这些：ep7 的冒烟带着 NaN 拿到了 SMOKE_OK（results/diverge/train_nan_scan_ml40.txt、agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-29-batch1.md），ep1 带着 NaN 训满了 3.5 亿步。新判据在这两类旧 run 上回测过（results/t2/smoke_check_regression_{ml40,mf4090}.txt）。下表是各种信号的处理。

信号来源处理
训练链、评测链或手写队列结束runs/logs/<链名>.log 最后一行：DAY2_TRAIN_DONE / DAY2_EVAL_DONE（带完成、失败、跳过的条数，10/1 起还带发散的条数）或 …_FAILED；手写队列是“队列 … 全部完成”或“队列在 <run> 停下”，queue_after.sh 另记“结束，退出码 N”。下一段开不开由 chain_after.sh 判断，不开时在下一段日志里写原因回流结果（scripts/dev/pull-results，ml40 加 V2D_ENV=ml40），先扫 NaN 与回合长度（下面两行），再跑判定
冒烟不过smoke_check.sh：DONE、编译完成、无 RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED、最新 checkpoint 步数、W&B 同步、显存上限、镜像指纹；10/1 起另加最新 policy 无非有限参数（等它写完满 11 秒再扫）、W&B 摘要无 nan 且 loss_scale 不为 0；再加 256 世界 rollout 核帧数、算不动基线这条的正式训练记 SKIPPED，继续下一条。显存上限要与计划登记的判据一致，否则会误杀（9/28 mf4090）。冒烟的 state 若已被 V2D_PRUNE_SMOKE_STATE 删掉，重判时认 STATE_PRUNED
训练发散（NaN）跑完的 run：W&B 摘要里动作均值、熵、回报是 nan，loss_scale 为 0（train_nan_scan.py）。在跑的 run：policy checkpoint 出现非有限参数（ckpt_nonfinite.py）；本地 .wandb 出现 NaN 字样（看守只报警）正式训练：10/1 起由 fc_run_watched.sh 自动处理，按步数连续两份完整 checkpoint 非有限就 fc_stop，链记“发散”（退出码 9）；训完但最新 policy 非有限的也记“发散”（退出码 10），写 NONFINITE，评测链不评。ep1 出 NaN 后一直到 3.5 亿步都没恢复（results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_ep1.txt），不用等跑完。冒烟：10/1 起冒烟判据会拦下，这条的正式训练记 SKIPPED。不走训练链的手写队列，仍按 --watch 手动挂看守（见下）；开跑前放的 STOP 会被 fc_run.sh 当成残留清掉（fc_run.sh:131–134），挡不住。先看是不是方盒支撑面（已知风险第 1 条）；不是方盒的，换种子冒烟一次，仍出现就记录并跳过
没有 NaN，但回合很短（卡住）train_nan_scan.py --table 的平均回合长度与回报冒烟对照计划附录 A.0 统计的 31 条逐帧满配冒烟里非方盒 28 条的下界：回合 ≥ 64.0、回报 ≥ 2.37（经验范围，不是登记判据），低于就当可疑，查支撑面与参考。正式训练没有登记的界限，同段对比着看（ep16 最终回合 118.7，同段另外四条 374–552，评测不通过）
训练明显变慢（比同机其他序列慢 30% 以上）日志里的 env steps/s；stages/policy/run_pace.sh 算 checkpoint 间隔先扫 checkpoint（ckpt_nonfinite.py --last）：ep1 与 L0 的变慢都从出 NaN 的那一段开始（ep1 3:17–3:20 → 6:22 → 7:17–7:25；L0 3:18 → 6:33 → 7:23–7:27）。有非有限就按上面止损；全部有限再查卡上有没有别的进程或查看器（开查看器约慢 35%，ep0，9/29）；只有训练进程就不停
来历变了，或卡上有别人的进程链在每条 run 前比对 .v2d-source-commit、查 nvidia-smi停整条链。链在跑时 scripts/dev/remote 拒绝同步，只读检查用普通 ssh，见下
评测器报 evaluation must disable task failure termination…冒烟的 diag_rollout 或评测日志这条记 SKIPPED，链继续；先挪走 runs/flash_chord/{smoke-,}<run> 或换 run 名，再放进下一批。第 1 批出过两次（ep7 冒烟、ep1 正式），都在 v0.4.0 的 1 m 立方体几何上；方盒支撑面的序列先看已知风险第 1 条，按方盒计划在 #185 的几何上重训。提交导出时报这个错，按规矩二退到同一条 run 里更早的有限 checkpoint
评测里有非有限世界评测 JSON 的 non_finite_worlds、评测日志的 worlds recorded non-finite poses批量判定按有效性闸记无效。先查训练有没有发散（上面两行），再决定要不要重训
想看 policy 的效果—录短的 rerun .rrd 拿回 Mac 看，或训完再开 viser（ssh -L 转发）。训练中在同一张卡上开会拖慢约 35%，而且训练链换 run 时查到卡上有别的进程会停链
显存 OOM进程退出码（fc_run.sh 返回 4）、XLA 报错记录后把 replay buffer 容量降到 800 万重跑（再不行 600 万），并在运行记录里标明偏离配方。训练链遇到 OOM 就停掉剩下的同配置 run；Day 2 的 B 按登记不降档，记“无结果”
W&B 上传把训练带崩日志里的 AuthenticationError长跑一律 V2D_WANDB_UPLOAD_CKPT=0；崩了按登记从 0 重来，不续训（续训不恢复 replay）
冒烟或正式训练在 W&B 初始化时网络超时分段日志里的 CommError: Error uploading run: context deadline exceeded队列设了 V2D_MAX_RETRY=0，按失败处理；冒烟失败时它的正式训练记 SKIPPED。与序列无关。目录里没有 state 时链重跑会从 0 开始，放到后面的段补跑，不改正在跑的链（10/1 ml40 的 ep10 改到 C 段，results/t2/ep10_smoke_failure_ml40.txt）
评测成功率为 0评测 JSON 的 reference_tracking.failure_cause_fraction（任务原生口径；termination_cause_fraction 是旧口径）按原因归类（object、palm、pelvis），不当场调参，攒够样本再统一处理

来源：ep1 首份非有限 checkpoint 50,001,920 步（results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_ep1.txt），节奏 results/batch1/pace_mf4090_a.txt；L0 results/diverge/{ckpt_nonfinite_L0_ml40,pace_trunc60m_ml40}.txt；ep16 results/diverge/train_summary_table_mf4090_b.txt；ep0 的查看器 agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-29-batch1.md。

链在跑时怎么查

链开跑时写 runs/.chain-active-<名字>（批量链用日志名，手写队列自己放，queue_after.sh 写自己的）。scripts/dev/remote 看到非空的标记就拒绝同步：同步会改写 .v2d-source-commit、换掉链后面才调用的脚本，链的来历核对会停掉剩下的训练。V2D_FORCE_SYNC=1 能绕过，链在跑时别用。

  • 只读查询直接 ssh（例子见下）。
  • 仓内只用标准库的 Python 脚本用 scripts/dev/pipe-py 跑：脚本经 ssh stdin 送过去，在远端仓库根用宿主机的 python3（两台都是 3.10）执行，不同步。参数只能用字母、数字和 _./=:,+-。要落盘就在前面加 scripts/dev/record <名字>，结果直接写到 Mac 的 results/。
  • 节奏用 stages/policy/run_pace.sh <日志> <run>...，同样经 stdin、不同步。日志可以是训练链日志，也可以是 fc_queue.sh 的队列日志（9/30 起两种都认）；时间换成 UTC-6。
  • 要在服务器上长时间跑的新脚本（看守、queue_after.sh）先复制到 runs/tools/，核对 md5，用 setsid nohup 或 tmux 起。别经前台 ssh 或 pipe-py 跑，Mac 断网它就断了。
只读查询与扫描（Mac，仓库根）
# 链的标记、队列日志末尾、卡上的进程、tmux 会话（只读，直接 ssh）
ssh ml40 'cd /lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge && cat runs/.chain-active-* 2>/dev/null; tail -n 5 runs/logs/diag_ml40_ext4750.log; nvidia-smi --query-compute-apps=pid,used_memory --format=csv,noheader; tmux ls'

# 跑完的 run：摘要里有 NaN 的；每条一行的回合长度、回报、loss_scale（只看 runs/logs/*.s1.log，即第 1 段）
V2D_ENV=ml40 scripts/dev/pipe-py stages/policy/train_nan_scan.py
V2D_ENV=ml40 scripts/dev/pipe-py stages/policy/train_nan_scan.py --table

# checkpoint 里的非有限参数：全部份数 / 只看最新一份完整的 / 按张量列出下标
V2D_ENV=ml40 scripts/dev/pipe-py stages/policy/ckpt_nonfinite.py diag-t1-ep1-cap4m-v040-fp16-s42-t60m-ml40
V2D_ENV=ml40 scripts/dev/pipe-py stages/policy/ckpt_nonfinite.py --last diag-t1-ep1-cap4m-v040-fp32-s42-t60m-ml40
scripts/dev/pipe-py stages/policy/ckpt_nonfinite.py --by-tensor --steps 40001536,50001920 t1-ep1-w2048-cap4m-mf4090

# 节奏（训练链日志或队列日志 + run 名）
V2D_ENV=ml40 stages/policy/run_pace.sh runs/logs/diag_ml40_trunc60m.log diag-t1-ep1-cap4m-v040-fp32-s42-t60m-ml40

不写 V2D_ENV 就是 mf4090（/home/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge）。各行前面加 scripts/dev/record diverge/<名字> 就落盘；同名结果会被替换。

发散看守（ckpt_nonfinite.py --watch）

每 --interval 秒（默认 120）看一次新写完的 policy checkpoint；按步数连续 --stop-after 份（默认 2）完整 checkpoint 都含非有限参数，就调 stages/policy/fc_stop.sh <run> 并退出。run 目录已有 DONE 或 STOP 时只报告、不止损；到 --timeout 秒（默认 14400）也退出。每轮另数一次本地 .wandb 里的 NaN 字样，变了只报警。唯一的写操作是 fc_stop.sh。日志末行是 NAN_WATCH_STOPPED、NAN_WATCH_DONE、NAN_WATCH_TRUNCATED 或 NAN_WATCH_TIMEOUT。

9/30 在 mf4090 上看守 ep28：09:23 扫到 50,001,920 步那份有 5892 个非有限参数，09:29:54 扫到 60,002,304 步那份同样，调 fc_stop，09:30:54 退出码 0；训练链记 ep28 失败，接着训 ep29（results/diverge/ep28_nan_watch_log.txt、ep28_chain_and_record.txt）。--stop-at-step N 是写出 N 步及以后的 checkpoint 就停的截断办法；从附录 A 第 4 版起截断改为按配置做（见批量训练），这个选项没再用。

命令（看守参数照 ep28 那次；先 Mac，再 mf4090）
# Mac，仓库根：复制到 mf4090 的 runs/tools/（不经同步），记下 md5
ssh mf4090 'mkdir -p /home/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge/runs/tools'
scp stages/policy/ckpt_nonfinite.py mf4090:/home/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge/runs/tools/
md5 -q stages/policy/ckpt_nonfinite.py

# mf4090，仓库根：md5 对上后起看守，脱离 ssh 运行
cd /home/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge
md5sum runs/tools/ckpt_nonfinite.py
setsid nohup python3 runs/tools/ckpt_nonfinite.py --watch t1-ep28-w2048-cap4m-mf4090 --timeout 21600 \
  --tag "commit af261bf, plan-ep1-ep7-divergence-2026-09-30 M1 M2" >> runs/logs/ep28_nan_watch.log 2>&1 < /dev/null &

# 结束后在 mf4090 落盘，再从 Mac 仓库根 scripts/dev/pull-results 拉回 results/
scripts/dev/record diverge/ep28_nan_watch_log cat runs/logs/ep28_nan_watch.log

看守的参数、日志名与 record 名取自 results/diverge/ep28_nan_watch_log.txt 和计划 M1；复制那几行是示例，ep28 那次怎么复制的没有记录。换别的 run 时改 run 名、日志名和 record 名。--tag 只能用字母、数字与 _.:=, -。看守必须在仓库根启动：它用相对路径找 runs/flash_chord/<run> 和 stages/policy/fc_stop.sh。

本地 .wandb 历史（wandb_local_history.py）

W&B 边训边把每行 history 写进 run 目录的 run-*.wandb，不需要 W&B API 与凭据。stages/policy/wandb_local_history.py 在 Mac 上用 uv 临时装 wandb 解析：默认报每个指标第一次非有限的行与环境步数；--keys 只列指标名；--by-stage 按课程段汇总（每段最后 25% 行的均值）。它要 wandb 包，不能走 pipe-py，要先把文件拷回 Mac。

命令（Mac，仓库根）
R=diag-t1-ep1-cap4m-v040-fp16-s42-t60m-ml40
mkdir -p runs/ml40/wandb_local/$R
scp "ml40:/lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge/runs/flash_chord/$R/wandb/latest-run/run-$R-s1.wandb" runs/ml40/wandb_local/$R/
uv run --no-project --with wandb==0.22.2 python stages/policy/wandb_local_history.py --keys runs/ml40/wandb_local/$R/run-$R-s1.wandb
uv run --no-project --with wandb==0.22.2 python stages/policy/wandb_local_history.py --by-stage runs/ml40/wandb_local/$R/run-$R-s1.wandb

服务器上的位置取自 ckpt_nonfinite.py 的 runs/flash_chord/<run>/wandb/run-*/run-*.wandb；本地放法照 results/batch1/wandb_by_stage_ml40_b.txt 的命令。9/30 14:22 拷回 L 组四份 .wandb 用的就是上面这个 wandb/latest-run/run-<run>-s1.wandb 路径（latest-run 指向最近一次的 run-* 目录；结果 results/diverge/wandb_by_stage_L.txt）。Mac 上已拷回的几份满配正式训练 .wandb 各约 43 MB（看的是本地文件大小，没落盘）。

Monitoring and stops

From 10/1 the training chain detects divergence itself, starting with Tier 2 batch 1. The smoke criteria gained two checks: no non-finite parameters in the latest policy, and no nan for action mean, entropy or return in the W&B summary with a loss_scale other than 0. Full runs go through fc_run_watched.sh, whose watcher calls fc_stop after 2 consecutive non-finite checkpoints by step. Batch 1 had none of this: the ep7 smoke got SMOKE_OK while carrying NaN (results/diverge/train_nan_scan_ml40.txt, agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-29-batch1.md), and ep1 trained all 350M steps with NaN. The new criteria were back-tested on both kinds of old run (results/t2/smoke_check_regression_{ml40,mf4090}.txt). The table lists how to handle each signal.

SignalWhereAction
A training chain, evaluation chain or hand-written queue finishesThe last line of runs/logs/<chain>.log: DAY2_TRAIN_DONE / DAY2_EVAL_DONE (with counts of done, failed and skipped, and from 10/1 diverged), or …_FAILED; a hand-written queue logs that it completed or where it stopped, and queue_after.sh adds a line with the exit code. chain_after.sh decides whether the next segment starts and, if it does not, writes the reason to that segment's logPull results (scripts/dev/pull-results, with V2D_ENV=ml40 for ml40), scan for NaN and episode length first (next two rows), then run the verdict
A smoke failssmoke_check.sh: DONE, compile finished, no RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED, latest checkpoint step, W&B sync, memory limit, image fingerprint; from 10/1 also no non-finite parameters in the latest policy (scanned once it is 11 seconds old) and no nan in the W&B summary with a loss_scale other than 0; plus a 256-world rollout to check frame counts and compute the do-nothing baselineThat run's full training is marked SKIPPED and the chain moves on. The memory limit must match the criterion registered in the plan, or it kills good runs (mf4090, 9/28). If V2D_PRUNE_SMOKE_STATE already deleted the smoke's state, re-judging accepts STATE_PRUNED
Training diverges (NaN)Finished runs: action mean, entropy and return are nan in the W&B summary and loss_scale is 0 (train_nan_scan.py). Running runs: non-finite parameters in a policy checkpoint (ckpt_nonfinite.py); NaN strings in the local .wandb (the watcher only warns on these)Full runs: from 10/1 fc_run_watched.sh handles this, calling fc_stop once two consecutive complete checkpoints (by step) are non-finite, and the chain records the run as diverged (exit 9); a run that finishes with a non-finite latest policy is also recorded as diverged (exit 10), gets NONFINITE, and the evaluation chain skips it. ep1 never recovered from NaN through 350M steps (results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_ep1.txt), so there is no point waiting for the end. Smokes: from 10/1 the smoke criteria catch it, and the run's full training is marked SKIPPED. Hand-written queues that bypass the training chain still need the --watch watcher started by hand (below); a STOP placed before the run starts is cleared by fc_run.sh as stale (fc_run.sh:131–134) and does not stop it. First check whether the sequence has a box support (first item in Known risks); if not, rerun the smoke once with another seed, and if it happens again, record it and skip
No NaN, but very short episodes (stuck)Mean episode length and return from train_nan_scan.py --tableFor smokes, compare with the floor of the 28 non-box runs among the 31 frame-by-frame full-recipe smokes counted in plan appendix A.0: episode length ≥ 64.0, return ≥ 2.37 (an observed range, not a registered criterion). Below that, treat it as suspect and check the support and the reference. Full runs have no registered bound; compare within the segment (ep16 ended at 118.7 against 374–552 for the other four in its segment, and failed evaluation)
Training clearly slower (more than 30% slower than other sequences on the same machine)env steps/s in the log; checkpoint intervals from stages/policy/run_pace.shScan the checkpoints first (ckpt_nonfinite.py --last): the slowdowns of ep1 and L0 both begin in the interval where NaN first appears (ep1 3:17–3:20 → 6:22 → 7:17–7:25; L0 3:18 → 6:33 → 7:23–7:27). If anything is non-finite, stop as above; if all is finite, check the card for other processes or viewers (a viewer costs about 35%, ep0, 9/29); with only training on the card, keep going
The source changed, or someone else's process is on the cardBefore every run the chain compares .v2d-source-commit and checks nvidia-smiThe whole chain stops. While a chain runs, scripts/dev/remote refuses to sync; use plain ssh for read-only checks, see below
The evaluator raises evaluation must disable task failure termination…The smoke's diag_rollout or the evaluation logThat run is marked SKIPPED and the chain moves on; move runs/flash_chord/{smoke-,}<run> aside or rename the run before queueing it in the next batch. Batch 1 hit this twice (the ep7 smoke and the full ep1 run), both on the v0.4.0 1 m cube geometry; for a box-support sequence, read item 1 of Known risks and retrain on the #185 geometry under the box plan. If a submission export hits this error, rule 2 falls back to an earlier finite checkpoint of the same run
Non-finite worlds in evaluationnon_finite_worlds in the evaluation JSON, worlds recorded non-finite poses in the evaluation logThe batch verdict marks the run invalid through its validity gate. Check whether training diverged (rows above) before deciding on a retrain
You want to see what a policy does—Record a short rerun .rrd and view it on the Mac, or open viser (forwarded with ssh -L) after training. On a card that is training, a viewer slows training by about 35%, and the chain stops if it finds another process on the card when it starts the next run
Out of memoryExit code (fc_run.sh returns 4), XLA errorRecord it, rerun with a replay buffer size of 8 million (then 6 million), and mark the run as deviating from the recipe. A training chain that hits OOM skips the remaining runs with the same config; Day 2's B arm, as registered, does not downsize and is recorded as "no result"
A W&B upload takes training downAuthenticationError in the logLong runs always set V2D_WANDB_UPLOAD_CKPT=0; if a run still crashes, restart from zero as registered instead of resuming (resuming does not restore the replay buffer)
A smoke or full run hits a network timeout while W&B initializesCommError: Error uploading run: context deadline exceeded in the segment logThe queue sets V2D_MAX_RETRY=0, so it counts as a failure; when a smoke fails, its full run is marked SKIPPED. It has nothing to do with the sequence. With no state in the folder, a rerun starts from zero, so put the run in a later segment without touching the running chains (on 10/1, ml40's ep10 moved to segment C, results/t2/ep10_smoke_failure_ml40.txt)
Evaluation success rate is 0reference_tracking.failure_cause_fraction in the evaluation JSON (task-native; termination_cause_fraction is a legacy measure)Group by cause (object, palm, pelvis); do not tune on the spot, handle them together once there are enough samples

Sources: ep1's first non-finite checkpoint is at 50,001,920 steps (results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_ep1.txt), pace in results/batch1/pace_mf4090_a.txt; L0 in results/diverge/{ckpt_nonfinite_L0_ml40,pace_trunc60m_ml40}.txt; ep16 in results/diverge/train_summary_table_mf4090_b.txt; the ep0 viewers in agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-29-batch1.md.

Checking while a chain runs

A chain writes runs/.chain-active-<name> when it starts (the batch chain uses its log name, a hand-written queue gets the marker you place, queue_after.sh writes its own). scripts/dev/remote refuses to sync while a non-empty marker exists: a sync rewrites .v2d-source-commit and replaces scripts the chain has yet to call, and the chain's source check would then stop the remaining runs. V2D_FORCE_SYNC=1 overrides this; do not use it while a chain runs.

  • Read-only checks go over plain ssh (examples below).
  • Repository Python scripts that use only the standard library run through scripts/dev/pipe-py: the script goes over ssh stdin and runs from the server's repository root with the host python3 (3.10 on both machines), without a sync. Arguments may only contain letters, digits and _./=:,+-. Put scripts/dev/record <name> in front to save the output straight into results/ on the Mac.
  • Pace comes from stages/policy/run_pace.sh <log> <run>..., also over stdin without a sync. The log can be a training chain log or an fc_queue.sh queue log (both work since 9/30); times are converted to UTC-6.
  • New scripts that run for a long time on the server (the watcher, queue_after.sh) are copied into runs/tools/ first, checked by md5, and started with setsid nohup or in tmux. Do not run them through a foreground ssh or pipe-py; they die when the Mac goes offline.
Read-only checks and scans (Mac, repository root)
# Chain markers, the end of a queue log, processes on the card, tmux sessions (read-only, plain ssh)
ssh ml40 'cd /lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge && cat runs/.chain-active-* 2>/dev/null; tail -n 5 runs/logs/diag_ml40_ext4750.log; nvidia-smi --query-compute-apps=pid,used_memory --format=csv,noheader; tmux ls'

# Finished runs: summaries with NaN; one line per run with episode length, return, loss_scale (reads runs/logs/*.s1.log, segment 1 only)
V2D_ENV=ml40 scripts/dev/pipe-py stages/policy/train_nan_scan.py
V2D_ENV=ml40 scripts/dev/pipe-py stages/policy/train_nan_scan.py --table

# Non-finite parameters in checkpoints: every checkpoint / only the latest complete one / per tensor with indices
V2D_ENV=ml40 scripts/dev/pipe-py stages/policy/ckpt_nonfinite.py diag-t1-ep1-cap4m-v040-fp16-s42-t60m-ml40
V2D_ENV=ml40 scripts/dev/pipe-py stages/policy/ckpt_nonfinite.py --last diag-t1-ep1-cap4m-v040-fp32-s42-t60m-ml40
scripts/dev/pipe-py stages/policy/ckpt_nonfinite.py --by-tensor --steps 40001536,50001920 t1-ep1-w2048-cap4m-mf4090

# Pace (training chain log or queue log, then run names)
V2D_ENV=ml40 stages/policy/run_pace.sh runs/logs/diag_ml40_trunc60m.log diag-t1-ep1-cap4m-v040-fp32-s42-t60m-ml40

Without V2D_ENV the target is mf4090 (/home/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge). Put scripts/dev/record diverge/<name> in front of any line to save it; a result of the same name is replaced.

Divergence watcher (ckpt_nonfinite.py --watch)

Every --interval seconds (default 120) it looks at newly completed policy checkpoints; once --stop-after consecutive complete checkpoints by step (default 2) contain non-finite parameters, it calls stages/policy/fc_stop.sh <run> and exits. If the run folder already has DONE or STOP it only reports; it also exits after --timeout seconds (default 14400). Each round it also counts NaN strings in the local .wandb and reports changes, as a warning only. Its only write action is fc_stop.sh. The log ends with NAN_WATCH_STOPPED, NAN_WATCH_DONE, NAN_WATCH_TRUNCATED or NAN_WATCH_TIMEOUT.

On 9/30 it guarded ep28 on mf4090: at 09:23 the 50,001,920-step checkpoint had 5892 non-finite parameters, at 09:29:54 the 60,002,304-step one had the same, it called fc_stop and got exit code 0 at 09:30:54; the chain recorded ep28 as failed and went on to ep29 (results/diverge/ep28_nan_watch_log.txt, ep28_chain_and_record.txt). --stop-at-step N stops the run once a checkpoint at or past N steps is written, a way to truncate; since appendix A version 4 truncation is done by config instead (see Batch training), and this option has not been used again.

Commands (watcher arguments as used for ep28; Mac first, then mf4090)
# Mac, repository root: copy into runs/tools/ on mf4090 (no sync) and note the md5
ssh mf4090 'mkdir -p /home/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge/runs/tools'
scp stages/policy/ckpt_nonfinite.py mf4090:/home/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge/runs/tools/
md5 -q stages/policy/ckpt_nonfinite.py

# mf4090, repository root: once the md5 matches, start the watcher detached from ssh
cd /home/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge
md5sum runs/tools/ckpt_nonfinite.py
setsid nohup python3 runs/tools/ckpt_nonfinite.py --watch t1-ep28-w2048-cap4m-mf4090 --timeout 21600 \
  --tag "commit af261bf, plan-ep1-ep7-divergence-2026-09-30 M1 M2" >> runs/logs/ep28_nan_watch.log 2>&1 < /dev/null &

# When it ends, save the log on mf4090, then scripts/dev/pull-results from the Mac repository root
scripts/dev/record diverge/ep28_nan_watch_log cat runs/logs/ep28_nan_watch.log

The watcher arguments, log name and record name come from results/diverge/ep28_nan_watch_log.txt and plan step M1; the copy lines are an example, since how the script was copied for ep28 was not recorded. For another run, change the run name, the log name and the record name. --tag accepts only letters, digits and _.:=, -. Start the watcher from the repository root: it finds runs/flash_chord/<run> and stages/policy/fc_stop.sh by relative path.

Local .wandb history (wandb_local_history.py)

W&B writes every history row into run-*.wandb in the run folder while training, with no need for the W&B API or credentials. stages/policy/wandb_local_history.py parses it on the Mac with wandb installed on the fly by uv: by default it reports the first non-finite row and environment step of each metric; --keys only lists metric names; --by-stage summarises by curriculum stage (mean over the last 25% of rows in each stage). It needs the wandb package, so it cannot go through pipe-py; copy the file to the Mac first.

Commands (Mac, repository root)
R=diag-t1-ep1-cap4m-v040-fp16-s42-t60m-ml40
mkdir -p runs/ml40/wandb_local/$R
scp "ml40:/lab_data/rxzhou/v2d/maniphys_lab_v2d_challenge/runs/flash_chord/$R/wandb/latest-run/run-$R-s1.wandb" runs/ml40/wandb_local/$R/
uv run --no-project --with wandb==0.22.2 python stages/policy/wandb_local_history.py --keys runs/ml40/wandb_local/$R/run-$R-s1.wandb
uv run --no-project --with wandb==0.22.2 python stages/policy/wandb_local_history.py --by-stage runs/ml40/wandb_local/$R/run-$R-s1.wandb

The server-side location comes from ckpt_nonfinite.py (runs/flash_chord/<run>/wandb/run-*/run-*.wandb); the local layout follows the command in results/batch1/wandb_by_stage_ml40_b.txt. The four L-group .wandb files were copied at 14:22 on 9/30 with exactly this wandb/latest-run/run-<run>-s1.wandb path (latest-run points to the most recent run-* directory; result results/diverge/wandb_by_stage_L.txt). The full-recipe .wandb files already copied to the Mac are about 43 MB each (local file sizes, not saved as a result).

已知风险

前三条是 9/30 新增的，来自 ep1、ep7、ep28 的发散排查（发散排查；计划 agent-doc/plan-ep1-ep7-divergence-2026-09-30.md，执行记录 agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md）。10/1 20:00 按组委会的新 kit 改写了第 1 条的处理，新增第 4 条。

1. v0.4.0 把方盒支撑面建成 1 m 立方体

FlashCHORD v0.4.0 的 scene/support.py:85–86、114–116 对 USD Cube 只读 size、不读 xformOp:scale。Tier 1 里支撑面是 Cube 的只有 ep1（vacuum）、ep7（iron）、ep28（squeegee）三条（results/diverge/support_shapes.txt），仿真里全被建成半尺寸 (0.5, 0.5, 0.5) 的实心立方体（results/ml40/diverge/support_import_v040.txt）。按这个几何，参考物体从第 0 帧起就埋在立方体里：ep1 519 / 747 帧、ep28 两块各 608、651 帧嵌入超过 5 mm；ep7 896 / 896 帧，第 0 帧深约 49 cm（results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt；ep7 这句未核实）。第 1 批这三条都无效。

官方 kit 指定的评测运行时 aee95f2 的 support.py 与 v0.4.0 相同（md5 95e5e33f…，results/diverge/support_py_versions.txt）。上游 8c778f905c（#185，9/29 12:11 UTC-6）已修；本仓库的补丁副本把 ep7 桌面建成 (0.42, 0.141, 0.005)（results/ml40/diverge/support_import_p185.txt），到 10/1 只用于诊断。组委会 10/1 13:43 重新发布 kit，评测运行时改钉在 8c778f9，这个版本已含 #185（results/submission/runtime_pin_facts.txt）。

按计划附录 A.3 登记的判据，H1′（v0.4.0 的 1 m 立方体是发散的触发）的最终判定是支持（9/30 14:35，results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt）：ep7 冒烟 R1（v0.4.0）5 / 8 发散，R3′（#185）0 / 8，配对种子 43–50，单侧 Fisher p = 0.0128。范围只到 ep7 冒烟（400 万步、L40S）；ep1 只有截断复现的单种子旁证（L0 用 v0.4.0 发散、L1 用 #185 不发散，同向），ep28 登记为只描述。fp16 存储和 BN 统计量在其中各占多少，这个判定说明不了（见第 2 条）。

怎么办：评测与导出都用 8c778f9，不再问组委会（决定记录 agent-doc/decision-organizer-q1-q3-2026-10-01.md）。ep1、ep7、ep28 在 Tier 1、Tier 2 各重训一条：训练挂 #185 补丁副本，评测用与 8c778f9 逐字节相同的副本，run 名加 -p185（方盒计划 agent-doc/plan-box6-and-eval-runtime-2026-10-01.md，第 2 版，已部署，mf4090 10/2 06:43 开训）。t1-ep7 的正式训练在约 1.4 亿步发散、被看守止损（冒烟通过）：#185 几何去掉了冒烟发散的触发，但不保证满 3.5 亿步不发散，其余方盒 run 同样靠看守兜底。Tier 2 的支撑面已查过：带 scale 的方盒同样只有这三条，文件与 Tier 1 的 md5 相同（results/t2/support_kinds_tier2_{mf4090,ml40}.txt）。

4. 打包器只认一个刚体变换：Tier 2 要加 --frame-references

10/1 的新打包器用第 0 帧求一个刚体变换，把评测器记下的参考对到这一档发布的输入上，逐帧误差都在 1 mm、1e-3 rad 以内才换算，否则整次打包报错（tools/pack_submission.py:78–79, 99, 118，results/submission/eval_runtime_facts.txt）。Tier 2 的参考是 v5.4 降噪后的轨迹，与发布的带噪输入差几厘米，不加 --frame-references 必然报错。Tier 1 的评测器参考是重定向后的物体位姿，与发布输入之间应当只差坐标系换算与地面找平，也就是一个刚体变换，还没实测。

怎么办：按 kit 的 npz 格式由 v5.4 生成 Tier 2 的帧参考（10/2 已生成，30 条通过粗核）；先拿兼容性验证的导出试打包（10/2 两轮预演都过，Tier 2 不给帧参考时打包器按预期拒绝）。打包器对三档都打印按干净参考算的本地分数；Tier 2、Tier 3 的本地分数按净室规矩不拿来做选择，打包时滤掉那一行（属解读，待定）。

2. 一处出事，整条训练死掉；fp32 存储也挡不住

读 v0.4.0 代码（results/diverge/code_facts.txt，执行记录 C4）：BatchNorm 的滑动统计按 0.01 的速率更新，不过滤非有限值；梯度非有限时 _apply_adam_if_finite 只跳过参数与 Adam 动量，统计量照写；loss scale 遇到非有限梯度就减半；G1 配方把观测存成 float16。按这几处代码推断（未在 GPU 上单独验证）：一个批次里出现非有限输入，统计量就一直是 NaN，之后的前向输出全是 NaN，loss_scale 掉到 0。观察到的是：发散的 run（ep1、ep7 冒烟、ep28、诊断里的 R0、R1、R2、L0、L3）最后一份 checkpoint 都有 5892 / 1,207,577 个 NaN（results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_*.txt，ep28 见 ep28_nan_watch_log.txt）；有 W&B 摘要的几条 loss_scale 都是 0（ep28 被停掉，没有摘要；results/diverge/train_summary_table_*.txt）。

登记的 H4（fp16 存储是必要条件）按 4 个种子已被拒绝：fp32 存储的 R2 同样 2 / 4 发散（results/diverge/diag_verdict_seeds43_46.txt）；最终判定照旧，⑥（R2 补种子）没跑（results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt）。截断复现 L3（ep1、v0.4.0、fp32 存储，L40S、单种子）从第一份 checkpoint（10,000,384 步）起就是 5892 个 NaN（results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_L3_ml40.txt），与 H4 被拒绝同向。R2 发散后，486 维输入归一化统计量里腕、物体、关节的位置，腕与关节的速度，未来关节与手掌的位置差，目标历史整块 NaN（手在物体系、目标在物体系两块只有位置部分 NaN）；6D 朝向块、只取自参考的未来根位置和参考相位仍有限，与 ep1（fp16）逐位相同（results/diverge/obs_block_nan_map{,_R2_s43,_R2_s44}.txt）。

怎么办：出 NaN 的 run 不用等跑完。10/1 起训练链自带看守，按步数连续 2 份 checkpoint 非有限就止损；不走训练链的手写队列要自己挂（监控与停止）。对策（统计量加非有限过滤、改存储或计算精度）会偏离配方，要另写计划登记，还没做。

3. W&B 摘要只数 NaN，看不出卡住的 run

train_nan_scan.py 和附录 A.3 的“发散”都只看有没有 NaN 或非有限参数。v0.4.0 的 13 条 ep7 冒烟（R0，R1 8 个种子，R2 4 个种子）里 8 条出 NaN；没出 NaN 的 5 条停在回合约 51、回报约 −3.7，按判据算“未发散”，其实一条都没学起来；换成 #185 的 8 条（R3′）回合 243–309、回报 25.6–34.8（results/diverge/{diag_verdict_final,train_summary_table_ml40_diag_final}.txt）。所以 H1′ 的 Fisher 检验（8 个种子，R1 5 / 8 对 R3′ 0 / 8，p = 0.0128）虽然过了 0.05，还是显得比这个差别弱。

怎么办：看 train_nan_scan.py --table 的平均回合长度与回报。冒烟对照非方盒冒烟的下界（回合 ≥ 64.0、回报 ≥ 2.37，计划附录 A.0；经验范围，不是登记判据）。正式训练没有登记的界限：ep16 摘要无 NaN，最终回合 118.7，同段另外四条 374–552，评测不通过（results/diverge/train_summary_table_mf4090_b.txt）。

之前记下的

风险现状与处理
构建走不走镜像源BuildKit 的 FROM 是否经 daemon 的 registry-mirrors 拉取，未核实。diff_id 对不上或拉取失败就先停下回报，不改 base。
共享卡JAX 的显存上限按整卡算，别人中途占卡，先被挤掉的是 Warp 那部分，可能跑了几小时才 OOM。9/28 起两台卡只有 rxzhou 在用；训练链开跑与每条 run 前查卡，有别人的进程就停。
回放速度kit 按源帧逐帧计分，训练固定用逐帧（见官方评测与提交）。“真实时长训练的 policy 会被错位计分”是读代码推出的（未核实），没在新评测器上实测；接上 8c778f9 后用 ep20 验一次。
陷桌面Tier 2 的 iron、rice cooker 陷进桌面约 2 cm，观察这两条的终止原因是否集中在物体。
官方 1 个世界评测本项目一直按 4096 世界的均值判，官方每条只评 1 个世界、用确定性动作，单个世界的结果可能和均值差很多。兼容性验证会把 3 份已评过的 policy 各导出 1 个世界，可以和 4096 世界的均值对比（方盒计划）。
评测器遇到被判终止的世界直接报错ep7 冒烟（256 个世界里 172 个发散）与 ep1 正式训练的评测都这样报错，两条都在 v0.4.0 的 1 m 立方体几何上（agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-29-batch1.md）。第 1 批除这两条和停训未评的 ep28 外，其余 18 条都有完整评测（四份 results/day2/batch_verdict_*_batch1{a,b}.txt）。官方每条只评 1 个世界，policy 在那个世界里若被判终止，这条交不出轨迹，而每次提交要有全部 30 条。处理（10/1 定下）：含 NaN 的 policy 不导出；提交导出时报这个错，就退到同一条 run 里更早的一份有限 checkpoint。评测器把 ReconBody 的终止阈值改成 1e6，只有数值发散才会触发；已落盘的 96 份评测没有一份报这个错（决定记录）。
mf4090 磁盘10/1 上午把第 1 批用不上的 39 个 state（6.24 GB）归档到 HF 私有仓库后删掉，可用空间从 5297 MB 增加到 11054 MB（agent-doc/plan-hf-archive-2026-10-01.md）；10/1 20:12 剩 9431 MB，10/2 07:00 方盒在训时剩 7031 MB，13:00 剩 5755 MB。Tier 2 第 1 批与方盒的正式 run 只留最新一份 state，冒烟判过就删自己的 state；按方盒计划的估计，方盒 4 条训完约剩 5.3 GB，磁盘守卫 1200 MB。
100 万 bufferDay 1 里那条从课程第 0 阶段起就明显更差（chord_sr 0.017 vs 0.79，单种子；两条同时换了 GPU 与机器，差别不能单独归因于 buffer）。4090 放不下默认的 1000 万；400 万跑逐帧可用（ep27 过线），跑真实时长不可用（Day 2 Q3）。
选序列起步序列按平移挑的，ep20 物体只平移 4 cm，却要原地转近一圈。按物体位移和在动帧排难度会漏掉旋转；Day 2 改为按帧数分档机械挑选，并要求支撑面类型不全相同。
判据与参数不一致9/28 mf4090 开链时给的显存上限（23000 MiB）没写进计划，与登记的判据（只看 OOM 字样）不一致，三条冒烟被误判跳过。开链参数要从登记的判据推出来，开链前逐项对一遍。
时长随序列变没发散的满配正式训练 ml40 2 h 03 min 到 2 h 31 min、mf4090 1 h 57 min 到 2 h 14 min（节奏文件见 SOP 首页），冒烟看不出来；出分时间报区间。旧版把 ep1 慢一倍当成序列本身的问题（推测接触计算变重），这个推测撤回：ep1 变慢的起点与出 NaN 重合，ml40 上的 L0 也一样（见监控与停止）。

Known risks

The first three were added on 9/30 from the ep1, ep7 and ep28 divergence investigation (Divergence investigation; plan agent-doc/plan-ep1-ep7-divergence-2026-09-30.md, record agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-30-divergence.md). At 20:00 on 10/1 the handling in item 1 was rewritten for the organizers' new kit, and item 4 was added.

1. v0.4.0 builds box supports as 1 m cubes

In FlashCHORD v0.4.0, scene/support.py:85–86, 114–116 reads only size from a USD Cube and ignores xformOp:scale. In Tier 1 only ep1 (vacuum), ep7 (iron) and ep28 (squeegee) have Cube supports (results/diverge/support_shapes.txt), and in simulation all of them become solid cubes with half extents (0.5, 0.5, 0.5) (results/ml40/diverge/support_import_v040.txt). With that geometry the reference object sits inside the cube from frame 0: ep1 is embedded more than 5 mm in 519 of 747 frames and ep28 in 608 and 651 frames for its two boxes; ep7 in 896 of 896 frames, about 49 cm deep at frame 0 (results/diverge/support_penetration_as_v040.txt; the ep7 figure is unverified). All three are invalid in batch 1.

The evaluation runtime named by the official kit, aee95f2, has the same support.py as v0.4.0 (md5 95e5e33f…, results/diverge/support_py_versions.txt). Upstream fixed it in 8c778f905c (#185, 12:11 UTC-6 on 9/29); the repository's patched copy builds ep7's tabletop as (0.42, 0.141, 0.005) (results/ml40/diverge/support_import_p185.txt) and served only for diagnosis up to 10/1. The organizers republished the kit at 13:43 on 10/1 and pinned the evaluation runtime to 8c778f9, which includes #185 (results/submission/runtime_pin_facts.txt).

Under the criteria registered in plan appendix A.3, the final verdict on H1′ (the v0.4.0 1 m cube triggers the divergence) is "supported" (14:35 on 9/30, results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt): in the ep7 smokes, R1 (v0.4.0) diverged in 5 of 8 and R3′ (#185) in 0 of 8, paired seeds 43–50, one-sided Fisher p = 0.0128. The scope is the ep7 smoke only (4M steps, L40S); ep1 has only the single-seed truncated reproductions as side evidence (L0 on v0.4.0 diverged, L1 on #185 did not, pointing the same way), and ep28 was registered as description only. The verdict says nothing about how much fp16 storage and the BN statistics each contribute (see item 2).

What to do: evaluate and export with 8c778f9; the organizers no longer need to be asked (decision record agent-doc/decision-organizer-q1-q3-2026-10-01.md). ep1, ep7 and ep28 are retrained once in Tier 1 and once in Tier 2: training mounts the #185-patched copy, evaluation a copy byte-identical to 8c778f9, and the run names get -p185 (box plan agent-doc/plan-box6-and-eval-runtime-2026-10-01.md, v2, deployed; mf4090 started at 06:43 on 10/2). The full t1-ep7 run diverged at about 140M steps and was stopped by the watcher (its smoke had passed): the #185 geometry removes the trigger of the smoke divergence but does not guarantee that a 350M-step run stays finite, and the other box runs rely on the watcher in the same way. The Tier 2 supports have been checked: the same three sequences are the only scaled boxes, with files whose md5 matches Tier 1 (results/t2/support_kinds_tier2_{mf4090,ml40}.txt).

4. The packer accepts only one rigid transform: Tier 2 needs --frame-references

The 10/1 packer solves one rigid transform from frame 0 that maps the evaluator's recorded reference onto the tier's released input, and converts only if every frame agrees within 1 mm and 1e-3 rad; otherwise the whole packing run fails (tools/pack_submission.py:78–79, 99, 118, results/submission/eval_runtime_facts.txt). The Tier 2 references are the v5.4 denoised trajectories, several centimetres from the released noisy input, so packing without --frame-references is bound to fail. The Tier 1 evaluator reference is the retargeted object pose, which should differ from the released input only by the frame change and the ground levelling, that is by one rigid transform; this has not been tested.

What to do: generate the Tier 2 frame references from v5.4 in the kit's npz format (done on 10/2, all 30 pass the sanity checks); rehearse packing on the compatibility check's exports first (both rehearsals passed on 10/2, and without frame references the packer refuses Tier 2 as expected). The packer prints local scores against the clean reference for all three tiers; under the project's clean-room rule, Tier 2 and Tier 3 local scores are never used to choose anything, and packing drops that line (an interpretation, to be decided).

2. One bad batch kills the whole run, and fp32 storage does not prevent it

From the v0.4.0 code (results/diverge/code_facts.txt, record step C4): BatchNorm running statistics update at a rate of 0.01 with no filter for non-finite values; when gradients are non-finite, _apply_adam_if_finite skips only the parameters and Adam moments and still writes the statistics; the loss scale halves on every non-finite gradient; the G1 recipe stores observations as float16. Inferred from this code (not separately tested on GPU): once a batch carries a non-finite input, the statistics stay NaN, every later forward pass outputs NaN, and loss_scale drops to 0. What was observed: every run that diverged (ep1, the ep7 smoke, ep28, and R0, R1, R2, L0 and L3 in the diagnosis) has 5892 NaN out of 1,207,577 parameters in its last checkpoint (results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_*.txt; ep28 in ep28_nan_watch_log.txt), and those with a W&B summary show loss_scale 0 (ep28 was stopped and has none; results/diverge/train_summary_table_*.txt).

The registered H4 (fp16 storage is necessary) is rejected at 4 seeds: R2 with fp32 storage also diverged in 2 of 4 (results/diverge/diag_verdict_seeds43_46.txt); the final verdict keeps this, and ⑥ (more R2 seeds) was not run (results/diverge/diag_verdict_final.txt). The truncated reproduction L3 (ep1, v0.4.0, fp32 storage, L40S, single seed) has 5892 NaN from its first checkpoint at 10,000,384 steps (results/diverge/ckpt_nonfinite_L3_ml40.txt), in line with H4 being rejected. After R2 diverged, the 486-dimensional input normalization statistics were NaN for the wrist, object and joint positions, the wrist and joint velocities, the future joint and palm position deltas and the target history, plus only the position parts of the hand-in-object and target-in-object poses, while the 6D orientation blocks, the reference-only future root positions and the reference phase stayed finite, index for index the same as ep1 with fp16 (results/diverge/obs_block_nan_map{,_R2_s43,_R2_s44}.txt).

What to do: do not wait for a NaN run to finish. From 10/1 the training chain runs a watcher that stops a run after 2 consecutive non-finite checkpoints by step; hand-written queues outside the chain need one started by hand (Monitoring and stops). Countermeasures (a non-finite filter on the statistics, a different storage or compute precision) deviate from the recipe, need their own registered plan, and have not been done.

3. W&B summaries count NaN only and miss stuck runs

train_nan_scan.py and "divergence" in appendix A.3 both look only for NaN or non-finite parameters. Of the 13 ep7 smokes on v0.4.0 (R0, R1 with 8 seeds, R2 with 4 seeds), 8 went NaN; the 5 without NaN stopped at an episode length of about 51 and a return of about −3.7. They count as "not diverged", but none of them learned. The 8 with #185 (R3′) reached episode lengths of 243–309 and returns of 25.6–34.8 (results/diverge/{diag_verdict_final,train_summary_table_ml40_diag_final}.txt). That is why the Fisher test for H1′ (8 seeds, R1 5 of 8 against R3′ 0 of 8, p = 0.0128) still looks weaker than the difference, even though it clears 0.05.

What to do: read the mean episode length and return from train_nan_scan.py --table. Compare smokes with the floor of the non-box smokes (length ≥ 64.0, return ≥ 2.37, plan appendix A.0; an observed range, not a registered criterion). Full runs have no registered bound: ep16 has no NaN in its summary and ended at 118.7, against 374–552 for the other four in its segment, and failed evaluation (results/diverge/train_summary_table_mf4090_b.txt).

Recorded earlier

RiskState and handling
Build mirror pathWhether BuildKit's FROM pulls through the daemon's registry-mirrors is unverified. If the diff_ids do not match or the pull fails, stop and report instead of changing the base.
Shared cardJAX sizes its memory limit from the whole card, so if someone takes the card mid-run, Warp's share is squeezed first and the run can fail hours in. Since 9/28 rxzhou is the only one using either card; training chains check the card when they start and before every run, and stop if another user's process is there.
Playback speedThe kit scores frame by frame against the source frames, and training uses frame-by-frame playback only (see Official evaluation and submission). That a real-time-trained policy would be scored out of step was inferred from the code (unverified) and has not been tested on the new evaluator; check it once on ep20 after the 8c778f9 evaluator is in place.
Tabletop sinkingThe Tier 2 iron and rice cooker sink about 2 cm into the tabletop; watch whether their termination causes concentrate on the object.
One-world official evaluationThe project has always judged by the mean over 4096 worlds, while the organizers evaluate one world per episode with deterministic actions, and a single world can land far from the mean. The compatibility check exports one world each from 3 policies already evaluated, which can be compared with their 4096-world means (box plan).
The evaluator errors out on a terminated worldThe ep7 smoke (172 of 256 worlds blew up) and the full ep1 run both hit this error in evaluation, both on the v0.4.0 1 m cube geometry (agent-doc/experiments/2026-09-29-batch1.md). Apart from these two and ep28, which was stopped and not evaluated, the other 18 batch 1 runs all have complete evaluations (the four results/day2/batch_verdict_*_batch1{a,b}.txt). The organizers evaluate one world per episode; if a policy is judged terminated in that world, the episode yields no trajectory, and every submission needs all 30. Handling (decided on 10/1): a policy with NaN is never exported; if a submission export hits this error, fall back to an earlier finite checkpoint of the same run. The evaluator sets the ReconBody termination thresholds to 1e6, so only a numerical blow-up triggers it; none of the 96 recorded evaluations raised it (decision record).
mf4090 diskOn the morning of 10/1 the 39 batch 1 states no longer needed (6.24 GB) went to a private HF repository and were deleted locally, taking free space from 5297 MB to 11054 MB (agent-doc/plan-hf-archive-2026-10-01.md); 9431 MB were free at 20:12 on 10/1, 7031 MB at 07:00 on 10/2 with the box runs training, and 5755 MB at 13:00. Full runs of Tier 2 batch 1 and of the box runs keep only the latest state, and each passed smoke deletes its own; the box plan expects about 5.3 GB free once the 4 box runs finish, against a disk guard of 1200 MB.
1M bufferOn Day 1 that run was clearly worse from curriculum stage 0 on (chord_sr 0.017 versus 0.79, single seed; the two runs also differ in GPU and machine, so the gap is not attributable to the buffer alone). The 4090 cannot hold the default 10M; 4M works for frame playback (ep27 passes) but not for real-time playback (Day 2 Q3).
Sequence choiceThe starting sequences were picked by translation; ep20 moves the object only 4 cm but turns it in place almost a full circle. Ranking difficulty by displacement and moving frames misses rotation; Day 2 instead picks mechanically by length tiers and requires mixed support types.
Criteria versus parametersOn 9/28 the memory limit given when launching the mf4090 chain (23000 MiB) was not in the plan and disagreed with the registered criterion (OOM messages only), so three good smokes were skipped. Launch parameters must follow from the registered criteria; check them item by item before launching.
Run time depends on the sequenceFull-recipe runs that did not diverge took 2 h 03 min to 2 h 31 min on ml40 and 1 h 57 min to 2 h 14 min on mf4090 (pace files listed on the SOP overview), and the smoke gives no hint; report expected results as ranges. The earlier version blamed ep1's doubled time on the sequence (a guess that contact got heavier); that guess is withdrawn: ep1's slowdown starts together with its NaN, and so does L0's on ml40 (see Monitoring and stops).
